### [1] [OCLDA-GS: Object-Centric Local Deformation-Aware Gaussian Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23416)
*Ashim Dahal,Nikesh Subedi,Rabab Abdelfattah,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OCLDA-GS：面向对象的流式3DGS框架，将锚点动态重建与刚体变换、局部变形校正解耦，提升大运动物体与边界处质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效流式3DGS依赖选取锚点高斯并用邻域插值，忽略物体特定运动，且在物体边界附近产生不一致。

Method: 动态重分配物体与运动感知的锚点高斯，为大运动物体分配更多锚点支撑；将高斯邻域限制在物体分组内以精确边界；估计物体级刚体变换（如手部运动），并据此学习逐锚点的局部变形校正以刻画小幅形变（如衣物褶皱）。

Result: 在三个动态多视图数据集上优于基线；相同细化预算下，PSNR较IGS在N3DV提升0.169 dB、Meeting Room提升0.202 dB、Google Immersive提升0.199 dB；细化时间步间平均PSNR跌幅分别降低9.2%（N3DV）与13.8%（Meeting Room）。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心、结合刚体运动与局部形变校正的锚点分配策略，可在同等细化预算下同时提升流式3DGS的重建质量与时间稳定性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) allows for online reconstruction of dynamic scenes from multi-view videos for Free Viewpoint Video (FVV). Current time and storage efficient methods rely on selecting a subset of anchor Gaussians and neighborhood interpolation for fast streamable 3DGS. However, these methods can overlook object-specific motion and introduce inconsistencies near object boundaries. We propose OCLDA-GS, an object-centric streaming framework that dynamically reallocates object and motion-aware Gaussian anchors to allocate anchor Gaussian support for objects undergoing large motion while at the same time, restricting the gaussian neighborhood within object groups for more precise object boundaries. Furthermore, it estimates object-wise rigid transformations (e.g. a hand moving) and leverages these estimates to learn per-anchor local deformation correction for small deformations (e.g. wrinkles on a shirt). Then, we conduct experiments on three dynamic multi-view video datasets and demonstrate improvements over the previous baselines alongside improved quality between refinement timesteps. Under matched refinement budgets, our method improves PSNR over Instant Gaussian Stream (IGS) by 0.169 dB on N3DV, 0.202 dB on Meeting Room and +0.199 on Google Immersive, while reducing the mean PSNR drop between refinement timesteps by 9.2% and 13.8% in N3DV and Meeting Room, respectively.

</details>


### [2] [Autoregressive View Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23417)
*Jensen,Zhou,Samuel Rota Bulo,Denis Rozumny,Duncan Zauss,Peter Hedman,Nikhil Keetha,Philip Torr,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Peter Kontschieder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVM 以自回归方式把场景生成为视图流，无需显式三维表示，通过统一 in-context 接口在单一模型中适配图像/视频输入与多种输出任务，并借助因果自滚动微调提升真实感、减少去噪步数、抑制长程漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免显式 3D 表示（如 NeRF/3DGS）带来的重建与渲染负担，探索用视频生成先验把"重建"变为"上下文条件下的视图序列生成"，并用统一接口覆盖重建、插值与外推等多种设置。

Method: 用 flow matching 将预训练双向视频扩散 Transformer 微调为 N-in-1-out 形式；通过检索输入与生成历史中的相关视图构成有界主动上下文；再以自身 rollout 训练因果模型（自回归因果化 + 自蒸馏式微调）。

Result: 训练规模约 8M 场景与视频片段；在相机控制、文本控制、视觉质量、动态性、稀疏与 4D 重建、长程一致性等多基准上验证了生成能力与场景持续性。

Conclusion: 单一 in-context 接口即可适配多样输入输出设置，视图流式自回归生成可作为显式 3D 表示的可替代范式；因果自滚动微调是控制漂移与成本的关键。

Abstract: We present the Autoregressive View Model (AVM), which generates a scene as a stream of views without constructing an explicit 3D representation. Through a unified in-context interface, AVM conditions on images or videos to generate short clips along a requested camera path. Visual context grounds reconstruction and interpolation, while a learned prior enables extrapolation into unseen regions. AVM retrieves relevant views from inputs and generated history to form a bounded active context. We fine-tune a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion transformer with flow matching in an N-in-1-out formulation. We then tune a causal model on its own rollouts to improve photorealism, reduce denoising steps, and limit long-horizon drift. We scale training to approximately 8M scenes and video clips. We evaluate generation capacity and scene persistence across benchmarks for camera control, text control, visual quality, dynamics, sparse and 4D reconstruction, and long-horizon consistency. The results demonstrate that AVM adapts to diverse input and output settings through one in-context interface.

</details>


### [3] [Bits, Not Boxes: Label-Free Layer Selection for Visual Localization in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23432)
*Haisheng Fu,Gexin Huang,Haikai Zhao,Jie Liang,Zhenman Fang,Jingning Han,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WhereBits，用无标注问题即可从注意力本身识别 VLM 中能定位目标对象的层，无需框标注或固定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力图虽可低成本反映 VLM 关注位置，但注意力汇（attention sinks）使其定位不可靠；对比通用提示可抑制 sinks，然而仅部分层能定位被查询对象。现有方法依赖框标注或固定规则来寻找这些层。

Method: 作者指出问题会改变两件事：头对图像的总注意量，以及注意力的空间去向；二者可精确分离，后者以比特度量（在无标注问题上计算），可指示哪些层具有定位能力。WhereBits 将这些"看向何处"的比特转化为层级先验，并与逐头对比得分结合；三个设计常数在带标注的 COCO 上一次性固定，新模型或新分辨率仅需无标注问题。

Result: 在八个 VLM 上，WhereBits 在 COCO 与 RefCOCO 上达到与框调优层相当的点示精度，且目标 token 排序更优；优势在留出模型族与更高分辨率下依然成立。COCO 点示精度为 0.72，平均注意力为 0.22。在免训练 zoom-in 中，其裁剪使 V* 强制选择评测准确率提升 6.0 点。

Conclusion: 注意力信息本身足以识别可定位层，WhereBits 提供了一种免框标注、可跨模型与分辨率迁移的层选择方案，显著优于朴素注意力定位。

Abstract: Attention maps cheaply show where a vision-language model (VLM) looks, but attention sinks make them unreliable for localization. Contrasting the question with a generic prompt suppresses sinks, yet only some layers localize the queried object. Existing methods find these layers with box annotations or fixed rules. We show that attention itself can identify them. A question changes how much a head attends to the image and where this attention goes. The two effects separate exactly, and the second, measured in bits on unlabeled questions, indicates which layers localize. WhereBits turns these where-to-look bits into a layer prior and combines it with per-head contrastive scores. Its three design constants were fixed once on labeled COCO data, and a new model or resolution needs only unlabeled questions. Across eight VLMs, WhereBits matches the pointing accuracy of box-tuned layers on COCO and RefCOCO and ranks target tokens better. This advantage holds on a held-out model family and at higher resolutions. Its COCO pointing accuracy is 0.72, against 0.22 for mean attention. In training-free zoom-in, its crops raise V* accuracy by 6.0 points under forced-choice evaluation.

</details>


### [4] [TransFIRA+: Transfer Learning for Biometric Recognizability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23442)
*Allen Tu,Kartik Narayan,Joshua Gleason,Jennifer Xu,Matthew Meyn,Tom Goldstein,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TransFIRA框架，将人脸/身体图像的可识别性直接建立在编码器嵌入空间中（用CCS与CCAS预测），无需质量标注；配合模板加权与CCAS>0过滤，以及针对身体模态的sigmoid后校准，在BRIAR与两个未见多摄像头数据集上取得SOTA验证与融合识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控、视频与网络图像中姿态、模糊、光照、遮挡、距离变化剧烈，现有视觉质量指标无法预测输入对部署编码器是否可识别；现有面部图像质量评估依赖视觉启发式、人工标注或昂贵生成流程，其预测与编码器决策几何脱节。

Method: TransFIRA：从预训练编码器的嵌入中导出类中心相似度（CCS）与类中心角分离度（CCAS），让轻量网络从图像直接预测二者，无需质量标注；用预测CCS做模板加权、以CCAS>0作为天然过滤阈值。TransFIRA+：针对跨摄像头多模态（人脸+身体）不裁剪视频，引入事后sigmoid校准，把饱和的身体可识别性分数映射为有信息量的权重，无需重训练。

Result: BRIAR上人脸验证达SOTA：10^-6 FMR下TAR从均匀平均的0.085提升至0.598；预测分数可揭示图像退化对编码器特异的可识别性影响。校准后，BRIAR训练的TransFIRA预测器在两个未见多摄像头数据集上提升人脸+身体融合识别，优于检测器置信度加权与未校准CCS-rank加权。

Conclusion: 以编码器为锚的可识别性度量可跨生物特征模态与识别场景泛化；TransFIRA与TransFIRA+构成统一框架。

Abstract: Biometric recognition in surveillance, video, and web imagery must contend with extreme variation in pose, blur, illumination, occlusion, and distance, where visual quality metrics fail to predict whether inputs are recognizable to the deployed encoder. Existing face image quality assessment methods rely on visual heuristics, curated annotations, or costly generative pipelines, leaving their predictions detached from the encoder's decision geometry. We introduce TransFIRA, a lightweight framework that grounds recognizability directly in embedding space. TransFIRA derives class-center similarity (CCS) and class-center angular separation (CCAS) from a pretrained encoder's embeddings and learns to predict them from images without quality annotations. Using predicted CCS for template weighting and CCAS>0 as a natural filtering threshold yields state-of-the-art face verification performance on BRIAR, raising TAR at $10^{-6}$ FMR from 0.085 with uniform averaging to 0.598. The predicted scores also reveal how image degradations affect encoder-specific recognizability. TransFIRA+ extends this framework to cross-camera multimodal person identification in untrimmed surveillance video. In this setting, body recognizability scores saturate within a narrow range, making direct weighting effectively uniform. We introduce a post-hoc sigmoid calibration that transforms these saturated scores into informative weights without retraining. After calibration, the BRIAR-trained TransFIRA predictor improves fused face and body identification on two unseen multi-camera datasets, outperforming detector-confidence weighting and uncalibrated CCS-rank weighting. Together, TransFIRA and TransFIRA+ demonstrate that encoder-grounded recognizability generalizes across biometric modalities and recognition settings.

</details>


### [5] [Where Global Context and Weight Sharing Work in Compact Vision Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23446)
*Michael Schroeder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在EfficientNet-B0上受控实验：将零门控残差自注意力适配器插入P3/P4/P5可超纯卷积控制组+0.95个百分点，且注意力幅度随语义深度单调上升；Stage 6交替两套共享MBConv模板可减少10.9%参数仅损失1.84个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑卷积主干（如EfficientNet）靠局部深度可分离卷积实现参数效率，但混合设计中两个结构性问题未定：(1)全局自注意力在何处优于纯卷积；(2)重复块共享权重是否会损失表征能力。

Method: 在ImageNet-1k上用EfficientNet-B0做受控实验。实验一比较"BiFPN多尺度特征金字塔包裹"与"在完整MBConv主干上插入轻量零门控残差自注意力适配器"（P3:28×28窗口化、P4:14×14全局、P5:7×7全局），采用88轮无状态回放调度与逐比特匹配的plain-B0对照。实验二测试重复MBConv块的循环权重共享（每次传递独立BN统计，每传递+1,536参数），包括事后卷积核平均、单模板阶段捆绑、Stage 6交替两套共享模板[B1,A,B,A]。

Result: BiFPN包裹单独未超过plain B0（69.99%–71.81% vs 72.86%），加阶段注意力后达73.15%。零门控适配器单独达74.02%，比逐比特匹配对照高+0.954个百分点（73.88% vs 72.92%）；学习到的注意力幅度随深度单调上升（P3 27.2% → P4 45.4% → P5 66.3%）。权重共享方面：事后核平均降至17.03%；单模板阶段捆绑（-27.6%参数）平台于66.10%；Stage 6交替两模板减少10.9%参数（582K权重），单轮恢复+10.82点至70.92%（仅-1.84点）。

Conclusion: 全局自注意力的收益依赖于嵌入在完整卷积主干中的位置与深度，而非外部特征金字塔；轻量零门控适配器在深层阶段贡献更大。重复块权重共享存在可行折中点——交替双模板的适度共享可显著压缩参数且保持接近的精度，而事后平均与激进捆绑不可行。

Abstract: Compact convolutional backbones such as EfficientNet achieve strong parameter efficiency through local depthwise separable convolutions, yet two structural questions remain central to hybrid design: where does global self-attention improve recognition over pure convolution, and can repeated blocks share weights without losing representational capacity? We investigate both questions on ImageNet-1k (50,000-image validation) using controlled EfficientNet-B0 experiments. First, we show that wrapping a classification backbone in multi-scale feature pyramids (BiFPN) fails to outperform a plain MBConv trunk (69.99% - 71.81% vs. 72.86% plain B0) until paired with stage attention (73.15%). In contrast, keeping the MBConv trunk intact and inserting lightweight zero-gated residual self-attention adapters at stages P3 (28x28 windowed), P4 (14x14 global), and P5 (7x7 global) reaches 74.02% standalone and outperforms a bit-for-bit matched plain-B0 control by +0.954 percentage points (73.88% vs. 72.92%) across an 88-epoch stateless replay schedule, with learned attention amplitude rising monotonically with semantic depth (27.2% at P3 -> 45.4% at P4 -> 66.3% at P5). Second, we evaluate recurrent weight sharing across repeated MBConv blocks with pass-specific BatchNorm statistics (+1,536 parameters per pass). While post-hoc kernel averaging degrades accuracy to 17.03% and single-template stage tying (-27.6% parameters) plateaus at 66.10%, alternating two shared block templates ([B1, A, B, A]) in Stage 6 cuts total model parameters by 10.9% (582K weights) and recovers by +10.82 points in a single epoch to 70.92% (-1.84 points vs. unshared control).

</details>


### [6] [NIRJALA: Draining the Sea out of Gaussian Splatting with Three-Path Optics and Reversible Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23450)
*Latchan Chhetri,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NIRJALA三维高斯泼溅框架，通过三路径成像模型TRIVENI与可逆物理锚定PRATIBIMBA，联合恢复水下场景的去水外观与几何，并构建SAJALA合成数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下高斯泼溅方法仅建模直接透射衰减与后向散射两项效应，并以随距离指数衰减的透射率做除法去水，导致反演增益随距离无界增长，水下图像形成中的多解性（场景与水体组合共用同一像素）未被约束。

Method: 提出三路径图像形成模型TRIVENI（直接透射、后向散射与距离饱和的前向散射项），证明其反演增益在所有距离上有界，并给出后向散射与衰减估计误差向恢复颜色传播的界；提出可逆物理锚定PRATIBIMBA，将柔性神经水体模型与十参数物理模型双向绑定，使场景内容无法隐藏于水体中；以SAMARASA整合物理约束目标，包含距离分箱颜色先验；构建SAJALA数据集（30个Blender Cycles路径追踪场景，含无水图像、深度、法线与受控水色与浑浊度）用于真值评估。

Result: 在SAJALA上相对最强基线，PSNR由24.34提升至24.76 dB，深度AbsRel由0.109降至0.091；在真实SeaThru-NeRF场景上LPIPS由0.194降至0.175，渲染速度44 FPS。论文还讨论水下外观指标与几何指标不一致之处及评估局限。

Conclusion: 将前向散射纳入成像模型并施加可逆物理约束，可获得有界反演的稳定去水重建，在外观与几何指标上均优于现有方法，并保持实时渲染性能。

Abstract: Water does not just tint an underwater photograph; it rewrites it. Each pixel mixes attenuated scene radiance, backscattered light, and scene light scattered forward on its way to the camera, and many combinations of scene and water explain the same pixels. Most Gaussian-splatting methods for underwater scenes model only the first two effects and remove the water by dividing by a transmission that decays exponentially with range, so their inversion gain grows without bound. We present NIRJALA (Sanskrit for "without water"), a 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that recovers water-free appearance and geometry together. TRIVENI, a three-path image formation model, adds a range-saturating forward-scattering term to direct transmission and backscatter; we prove that this keeps the inversion gain bounded at every range and bound how errors in the estimated backscatter and attenuation propagate into the recovered colours. PRATIBIMBA, a reversible physics anchor, ties a flexible neural water model to a ten-parameter physical model in both directions, so scene content cannot hide inside the water. SAMARASA collects the physics-informed objectives, including a range-binned colour prior. To score water removal against ground truth, we built SAJALA ("with water"), a 30-scene suite path-traced in Blender Cycles with water-free images, depth, and normals under controlled water colour and turbidity. On SAJALA, NIRJALA raises PSNR from 24.34 to 24.76 dB and lowers depth AbsRel from 0.109 to 0.091 against the strongest baseline; on real SeaThru-NeRF scenes it lowers LPIPS from 0.194 to 0.175 while rendering at 44 FPS. We also discuss where appearance metrics and geometry disagree underwater, and the limits of our evaluation.

</details>


### [7] [mmTherm: Absolute 3D Human Pose Estimation from Low-Resolution Thermal Images and mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23477)
*Zhaoxin Chang,Badii Jouaber,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmTherm 是一个轻量级多模态框架，利用热成像估计身体结构与关节方向、利用毫米波雷达提供距离信息，显式结合二者互补的几何特性，实现隐私保护下的绝对 3D 人体姿态估计与全局定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对 3D 人体姿态估计需同时恢复姿态与全局位置。毫米波雷达具备测距与测向能力且保护隐私，但点云稀疏导致问题欠约束；低分辨率热成像可补充身体方向与结构信息。然而作者评估发现，强特征级融合基线在跨受试者泛化时丢失大部分定位与姿态优势，其径向定位甚至不如纯雷达基线，说明简单特征融合未有效利用两模态的几何互补性。

Method: 提出 mmTherm 轻量框架：不再做黑箱式特征级融合，而是显式解耦几何量——由热图像估计身体结构与关节方向，由雷达提供距离（range）信息，二者结合完成人体定位与 3D 姿态重建。模型参数与推理计算量显著低于对应基线。

Result: 在 OctoNet 数据集上，相较最强对应基线，mmTherm 将跨用户绝对关节误差降低 33%，根节点定位误差降低 38%，同时推理阶段参数量与计算量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 显式建模热成像与毫米波雷达的几何互补性，比特征级融合更能在跨受试者场景下保持定位与姿态精度，并兼顾轻量化与隐私保护，是绝对 3D 人体姿态估计的可行方向。

Abstract: Absolute 3D human pose estimation (HPE) aims to recover both a person's body pose and their global position in 3D space. Privacy-preserving millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides distance and direction measurement capabilities, but its sparse point clouds make HPE under-constrained. Low-resolution thermal imaging can provide complementary observations of body direction and structure. However, our evaluation shows that a strong feature-level fusion baseline loses much of its localization and HPE advantage when generalizing to unseen subjects, with radial localization becoming less accurate than a radar-only baseline. Motivated by this finding, we introduce mmTherm, a lightweight framework that explicitly incorporates the complementary geometric properties of thermal imaging and mmWave radar. It combines body structure and joint directions estimated from thermal images with range information from radar to localize the body and reconstruct its 3D pose. Experiments on OctoNet show that mmTherm reduces cross-user absolute joint error by 33% and root localization error by 38% relative to the strongest corresponding baselines, while requiring substantially fewer parameters and less computation at inference.

</details>


### [8] [Reference-Guided Data Curation for 3D Medical Vision-Language Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23484)
*Faidra Patsatzi,Ashwin Kumar,Chong Wang,Curtis P. Langlotz,Reinhard Heckel,Akshay S. Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种离线、免训练、参考集引导的数据筛选方法，为大模型预训练从海量未整理医学图文对中优先选出高质量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向多机构收集的大规模医学影像数据集质量参差：冗余、类别不平衡、机构间异质性显著，而高质量参考集往往规模有限，需要在不额外训练的前提下完成数据筛选以提升3D视觉语言模型预训练效率。

Method: 以小型高质量参考集为锚，对大规模未整理池的每一模态独立提取质量信号，再通过秩聚合（rank aggregation）融合为单一排序，据此指导样本选择；流程为离线、免训练。

Result: 在3D腹部CT（两机构共35k体数据-报告对）上，仅用约1/3.3训练数据与5k参考样本即达到Merlin基线的零样本疾病分类性能；在2D胸片（58k图文对）上，用至多1/10训练数据与仅74个参考样本即持平或超越全量池训练模型。消融显示选择对参考集重采样稳定、更小参考集仍优于随机采样、标签重平衡无增益。

Conclusion: 参考集引导的秩聚合筛选可在显著降低数据量与标注依赖的同时保持或提升零样本诊断性能，且方法通用（2D/3D）、可复现（已开源）。

Abstract: Training 3D vision-language models (VLMs) on large medical imaging datasets collected across institutions is constrained by data quality. Vast, uncurated pools of image-report pairs are characterized by redundancy, class imbalance, and cross-institutional heterogeneity. We address this challenge of data curation for improving 3D VLM pretraining by proposing an offline, training-free, reference-guided data curation recipe. Our approach leverages a small, high-quality reference set to independently extract quality signals for each modality of a large, uncurated pool and merges them through a rank-aggregation step into a single ranking that guides sample selection. We validate this method on datasets comprising 35k 3D abdominal CT volume-report pairs from two institutions and 58k 2D chest X-ray image-report pairs. On 3D CT, using our reference-guided data curation strategy, our curated model matches the zero-shot disease classification performance of a state-of-the-art baseline (Merlin) while using $3.3\times$ less training data and a reference set of 5k samples. On 2D chest X-rays, our method yields a curated model that matches or exceeds the zero-shot disease classification performance of a model trained on the full pool while using up to $10\times$ less training data and a reference set of only 74 samples. Ablations show that the selection is stable under resampling of the reference, that smaller reference sets still outperform random sampling, and that label rebalancing does not improve performance. To support reproducibility, we release our code.

</details>


### [9] [GRID-UAV: Geometry and Ray-depth Integrated Decision-making for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23491)
*Anas Anwarul Haq Khan,Amrin Kareem,Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Biplab Banerjee,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRID：将单目深度射线校正提升为UAV机体系3D位置，并在训练时用冻结的多视图几何模型对视觉特征做隐式对齐，二者互补，在TravelUAV各划分上取得SOTA成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA的2D编码器缺乏3D结构；直接输入估计深度在长航程无人机任务中无显著增益（42.5% vs 42.6%），需研究3D信息应以何种形式注入策略。失败分析显示显式3D提升与隐式几何对齐各有相反缺陷。

Method: GRID 同时采用两种3D接地：对前视与下视两路相机，将单目深度提升为机体坐标系下经射线校正的3D位置点；训练阶段仅用冻结的多视图几何模型对视觉特征做对齐（无推理开销）。

Result: 显式提升在隐式对齐基础上将到达目标率从61.5%提升至69.5%（p<0.001）；隐式对齐在显式提升基础上将到达后完成任务率从57.7%提升至61.4%（p<0.05）。TravelUAV（3,005测试回合）各划分成功率最优，相对SOTA提升9.5%（已见）、17.1%（未见物体）、25.4%（未见地图）。

Conclusion: 3D信息的注入形式比其本身更关键；显式几何提升与隐式几何对齐在失败模式上互补，二者结合可同时改善导航避障与目标处任务完成。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) encode images with 2D encoders that capture little 3D structure. A common approach is to feed the policy estimated depth, but a UAV chooses its own altitude, must judge distances tens of meters away, where monocular depth is less reliable, and must relate its forward and downward views. On long aerial routes, adding depth features directly brings no significant gain (42.5% vs. 42.6% success), so we study how 3D information should be given to the policy. Analyzing flight failures, we find that two forms of 3D grounding fail in opposite ways: explicit 3D lifting of depth collides less on the way but more often fails to finish at the target, while implicit alignment with a 3D geometry model finishes more often but collides more. We propose GRID, which combines both: (1) it lifts monocular depth into ray-corrected 3D positions in the UAV's frame for both cameras, and (2) during training only, it aligns its visual features with a frozen multi-view geometry model, at no inference cost. GRID adding explicit lifting to implicit alignment significantly increases how often the policy reaches the target ($61.5\% \rightarrow 69.5\%$, $p<0.001$), and adding implicit alignment to explicit lifting significantly increases how often it completes the task once there ($57.7\% \rightarrow 61.4\%$, $p<0.05$). On TravelUAV (3,005 test episodes), GRID achieves the best success rate on every split, with relative gains over the state of the art of 9.5% (seen), 17.1% (unseen objects) and 25.4% (unseen map).

</details>


### [10] [MedHarness: A Unified Budgeted Runtime for Reliable Medical Imaging Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23501)
*Haofan Wu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHarness：面向医学影像智能体的注册式多表面运行时可靠性层，将答后控制重构为共享决策对象挂载，用单一策略+表面配方适配器统一实现停止/补证/修复/拒绝控制。512例实测错误接受降约47%，接受精度83.2% vs 72.9%，远程调用降约71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像智能体已能在异构成像表面返回答案，但验证预算有限，每次错误接受都会损害临床信任。浅层核查与深层证据复核在同一病例上不一致时，缺乏共享控制器使"停止、开启更多证据、修复还是拒绝"难以良定义。现有工具型工作流要么按数据集锁定表面专属证据顺序，要么私设始终全深度验证路径，成本高、错误接受多，且无法跨模态共享同一控制器。

Method: 提出MedHarness——注册式多表面运行时可靠性层，把答后控制重构为proposer初始预测之后的共享决策对象挂载。三条设计原则：Shared Decision Object（共享决策对象）、Minimum-Sufficient Verification（最小充分验证）、Intermediate Repair（中间修复）。由单一共享策略加表面配方适配器实现；新成像表面通过配方注册即可接入同一stop/open-evidence/repair/reject控制器。

Result: 512例实时池：相对全量验证基线，错误接受降低约47%；在86.1%覆盖率下接受精度83.2% vs 基线72.9%（基线为全量放行）。在失败富集的多表面压力排序上，匹配上下文负载设置下每例估计远程服务调用降低约71%。留出的三表面检验与替代语言模型迁移保持相同控制排序。

Conclusion: 单一共享控制策略配合轻量表面配方适配器即可在多成像表面上统一管理验证预算，显著减少错误接受与远程调用，并具备跨表面与跨模型的可迁移性；共享决策对象挂载是可行的答后可靠性范式。

Abstract: Medical imaging agents already return answers on heterogeneous imaging surfaces, yet deployment still faces a limited verification budget where each wrong accept can harm clinical trust. When a thin check and a deeper evidence pass disagree on the same case, deciding when to stop, open more evidence, repair, or reject without a shared controller remains ill-posed. Tool-using workflows often lock a surface-specific evidence order per dataset, or a private always-open full-depth verification path; those pipelines stay costly, leave many wrong accepts, and cannot share one controller across modalities. We present MedHarness, a registered multi-surface runtime reliability layer that reformulates post-answer control as shared decision-object attachment after a proposer's initial prediction. Under Shared Decision Object, Minimum-Sufficient Verification, and Intermediate Repair, one shared policy with surface recipe adapters lets a new imaging surface join the same stop / open-evidence / repair / reject controller by recipe registration. On a 512-case live pool, MedHarness cuts wrong accepts versus a full-verification baseline by about 47% and raises accepted accuracy to 83.2% vs. 72.9% at 86.1% coverage versus full release under that baseline. On a failure-enriched multi-surface stress ranking, the same controller cuts estimated remote service calls per case by about 71% versus that baseline under a matched context-load setting. A held-out three-surface check and an alternate language-model transfer keep the same control ordering. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MedHarness-paper-release-A7F3/.

</details>


### [11] [Trust the Ray, Not the Depth: Ray-Lateral Agreement for Multi-View 3D Human Pose Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23513)
*Farnoosh Koleini,Ahmed Helmy,Pu Wang,Xiwu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ray-Lateral Agreement (RLA)，先沿各视角视线消除单目网格估计的深度偏置，再以射线精度为权做闭式最大似然融合，并用射线横向残差实现稳健评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标定多相机系统中，各相机后的单目网格估计结果相互偏差可达一米以上。根源是几何性的：每个估计的深度由网络假定的焦距决定而非相机实际焦距，误差几乎沿其自身视线方向，而标定恰好精确给出该方向。

Method: RLA遵循"信射线、不信深度"：先将每个估计沿自身射线滑动以去除深度偏置且保持形状不变；再逐点融合各视角，采用精度随视线变化的高斯误差模型，最大似然解为闭式，且在平均与中点三角化之间插值、在三角化奇异处仍良态。稳健性方面，用每个视角、每个点在其自身射线横向上的残差打分，避免完整残差把正确视角与错误视角一并惩罚。

Result: 在多相机公开基准数据集上，2、3、4相机配置及三种单视角姿态估计器下，RLA的最坏情况误差一致低于所有三角化基线。

Conclusion: 沿射线而非深度建模是消除单目多视角不一致的关键，射线横向一致性为融合与稳健加权提供了统一且闭式的框架。

Abstract: Put a monocular mesh estimator behind every camera of a calibrated rig, and the reconstructed bodies land a meter or more apart. The cause is geometric: each estimate sits at the depth implied by a focal length the network assumed, not the one the camera has. Its error therefore points almost entirely along its own viewing ray, and the calibration tells us that direction exactly. To address this, we introduce the Ray-Lateral Agreement (RLA), which follows one rule: trust the ray, not the depth. RLA first slides every estimate along its own rays, which removes the depth bias and leaves the shape untouched. It then fuses the views one point at a time, under a Gaussian error whose precision follows each viewing ray. The maximum-likelihood solution is closed-form, interpolates between averaging and midpoint triangulation, and stays well conditioned where triangulation is singular. Robustness is the harder half, because correct views also disagree along their rays. Any weight built on the full residual therefore punishes good views together with bad ones. Ray-lateral agreement instead scores every view, at every point, by its residual across that view's own ray alone. We evaluated RLA on multi-camera public benchmark datasets. Across two, three, and four cameras and three per-view pose estimators, RLA consistently achieves lower worst-case error than all triangulation baselines.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond the Sink Token: Sink-Guided Key Editing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23516)
*Hui Zhu,Saleh Zare Zade,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Xiangyu Zhou,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究VLM中sink-key方向对注意力分配的影响，并提出免训练的SHRINK方法，通过压制与sink对齐的关键分量提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注"为何sink token吸引注意力"，但未探究sink-key方向如何在自身位置之外影响对图像与问题token的注意力；本文希望厘清该机制并据此实现可控注意力引导。

Method: 先对四个VLM做探针实验：放大与正向sink对齐的分量会提升注意力、放大负向分量则降低注意力，且效应可延续到后续层，从而把原生sink key确立为注意力控制的参考。基于此提出SHRINK——一种免训练方法，在生成过程中对各可见key的sink对齐分量做部分削减。

Result: 在九个基准的采样推理下，LLaVA-OneVision-7B与Qwen2.5-VL-3B平均准确率分别提升5.1与2.7个百分点，Qwen2.5-VL-7B与Gemma-3-4B增益较小；此外，对sink对齐key分量进行重缩放可替代GIFT与ACG两种注意力引导方法的注意力操作，在幻觉基准上取得相当准确率。

Conclusion: sink-key方向是超越其自身位置、可泛化的注意力控制轴；SHRINK以极简的免训练改动带来稳定增益，并揭示现有注意力引导方法可被统一的key重缩放机制所解释与替代。

Abstract: In vision-language models (VLMs), a few sink tokens attract a disproportionate share of attention. While previous studies have examined why these tokens attract attention, we investigate how the sink-key direction affects attention to image and question tokens. Across four probed VLMs, boosting the magnitude of positive sink-aligned components increases attention on average, whereas boosting negative components decreases it; these responses persist through later layers. This identifies the native sink key as a reference for controlling attention beyond its own position. Motivated by this finding, we propose SHRINK, a training-free method that partially reduces sink-aligned components across visible keys during generation. On nine benchmarks under sampled reasoning, it improves mean accuracy by 5.1 and 2.7 percentage points on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-3B, with smaller gains on the other two main models, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Gemma-3-4B. Beyond SHRINK, rescaling sink-aligned key components can replace the attention operations of two existing attention-steering methods, GIFT and ACG, with comparable accuracy on hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [13] [ORGANIC3D: Grounding 3D CT Vision Language Models in Organ Level Image Text Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23529)
*Zelong Liu,Hamza Ahmed,Wynn Wingyi Lee,Mickael Torjman,Alexander Dimitrakakis,Jiahua Lu,Timothy Deyer,Bachir Taouli,Zahi A. Fayad,Xueyan Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ORGANIC3D框架，通过可控解剖干预（器官擦除、病灶替换、侧别扰动等）评测并提升3D CT视觉语言模型的器官级图文对齐，同时实现SOTA分类与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D CT分类上准确率高，但高准确率无法反映预测是否依赖正确的解剖证据，存在"对齐失败"风险；缺乏系统评估器官级图文对应关系的手段。

Method: 构建基准：用配对患者比较、器官擦除、病灶替换、侧别扰动评估12个视觉编码器检查点与7个生成式VLM；将每条发现句与解剖感知图像特征对齐，并在编辑后扫描上训练以锚定证据；扩展至生成式VLM，促进依赖证据的VQA与报告生成；仅用公开数据训练。

Result: 在CTRATE、Merlin及留出集RADChestCT上达到SOTA分类AUROC；侧别准确率77%（此前最高66%）；生成式VLM在VQA上匹配或超越所有已评估的公开VLM，报告准确率最高；器官移除后撤回96%~99%的目标器官发现，其他模型最高仅69%。

Conclusion: ORGANIC3D既是检验3D CT VLM是否基于解剖证据进行预测的评测框架，也能在提升预测准确率的同时改善器官级图文对齐，表明准确率与证据依从性可协同提升。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in 3D computed tomography (CT) classification, but high accuracy does not reflect whether their predictions rely on the correct anatomical evidence. We introduce ORGANIC3D, a framework for evaluating and improving organ level image text alignment through controlled anatomical interventions. Our benchmark assesses 12 vision encoder checkpoints and 7 generative VLMs using matched patient comparisons, organ erasure, lesion replacement, and laterality perturbations, revealing substantial alignment failures even among top performing models. To learn organ level image text correspondence, ORGANIC3D aligns each finding sentence with the anatomy aware image features and trains on edited scans to anchor this alignment in the organ's image evidence. We further extend this framework to generative VLMs, promoting evidence dependent question answering and report generation. Trained exclusively on public data, ORGANIC3D achieves SOTA classification AUROC on CTRATE, Merlin, and the heldout RADChestCT dataset and reaches 77% laterality accuracy, compared with at most 66% for prior models. Its generative VLM matches or exceeds all evaluated publicly released VLMs on visual question answering, achieves the highest report accuracy, and retracts 96 to 99% of target organ findings after organ removal, versus a maximum of 69% for other models. Together, these results establish ORGANIC3D as a framework for testing whether 3D CT vision language models ground their predictions in anatomical evidence and for improving organ level alignment alongside predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [14] [Layer-Matched Sparse Attention for Low-Redundancy 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23554)
*Sammam Mahdi,Fariha Binta Salim,K. M. Jubair Sami,Rakin Bin Rabbani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的逐层自适应稀疏注意力框架 LMSA：以"均值 token 所遗漏的 log-mass"作为逐层统计量，用其无行代理量（logit 方差）自动判定该层是"扩散层"还是"匹配层"，扩散层采用重要性采样的共享 hub keys 并配以无偏 log 权重，匹配层保持精确计算，从而在低冗余多视角场景下以更低代价逼近稠密注意力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 的全局注意力随视角数二次增长。现有免训练加速器采用统一稀疏化，在冗余视频上有效，但在视角间几何重叠很少时位姿精度显著下降。作者认为原因在于跨视角信息存在两类性质不同的部分，统一的稀疏近似无法同时保留：一类是"扩散层"，负责聚合分散的上下文信息；另一类是"匹配层"，依赖查询特异的交互，其注意力质量很小，均匀稀疏化会直接丢弃这些关键对应关系。

Method: 1) 引入逐层诊断统计量：度量均值 token 相对真实注意力所遗漏的 log-mass，用于刻画层的"可均匀化"程度；2) 采用其无行依赖的代理量——logit 方差——作为层选择器，避免需逐行计算；3) 对判定为扩散的层，用重要性采样选出的共享 hub keys 近似，并给出使 softmax 分子与分母均无偏的 log 权重；4) 对判定为匹配的层保留精确注意力（实验表明任何激进稀疏化都会损失精度）；5) 等预算下将该选择器与四种移位窗口方案对比，并作为事后工具用于选择 GPA-VGGT 需保护的层，以及未调参地迁移至 π³。

Result: 在 32 视角、13 个未见低冗余场景上，LMSA 与稠密注意力的 AUC@5 差距为 1.4，AVGGT 为 10.3、SAF3R 为 4.5；与同期 GPA-VGGT 的差异不显著，且仅相对 AVGGT 的提升同时通过两项场景级检验。在 100 视角下差距为 1.9 AUC@5，其聚合器运行速度快 2.0 倍。事后用于选择 GPA-VGGT 所保护的层，可在仅增加 5% FLOPs 的情况下带来 1.2 AUC@5 的提升。未调参应用于 π³ 时优于 AVGGT，但与稠密精度仍有较大差距。

Conclusion: 统一稀疏化失效的根源是扩散层与匹配层对近似误差的容忍度不同；用 logit 方差这一廉价统计量做逐层分派，并对扩散层使用无偏的 hub-key 估计，可在近稠密精度下获得显著加速，同时该统计量本身可作为通用的事后层选择工具迁移到其他 VGGT 类加速器与模型。

Abstract: VGGT reconstructs cameras and geometry from unposed images in one pass, but its global attention grows quadratically with the number of views. Training-free accelerators thin this attention and work well on redundant video, but lose pose accuracy when views share little geometry. This paper traces these losses to two kinds of cross-view information that a uniform sparse approximation can fail to preserve together: diffuse layers aggregate distributed context, whereas matching layers depend on query-specific interactions that carry little attention mass. A per-layer statistic measures the log-mass missed by a mean token. Its row-free proxy is the logit variance, and it selects the estimator for each layer. Diffuse layers use shared, importance-sampled hub keys, with log-weights that make the softmax numerator and denominator unbiased. Matching layers stay exact: every aggressive thinning tested cost accuracy. At equal cost, the selected layers outperform the four shifted windows tested. On 13 unseen low-redundancy scenes with 32 views, LMSA stays within 1.4 AUC@5 of dense attention, compared with 10.3 for AVGGT and 4.5 for SAF3R; its difference from contemporaneous GPA-VGGT is not significant, and only its gains over AVGGT pass both scene-level tests. With 100 views, it stays within 1.9 AUC@5 while its aggregator runs $2.0\times$ faster. Used post hoc to choose the layers GPA-VGGT protects, the statistic improves it by 1.2 AUC@5 for 5% more FLOPs. Applied untuned to $π^3$, it beats AVGGT but remains further from dense accuracy.

</details>


### [15] [DOCK: Dual-Stream-Inspired Self-Supervised Object-Centric Representations Grounded in Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23578)
*Pengju Liu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文诊断了潜空间规划器中"嵌入距离≈到目标距离"的隐含假设不成立，并提出DOCK学习与物理运动一致的物体表示，在21个任务中15个成功放置物体，媲美真值位置oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间规划器以预测未来图像嵌入与目标图像嵌入的距离作为代价，隐含假设嵌入空间距离能反映场景与目标的接近程度。作者怀疑该假设在冻结视觉编码器特征中并不成立，因此需要先诊断、再改进。

Method: 用仿真器执行的真实未来帧（消除预测误差）来检验假设，对6个冻结DINO与V-JEPA编码器测量特征距离与真实物体到目标距离的秩相关。随后受腹侧(what)与背侧(where/how)视觉通路启发提出DOCK：基于抓取物体随夹爪运动、用关节角前向运动学给出位移代理，从21个仿真厨房场景的9,951条持物搬运轨迹中挖掘404K位移对，训练三部分——从patch特征池化的物体token(what)、令读出坐标差等于实测位移的空间标尺(where)、物体token上的动力学预测器(how)。

Result: 冻结特征距离与物体到目标距离的秩相关最高仅0.11，而物体掩码质心达0.87；距离主要被背景与机器人patch主导，读出更善于捕捉机械臂而非物体。DOCK作为规划代价在21个任务中15个成功放置物体，与读取真实物体位置的oracle持平，掩码几何为10、冻结特征距离为9；在可见未见过的机械臂时仍解决最多任务，且在未适应的情况下其排序可迁移到含未见物体实例与类别的留出布局。

Conclusion: 冻结视觉编码器的嵌入距离并非可靠的目标接近度信号，学习与物理运动对齐的物体中心表示可显著提升潜空间规划性能，并具备跨布局、跨实例的迁移性。

Abstract: Latent-space planners select actions by comparing the embedding of a predicted future image with that of a goal image. This assumes that distance in the embedding reflects how far the scene is from the goal. We profile this assumption with simulator-executed futures, which removes prediction error. For six frozen DINO and V-JEPA encoders, feature distance barely tracks the object's distance to its goal: the rank correlation is at most 0.11, against 0.87 for object-mask centroids. It is dominated by background and robot patches, and frozen-feature readouts capture the arm far better than the object. Inspired by the ventral (what) and dorsal (where/how) visual streams, we propose DOCK, which learns an object representation whose changes agree with measured physical motion. A grasped object moves with the gripper, so forward kinematics on the joint angles gives a proxy for the object's displacement without annotation. From 9,951 held-object transport trajectories in 21 simulated kitchen scenes, we mine 404K displacement pairs and train three components: an object token pooled from patch features (what), a spatial ruler that makes differences of readout coordinates equal to measured displacements (where), and a dynamics predictor over object tokens (how). Used as a planning cost, the learned representation places the object in 15 of 21 tasks, as many as an oracle that reads the true object position, while mask geometry reaches 10 and frozen feature distance 9. It still solves the most tasks when the unseen robot arm is visible, and its ranking transfers to held-out layouts with unseen object instances and categories without any adaptation.

</details>


### [16] [ExiST: Existence-Aware Self-Training for Semi-Supervised Multi-Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23581)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Prakhar Kumar,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出存在感知自训练（ExiST），将"教师漏检的小器官"视为待定而非背景，避免半监督多器官分割中尾部器官被自训练循环抹除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督多器官分割需从少量标注扫描中学十余个器官，最小器官占前景不足1%。作者识别出该场景特有的自训练失效模式：教师漏检某小器官时，其全部体素被赋予高置信背景伪标签，学生被训练为"器官缺失"，多轮迭代后尾部器官被消除（去除胆囊伪标签使AMOS上其Dice从36.5降至1.0）。

Method: 核心观点是漏检器官应视为"未确定"而非"缺失"。1）用多个独立训练网络的留一法共识构建伪标签；2）用验证集上的质量门控筛选伪标签；3）逐扫描逐器官推断该器官是否被找到；4）对未确定器官，将其所在扫描的背景体素赋予集合值目标，采用边际交叉熵（接受任一答案）与跳过不确定体素的Dice项联合训练。作者证明该损失既不将候选器官推向缺失，也不偏向集合中的某一成员，把决策权留给标注证据。

Result: 在Synapse（20%标注）上平均Dice达77.1，AMOS（5%标注）上达75.9，优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 自训练中的漏检应被建模为不确定性而非阴性监督；集合值目标配合边际交叉熵可有效防止尾部器官消失，在极少标注下取得领先性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised multi-organ segmentation should learn more than a dozen organs from a handful of labeled scans, and the smallest organs occupy less than one percent of the foreground. We identify a failure mode of self-training that is specific to this regime. When a teacher misses a small organ in a scan, every voxel of that organ receives the confident pseudo-label background, and the student is trained toward the organ absence. Repeated over many scans, this loop extinguishes tail organs. In a pilot study, removing the gallbladder from the pseudo-labels reduced its Dice on AMOS from 36.5 to 1.0. We argue that a missing organ should be treated as unresolved rather than absent and propose Existence-aware Self-Training (ExiST). It builds pseudo-labels by leave-one-out consensus of independently trained networks, screens them with a quality gate on validation scans, and infers, per scan and per organ, whether the organ was found. For every unresolved organ, the background voxels of that scan receive the set-valued target, trained with a marginal cross-entropy that accepts either answer and a Dice term that skips uncertain voxels. We show that this loss never pushes a candidate organ toward absence and never favors one member of the set, which leaves the decision to labeled evidence. ExiST outperforms the current state-of-the-art methods and reaches 77.1 mean Dice on Synapse with 20% labeled scans and 75.9 on AMOS with 5% labeled scans.

</details>


### [17] [GRAFT: Graph-Regularized Prototype Fields for Realistic Test-Time Adaptation of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23591)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAFT将适配原型建模为"文本锚点+位移场"，借助语义类图上的高斯马尔可夫随机场先验实现部分池化，并引入类别存在模型，以闭式、无梯度的推理方式解决小批量少类与相关数据流下转导/测试时适配劣于零样本基线的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签测试数据本可改进视觉-语言模型的零样本分类器，但转导与测试时适配方法在批次中仅含少量词表类别或数据流相关时，常常低于零样本基线。作者将失败归因于共同设计缺陷：每个类拥有从自身样本独立估计的参数，导致缺失类或稀有类停留在有偏的文本锚点或被外来样本错误拟合。

Method: 提出图正则锚点位移场转导（GRAFT）：把适配后的原型表示为文本锚点加一个位移场，该位移场通过高斯马尔可夫随机场先验在语义类图上进行部分池化，并配合显式的类别存在模型。推理在缓存特征上进行，为闭式解、无需梯度。锚点式收缩与单一全局平移是该先验的两个极端设置；缺失类可证明为存在类的调和延拓，池化强度可由经验贝叶斯选取。

Result: 在11个基准与4种ImageNet偏移上，于贴近实际的批处理与在线协议下，GRAFT在所有设置中均优于零样本模型；在仅存在少量类别的场景中优于基于锚点的转导方法。

Conclusion: 通过类图上的部分池化位移场与类别存在建模，GRAFT在少类、相关数据流等困难条件下稳定超越零样本基线，且推理闭式、高效，为转导式视觉-语言适配提供了稳健方案。

Abstract: Unlabeled test data can improve the zero-shot classifier of a vision-language model, yet transductive and test-time adaptation methods often fall below the zero-shot baseline when only a few vocabulary classes are present in a batch or when streams are correlated. We trace this failure to a shared design: every class owns independent parameters estimated from its own samples, so absent or rare classes are left at biased text anchors or fitted to foreign samples. We propose Graph-Regularized Anchor-displacement Field Transduction (GRAFT), which models the adapted prototypes as text anchors plus a displacement field that is partially pooled over a semantic class graph through a Gaussian Markov random field prior, together with an explicit class-presence model. Inference is closed-form and gradient-free on cached features. Anchor-only shrinkage and a single global shift are the two extreme settings of the prior; absent classes are provably harmonic extensions of the present ones, and the pooling strength can be chosen by empirical Bayes. Under the realistic batch and online protocols on eleven benchmarks and four ImageNet shifts, GRAFT improves over the zero-shot model in every setting and over anchor-based transduction where few classes are present.

</details>


### [18] [VIBRA: Backward-Compatible Cross-Modal Retrieval with Consensus Barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23606)
*Shubing Yang,Xutong Liu,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIBRA：利用多视图质心作为旧空间中的查询来匹配旧图库相关性，并训练新编码器继承该共识，实现向后兼容跨模态检索，在50个设置中均为最优，R@1最高提升5.6/18.0点，延迟增加<1ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态检索系统升级时若重新编码数十亿条目代价过高，需向后兼容检索：新编码器直接查询旧模型已编码的图库。现有兼容方法要么仅拟合旧查询、精度停留在旧模型水平，要么对每个查询同时运行新旧编码器，成本高。

Method: 提出VIBRA：在旧图库上匹配相关性；用多个视图的质心纠正单一模型的错误，该质心本身是旧空间中的一个查询；再训练新编码器继承这一共识，使部署时不存在的模型仍能塑造每个查询。理论上证明：当各视图最难的无关项不同时，质心相对视图平均获得间隔增益，并刻画了单个编码器能达到的增益份额。

Result: 在6个CLIP、MetaCLIP、SigLIP2骨干的5种升级组合、5个基准共50个设置中，VIBRA均为最佳兼容方法；Recall@1较最强基线最高提升5.6点，较旧模型最高提升18.0点；新编码器延迟增加不足1ms。

Conclusion: VIBRA以近乎零额外开销实现高效的向后兼容跨模态检索升级，兼顾旧图库复用与精度超越旧模型，是当前最优的兼容方案。

Abstract: Backward-compatible retrieval enables a new encoder to query the gallery that an old model has already encoded, so that a cross-modal retrieval system can be upgraded without re-encoding billions of items. Existing compatible methods either fit the old query and stay near the accuracy of the old model, or run the old and the new encoder for every query. We propose VIBRA, which matches relevance on the old gallery, corrects the errors of a single model with the barycenter of several views, which is itself a query in the old space, and trains the new encoder to inherit this consensus, so that models absent at deployment still shape every query. We show that the barycenter gains margin over the average of its views exactly when their hardest irrelevant items differ, and we characterize the share of this gain that one encoder can reach. On five benchmarks with five upgrades among six CLIP, MetaCLIP and SigLIP2 backbones, VIBRA is the best compatible method in all 50 settings, improving Recall@1 by up to 5.6 points over the strongest baseline and by up to 18.0 points over the old model, while adding less than 1 ms to the latency of the new encoder.

</details>


### [19] [Weight Space Programming for Vision Language Model Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23615)
*Avinash Goutham Aluguvelly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在SmolVLM-256M与LFM2-VL-450M中植入权重故障，检验编码智能体在无梯度、直接编辑权重时修复网络的能力来源。核心发现：修复依赖故障计算过程在低比特位留下的"痕迹"（16位原始权重中的32位运算残差）；抹去低比特后智能体无法精确修复，而提示中一句指明统计量的语句可将编辑引导到受损行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权空间编程（weight-space programming）让语言模型无梯度地直接改写网络权重，但此类"修复"究竟依赖何种信息尚不清楚。作者希望厘清成功的权重修复是靠真实的统计/结构证据，还是靠故障生成过程中遗留的数值痕迹，从而为这一研究范式建立可证伪的评测规范。

Method: 在SmolVLM-256M与LFM2-VL-450M两个视觉语言模型的权重中植入人为故障（projector中的行缩放、视觉编码器中的噪声）。由编码智能体在不知故障位置的前提下进行编辑，并逐条与原始权重比对。设置关键对照：将权重舍入以抹去32位运算在16位权重中留下的低位痕迹，检验智能体是否仍能定位与精确还原；再通过重新标记低位、或在提示中加入一句指明特定统计量（存储值分布、行范数）的语句，测试修复是否恢复。此外测试交换行/列这类保持取值集合不变的故障。

Result: 智能体无需被告知位置即可发现两个模型中projector的缩放行与视觉编码器的噪声，推断未知缩放因子，将大部分或全部缩放权重精确还原为原值，并将准确率恢复至接近原始水平；由其撰写的程序在无反馈条件下可修复同模型未见过的检查点。舍入抹除低位后，模型对测试问题的回答不变，但智能体无法再精确还原任何权重：在SmolVLM上完全找不到损坏，在LFM2-VL单次会话中唯一收益来自对答案token嵌入的缩放（会改变回答"yes"的频率）。重新在低位标记受损权重可使编辑重新定位。舍入后的权重仍在存储值分布与行范数中保留缩放的统计证据，一句点名该统计量的提示即可在两个模型上把编辑导向受损行。交换行或列在会话预算内均未被自主发现。

Conclusion: 所谓成功的权重修复在很大程度上依赖故障计算过程遗留的低比特痕迹，而非仅凭权重本身的统计结构；抹去痕迹后精确修复失败，但统计证据在恰当提示下仍可被利用。作者建议权重修复类研究必须报告权重的数值格式，并纳入"舍入对照"（rounded control）作为标准实验条件。

Abstract: In weight-space programming, a language model edits a network's weights directly, without gradients. We ask what a successful repair of this kind relies on. We plant faults in the weights of two vision-language models, SmolVLM-256M and LFM2-VL-450M, and check each edit made by a coding agent against the original weights. Without being told where to look, the agent finds rescaled rows in the projector and noise in the vision encoder of both models, infers the unknown scale factors, restores most or all of the rescaled weights to their exact original values, and returns accuracy to near its original level; programs that it writes from such weights repair unseen checkpoints of the same model without feedback. These repairs depend on a trace of how the faults were computed. The original weights have 16-bit precision, and faults computed in 32-bit arithmetic leave low bits that only the damaged weights carry. Rounding those bits away leaves each model's answers to our test questions unchanged, and the agent, unaided, then restores no weight exactly: on SmolVLM it no longer finds the damage, and in a single session on LFM2-VL its only gain comes from rescaling answer-token embeddings, which changes how often the model answers yes. Marking the damaged weights again in their low bits brings the edits back to them. Rounded weights still hold statistical evidence of the rescaling, in the distribution of stored values and in row norms, and one sentence in the prompt that names such a statistic directs the agent's edits to the damaged rows on both models. Swapped rows or columns, which leave the set of stored values unchanged, are not found unaided on either model within our session budgets. We recommend that studies of weight repair report the number format of the weights and include a rounded control.

</details>


### [20] [Isthmus: Bridging Supervision Islands Restores Uncertainty and Stability in One-Step Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23616)
*Luming Yang,Siqing Li,Haoxian Liu,Zhenyu Bu,Jiazheng Zhu,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一步流映射作为后验采样器会丢失不确定性并变得不稳定；作者将原因归结为训练时对(起点、终点)时间对的放置，提出"isthmus"方法，将5%训练对沿三角形整条边分布以连接孤立区域，从而在不改网络、损失与推理代码、不增成本的前提下改善一步采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步流映射被日益用作后验采样器，其采样点的价值在于样本所携带的不确定性。然而作者发现一步采样器会丢失这一不确定性、稳定性下降：与同一网络的多步采样相比，其样本方差减小且去相关；直接对一步跳跃进行监督会进一步恶化，样本向条件均值坍缩，延长训练时更会崩溃。作者旨在定位这些失败的根源并加以修复。

Method: 作者将两类失败归因于训练中起点—终点时间对的放置位置：只有时间三角形对角线上的时间对能从数据获得监督目标，而一步采样所用的时间对要么未被监督，要么位于"孤岛"上——即一组不被自一致性恒等式连接到对角线的对，目标函数既无法确定一步映射，也无法锚定其训练。为连接该孤岛，作者提出"isthmus"：把5%的训练对沿连接一步对与对角线的三角形整条边放置，从而使一步映射的误差可由沿该边的自一致性残差所界定。

Result: 在网络、损失与推理代码均不变且不增加成本的情况下，isthmus在多种流映射目标、MRI重建、人脸修复以及CIFAR-10与ImageNet 32×32无条件生成任务上均改善了一步采样。在脑部MRI上，它将一步采样的校准度恢复至0.96、与多步样本的相关性恢复至0.93；这两项指标在不同随机种子间波动显著减小，并且在训练延长至三倍时长时该一致性依然成立。

Conclusion: 一步流映射的不确定性丢失与不稳定性源于时间对分布的结构缺陷（一步对未被自一致性恒等式锚定）。通过isthmus对时间对分布进行低成本、无需改动模型的修正，即可恢复一步采样的校准性与稳定性，并在多任务、多目标及更长训练下保持稳健。

Abstract: One-step flow maps are increasingly used as posterior samplers, where the point of sampling is the uncertainty that the samples carry. We find that the one-step sampler loses this uncertainty and becomes less stable. Compared with multi-step samples of the same network, its samples lose variance and decorrelate. Supervising the one-step jump directly makes this worse; the samples collapse further toward the conditional mean and break down when training is extended. We trace both failures to the locations where training places its pairs of start and end times. Only pairs on the diagonal of the time triangle receive targets from data, and the pair used for one-step sampling is either left unsupervised or supervised on an island, a set of pairs that the self-consistency identity does not connect to the diagonal, where the objective neither determines the one-step map nor anchors its training. We bridge the island with an isthmus, which places 5% of the training pairs along the whole edge of the triangle that joins the one-step pair to the diagonal. The error of the one-step map is then bounded by the self-consistency residual along this edge. With the network, the loss and the inference code unchanged and at no added cost, the isthmus improves one-step sampling across several flow-map objectives, in MRI reconstruction, face inpainting, and unconditional generation on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet 32x32. On brain MRI, it restores one-step calibration to 0.96 and the correlation with multi-step samples to 0.93; both vary far less across seeds, and the agreement holds when training is extended to three times its length.

</details>


### [21] [PRISM: Profiling and Representation of Individual Stereotypical Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23646)
*Austin A. MBaye,Savik Kinger,Matthew S. Goodwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM表征：用跟踪掩码与稠密光流刻画重复性运动，弥补姿态关键点无法完整描述运动的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖学姿态提供可解释的关节结构，但不存在固定物理关键点集合能完整刻画一个人的运动；需补充性表征。

Method: PRISM由四项测量构成：躯干相对光流测强度、逐帧掩码变化测空间范围、带符号光流回归估计频率、持续滑窗几何描述节律性；并用1,890个合成条件、理想化手部运动、受控损坏实验、两种姿态方法审计及36,930段外部骨架片段验证。

Result: 合成条件可恢复受控的速度、幅度与频率并揭示观测极限；理想手部运动显示相同COCO-17轨迹下被遗漏的变异；在6名被试1,404个标注刻板运动片段中总光流与姿态运动收敛，但同坐标样本与重复会话画像在强度、范围、频率、节律上存在差异；VLM对比评估定量频率估计，画像条件解码器将测量映射为示例骨架运动。

Conclusion: PRISM将数值画像与可见样例相连，提供可解释方式比较同一行为在标签或事件计数之外的具体表达差异。

Abstract: An anatomical pose provides interpretable joint structure, but no fixed set of physical landmarks can fully specify a person's movement. We introduce PRISM, a complementary representation that characterizes repetitive movement using tracked masks and dense optical flow. Torso-relative flow measures intensity, frame-wise mask variation measures spatial extent, signed-flow recurrence estimates frequency, and persistent sliding-window geometry describes the rhythmicity of the movement. Across 1,890 synthetic conditions, the measurements recover controlled speed, amplitude, and frequency while exposing observation limits. An idealized hand-motion experiment demonstrates variation omitted by identical exact COCO-17 trajectories; controlled corruption experiments separately examine measurement sensitivity. Audits of two pose methods on the same data cohort and 36,930 external skeleton clips document incomplete observation and dependence on processing choices. In 1,404 annotated complex stereotypical motor movement bouts from six autistic participants, total flow converges with pose motion, while matched-coordinate examples and repeated-session profiles show differences in intensity, extent, frequency, and rhythmicity within behavior labels. A separate VLM comparison evaluates quantitative frequency estimation, and a profile-conditioned decoder links the measurements to illustrative skeletal movements. PRISM connects numerical profiles to visible examples, providing an interpretable way to compare how the same behavior is expressed beyond its label or event count.

</details>


### [22] [LightSEAD: Lightweight SE(3)-Aware Keypoint Detector through Selective Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23661)
*Mayowa Adebambo,Alexander D. Rast,Andrew Bradley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级SE(3)感知关键点检测器LightSEAD，仅6层卷积、84289参数，通过结构化监督以更少资源实现有竞争力性能，混合精度下可达约909 FPS（RTX 4090）/139 FPS（Jetson Orin Nano Super）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习性能提升往往伴随架构复杂度与算力需求增长；作者探索相反方向，即通过结构化监督（structured supervision）以更少资源获得更多收益，面向资源受限平台的高效关键点检测。

Method: LightSEAD：轻量级SE(3)-aware关键点检测器，用标定后的Harris伪标签初始化，并结合视角感知（viewpoint-aware）与外观感知（appearance-aware）监督以及选择性知识蒸馏；网络仅6个卷积层。另有进一步优化的LightSEAD Coarse适配混合精度推理。

Result: 在HPatches、KITTI 06、4Seasons上取得有竞争力的重复性（repeatability），并在MegaDepth-1500上验证相对位姿估计的下游可用性；LightSEAD Coarse约909 FPS（NVIDIA RTX 4090）、139 FPS（Jetson Orin Nano Super）。

Conclusion: 结构化监督可在极小模型规模下维持关键点检测竞争力，并具备嵌入式实时推理能力，为高效特征检测提供了可行范式。

Abstract: The pursuit of improved performance in deep learning has often been accompanied by increasing architectural complexity and computational demands. We explore an alternative direction: achieving more with less through structured supervision. We introduce LightSEAD, a lightweight SE(3)-aware keypoint detector initialised with calibrated Harris pseudo-labels and enriched through viewpoint-aware and appearance-aware supervision and selective knowledge distillation. LightSEAD comprises only six convolutional layers and 84,289 learnable parameters, achieving competitive repeatability across HPatches, KITTI 06 and 4Seasons while demonstrating its downstream applicability to relative pose estimation on MegaDepth-1500. Further optimised for mixed-precision inference, LightSEAD Coarse achieves approximately 909 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 and 139 FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano Super.

</details>


### [23] [EyeDiT: Anatomy-Grounded Multi-Modal Eye Generation and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23670)
*Yonghan Lee,Zachary Willms,Kun Liu,Pushkar Anand,Ali Behrooz,Dmitri Model,Dinesh Manocha,Yatong An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EyeDiT：解剖学基础的多模态3D眼睛生成与仿真模型，用OCT、解剖点云与近眼图像训练，实现可控的瞳孔、身份与视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成眼数据生成器以图像为中心，缺乏高质量3D解剖结构，控制维度基本局限于视线方向，且不提供解剖标签，限制了AR/VR眼动追踪数据合成的多样性与标注可用性。

Method: 先训练多模态解剖VAE，将OCT、解剖点云与近眼图像编码为解剖感知隐表示；再训练Eye Latent DiT，以光轴、瞳孔位置与半径、受试者身份为条件生成隐样本；配合高斯解码器进行新视角仿真。

Result: 单视角新视角合成PSNR达24.9 dB，优于此前方法的21.8 dB；用其合成数据训练公开眼动网络，sim-to-real差距为3.5°（其他生成器为8.4°），接近真实数据训练效果，与真实数据结合后眼动误差进一步降至2.3°（仅真实数据为2.6°）。

Conclusion: 解剖学基础的3D生成可有效提升合成眼数据质量与实用性，缩小仿真到现实的差距，并可作为真实数据的补充提升眼动追踪性能。

Abstract: Synthetic eye data are critical for rapid development of eye tracking (ET) in AR/VR applications. However, existing synthetic eye generators are largely image-centric and lack a high-quality underlying 3D anatomy of the eye, which limits their control mainly to the gaze and provides no anatomical labels. To address this problem, we propose EyeDiT, an anatomy-grounded, multi-modal 3D eye generation and simulation model trained with optical coherence tomography (OCT), labeled anatomy point clouds and near-eye images. Our pipeline first trains a Multi-Modal Anatomy Variational Autoencoder (VAE), which encodes an anatomy-aware latent representation from all input modalities. Then, our Eye Latent DiT is trained to generate latent samples conditioned on the optical axis, the pupil position and radius, and the subject identity. Together with Gaussian decoders for novel-view simulation, our model enables control over the pupil, subject identity and viewpoint for data synthesis. We show that our model outperforms previous eye image synthesis models in novel-view synthesis (24.9 against 21.8 dB PSNR from a single view), and that a public ET neural network trained on our synthetic data shows a smaller sim-to-real gap than when trained on data from other generators (3.5$^\circ$ vs. 8.4$^\circ$ gaze error), approaches a network trained on real data, and further improves ET performance when combined with real data (2.3$^\circ$ vs. 2.6$^\circ$). More information is available on the project page: https://lyhan12.github.io/eyedit

</details>


### [24] [Image-Condition-Controlled Diffusion Network with Large-Scale CT Pretraining for CT-Free Attenuation Correction in Dynamic Cardiac PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23672)
*Zhejia Zhang,Scott M. Leonard,Jinkui Hao,Ryan J. Avery,Richard L. Weinberg,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LUCID框架，利用大规模无配对CT预训练的扩散模型从动态心脏82Rb PET的NAC帧合成μ-map，避免CT辐射与配准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态PET定量心肌灌注需可靠的衰减校正；临床μ-map多由CT导出，增加辐射暴露并可能因呼吸/心脏运动产生PET-CT配准误差。监督式无CT方法依赖稀缺的配对NAC PET与CT数据，而大规模无配对CT数据更易获取。

Method: LUCID：先用CNN从每帧NAC PET预测初始μ-map；再用在CT-RATE数据集10000例胸部CT上预训练的扩散骨干，以对应NAC帧经ControlNet为条件，对CNN预测的低噪声扰动做单步去噪精修；最后逐帧体素平均得最终μ-map。并提出体素级时间标准差（TSD）在无参考μ-map时量化帧间变异性。

Result: 在102例院内数据上评估，LUCID取得最佳定量精度，同时保持有竞争力的帧间一致性。消融显示：仅用训练患者院内CT预训练扩散骨干进行精修，精度反而不如单独CNN预测，凸显大规模CT预训练的必要性。

Conclusion: 大规模无配对CT预训练的扩散先验可有效迁移至动态心脏PET的μ-map合成，优于小规模配对/院内数据训练，为无CT衰减校提供可行路径。

Abstract: Positron emission tomography (PET) provides quantitative information on myocardial perfusion. In dynamic PET, tracer kinetics are tracked through a series of reconstructed time frames. Reliable attenuation correction is essential for accurate measurements. Clinical attenuation maps ($μ$-maps) are typically derived from computed tomography (CT), which adds radiation exposure and may introduce PET-CT misregistration due to respiratory and cardiac motion. Moreover, supervised CT-free methods rely on paired non-attenuation-corrected (NAC) PET and CT data, which are scarce, whereas large-scale unpaired CT data are more readily available. To address these challenges, we propose Large-scale Unpaired CT-Informed Diffusion (LUCID), a framework that transfers anatomical priors learned from 10,000 chest CT volumes in the public CT-RATE dataset to $μ$-map synthesis for dynamic cardiac $^{82}$Rb PET. First, a convolutional neural network (CNN) predicts an initial $μ$-map from each NAC PET frame. Second, the CT-pretrained diffusion backbone refines a low-noise perturbation of the CNN prediction through a single denoising step, conditioned on the corresponding NAC PET frame via ControlNet. Finally, frame-wise predictions are averaged voxel-wise to obtain the final $μ$-map. We further introduce the voxel-wise temporal standard deviation (TSD) to quantify inter-frame variability without requiring a reference $μ$-map. We evaluated LUCID on an in-house dataset of 102 patients and found that it achieved the best quantitative accuracy while maintaining competitive inter-frame consistency. Ablation studies showed that refinement with a diffusion backbone pretrained on in-house CT data from the training patients was less accurate than the CNN prediction alone, highlighting the importance of large-scale CT pretraining.

</details>


### [25] [Learning What to Degrade: A Recoverability-Aware Curriculum for Reliable Smart-Glasses Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23674)
*Yaopeng Wang,Qingliang Wang,Yilun Jin,Xiujin Liu,Han Yue,Chen Xia,Renjie Zhu,Chuang Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HR参考式语义评测协议，发现生成式SR会"编造"细节、复原式SR会遗留模糊证据两种失败模式；据此提出ReDiC课程（可复原性代理+相对难度自适应退化强度），仅改动训练退化生成，在NAFNet/SwinIR/MambaIR上PSNR约+2.5 dB、语义分数约+3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜图像退化会模糊小文本与细粒度结构，而这些正是VLM做视觉辅助所需的证据。现有评测多停留在像素级指标，未直接衡量"复原结果能否支撑VLM正确理解"；且生成式SR可能合成看似合理却错误的细节，复原式SR则可能保留模糊或歧义的关键证据，二者缺乏统一、忠实的评估与改进路径。

Method: 1) 评测：设计高分辨率（HR）参照的语义评测协议，用VLM在复原图上与在对应HR参考图上的输出进行对比。2) 改进：面向复原式SR的忠实细节恢复，提出ReDiC（Recoverability-Difficulty Curriculum）训练课程——结合与模型无关的"可复原性"代理指标和与模型相关的"复原难度"来动态调整训练退化强度：当输入可复原且复原容易时提高严重度，保持"有挑战但可复原"的状态，当估计可复原性低时降低严重度。3) ReDiC仅修改训练阶段的退化生成，不改变SR网络结构与重建目标，具有即插即用性。

Result: 在合成基准上，ReDiC用于NAFNet、SwinIR、MambaIR时，相较各自基线平均PSNR提升约2.5 dB，HR参考语义分数提升约3点；在200张真实智能眼镜拍摄图像上，LLM评测与人工评测均确认了进一步增益。

Conclusion: 像素级复原质量与下游语义可用性并不等价，需以HR为参照评估VLM输出。复原式SR应优先保证"可复原证据的忠实恢复"而非生成式补全。通过只在训练退化生成层面引入"可复原—难度"课程，即可在不改动模型架构的前提下，同时提升PSNR与语义级表现，并泛化到多种SR骨干与真实拍摄场景。

Abstract: Degradations in smart-glasses images obscure small text and fine-grained structures needed by vision-language models (VLMs) for visual assistance. We introduce an high-resolution (HR)-referenced semantic evaluation protocol that compares VLM outputs on restored images with those on corresponding HR references. Our evaluation reveals two failure modes: generative SR can synthesize misleading details, whereas restoration-oriented SR can leave critical evidence blurred or ambiguous. These findings motivate improving faithful detail recovery in restoration-oriented SR, where a key training challenge is to provide sufficiently difficult inputs while retaining recoverable visual information. We propose ReDiC (Recoverability-Difficulty Curriculum), which combines a model-independent recoverability proxy with model-relative restoration difficulty to adapt training degradation severity. ReDiC increases severity when inputs are recoverable and restoration is easy, maintains challenging but recoverable conditions, and reduces severity when estimated recoverability is low. It modifies only training degradation generation, leaving the SR architecture and reconstruction objective unchanged. Across NAFNet, SwinIR, and MambaIR, ReDiC improves average PSNR by approximately 2.5 dB and HR-referenced semantic scores by about 3 points over the corresponding baselines on synthetic benchmarks, with further gains confirmed by LLM-based and human evaluations on 200 real smart-glasses captures.

</details>


### [26] [Structured Extraction of Diffraction Data from Covalent Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23680)
*Shenghan Gao,Zhiling Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉大语言模型与经典计算机视觉结合的流水线，将文献中的PXRD图像自动还原为像素级校准的衍射曲线，构建开放数据库COF-PEAK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXRD是COF等材料的主要结构证据，但数十年的实测谱图仅以论文插图形式存在；现有图像格式差异大（单曲线、带标签的堆叠曲线、插图、相邻图），传统CV难以自动提取。

Method: 视觉LLM负责理解版面布局，定位衍射图及其曲线并给出粗略描迹；经典CV对每条描迹进行像素级精化，实现曲线校准与数字化。

Result: 处理3,954篇COF文献，获得11,445条校准衍射曲线并发布为COF-PEAK数据库；与60个面板的人工数字化结果相比，峰位中位偏差仅0.07°。

Conclusion: 该流水线可将图件转化为可复用实验数据，并支持追踪峰显著度（结晶度的潜在指标）随合成条件的变化。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) is the primary structural evidence for covalent organic frameworks (COFs) and many other materials. Yet decades of measured patterns exist only as images in published figures. Automatic recovery is difficult, because PXRD figures vary widely in format, from a single curve to crowded stacks with labels, insets, and neighboring plots that challenge traditional computer vision. This work presents a pipeline in which a vision large language model (LLM) interprets the layout of each figure, locating the diffraction plot and its curves and sketching a coarse trace of each, while classical computer vision (CV) refines every trace to pixel-level accuracy. Applied to 3,954 COF articles, the pipeline produces 11,445 calibrated diffraction curves, released in the open database COF-PEAK. Compared with a hand digitization of 60 panels, extracted peak positions differ by a median of only 0.07 degrees. Whereas papers usually report only a few peak angles, which limits further analysis, the digitized curves enable tracking how peak prominence, a possible marker of crystallinity, changes with synthesis conditions. Vision LLMs and classical CV can thus turn such figures into reusable experimental data.

</details>


### [27] [MoMoVL: Modality-Aware Adaptive Compression for On-Device Sparsely-Activated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23695)
*Dongkeun Lee,Wonjun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoMoVL：面向MoE视觉语言模型的模态感知自适应混合精度压缩框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧VLM需求增长，MoE结构虽计算高效但显存开销大；现有MoE量化工作忽略了视觉/文本token的模态特异特性对专家量化的影响。

Method: 基于"模态诱发专家差异"的实证发现，联合利用每专家激活剖面与每模态量化敏感度，指导比特宽度分配。

Result: 相比SOTA混合精度基线改善精度-压缩权衡；相对均匀精度基线内存占用最多降低15.44%，精度仅小幅下降。

Conclusion: 模态感知的细粒度比特分配可有效将MoE-VLM推理带入端侧资源预算；代码数据已开源。

Abstract: The rise of the agentic web has been accompanied by growing demand for on-device Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that perform diverse cross-modal web tasks with enhanced privacy and reduced latency. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely adopted in modern VLMs for compute-efficient scalability, yet their multi-expert architecture poses deployment challenges due to substantial memory demands. Mixed-precision quantization is an effective compression technique that reduces bit-widths while adapting to the heterogeneous hardware budgets of edge devices, particularly well-suited for MoE due to its inherent structural sparsity, which causes imbalanced importance among experts. While prior work has applied quantization for efficient MoE inference at the edge, we point out that in MoE-based VLMs, the modality-specific characteristics of visual and text tokens further impact expert quantization. To address this issue, we present MoMoVL, a modality-aware adaptive compression framework for MoE-based VLMs that brings vision-language inference within reach of on-device resource budgets. Motivated by our empirical findings on modality-induced disparities across experts, MoMoVL jointly exploits the per-expert activation profile and per-modality quantization sensitivity to guide bit-width assignment. Experimental results show that MoMoVL improves the accuracy-compression trade-off relative to state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines and reduces memory footprint by up to 15.44% with a modest drop in accuracy over its uniform-precision baseline. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/sco-edge/MoMoVL.

</details>


### [28] [What Do Learned 3D Models Add to Localization from Posed References?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23697)
*Jaehyun Kim,Wonseok Kang,Jeongmin Lee,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了学习式3D模型的相机预测与基于同一批参考图像的几何估计在少样本定位中的表现，指出在精细位姿阈值下几何估计加一张参考图即显著优于所有模型的双参考设置，且失败与否取决于参考选择与对齐方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量带位姿参考图像定位查询，可避免构建3D点图。作者追问：学习式3D模型的相机预测相对基于同一批参考的几何估计究竟带来什么增益，以及失败发生何处。

Method: 覆盖8个学习式模型及变体、5个测试集；比较参考数量预算、交换位姿分量、干预模型所见参考；引入共享接受门控的fallback机制；在NAVER江南站引入跨位置检索参考，并提出鲁棒对齐；开展受控实验，对远处参考位姿进行掩码并移出输入位姿归一化。

Result: 在精细位姿误差阈值下，多一张参考的几何估计在Cambridge与NAVER B1上显著优于所有模型的双参考结果，单独使用或作为门控后fallback均不显著更差；三参考时更优方法依场景而定。学习式平移幅值的增益依赖参考选择：在12-Scenes上用所选分散图像对可提升8个模型中的7个，而用检索排名前二参考时5个模型均无提升。跨位置参考下模型在不同阶段失败；鲁棒对齐使Pi3X与MapAnything与按需加参考的差距缩小至3.3个百分点。受控测试中，对齐邻近参考可基本恢复VGGT、Pi3X、MapAnything，但Depth Anything 3不行；位姿条件化的Pi3X仅在远处位姿被掩码并排除于输入位姿归一化时才能恢复。

Conclusion: 学习式模型相机预测的增益有限且高度依赖参考选择与对齐质量；几何估计配简单fallback门控是强基线，鲁棒对齐可显著缩小与学习式方法的差距。

Abstract: Localizing a query from a few posed reference images avoids building a 3D point map. We ask what the camera predictions of learned 3D models add to geometric estimation from the same references, and where failures arise. Across eight learned models and variants and five test sets, we compare reference budgets, swap pose components, and intervene on the references a model sees. At fine pose-error thresholds, geometric estimation with one additional reference is significantly more accurate than every model on two references on Cambridge and NAVER B1 and never significantly worse, alone or as a fallback behind a shared acceptance gate; with the same three references, the more accurate approach depends on the scene. The gain from the learned translation magnitude depends on reference selection: it improves seven of the eight models on 12-Scenes with the spread-out pairs we select, but none of the five we test with the two top-ranked retrieved references. Where retrieval also returns references from other locations, at NAVER Gangnam Station, models fail at different stages. A robust alignment brings Pi3X and MapAnything within 3.3 pp of adding references as needed. In controlled tests, aligning on the nearby references largely restores VGGT, Pi3X and MapAnything but not Depth Anything 3, and pose-conditioned Pi3X recovers only if the far poses are masked and left out of its input-pose normalization.

</details>


### [29] [A Hardware-Aware Comprehensive Study of Edge Vision Deployment with A Unified Hardware-Model-Runtime Mapping Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23705)
*Le Duc An,Le Duc Thanh,Vo Hoai Viet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理边缘视觉部署中硬件约束、模型架构与软件工具链的关联：将边缘硬件分为GPU/专用NPU/TPU、嵌入式MPU/SoC、MCU三层，聚合分类与检测多源基准结果，提出双层硬件—模型映射框架及设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习快速发展推动计算机视觉向资源受限边缘设备部署，但边缘硬件架构极度异构，导致视觉模型的选择与设计缺乏系统指导。现有工作缺乏对硬件约束、模型架构与部署工具链三者的整体贯通分析。

Method: 构建边缘硬件三层分类体系（中端GPU与专用NPU/TPU、嵌入式MPU/SoC、微控制器MCU）；聚合多源图像分类与目标检测基准结果，覆盖数十瓦至毫瓦级裸机MCU的算力谱；据此提出双层硬件—模型映射框架，给出按性能优先级（速度/均衡/精度）划分的模型选择矩阵，并提炼架构设计原则（峰值SRAM预算、分辨率优先缩放、算法—编译器协同设计）。

Result: 给出跨算力谱的实证基准分析，形成可操作的模型选择矩阵与三项架构设计准则，并识别关键部署瓶颈：工具链碎片化、算子不兼容以及Vision Transformer在边缘端的可行性。

Conclusion: 该综述弥合了边缘硬件约束、模型架构与部署工具链之间的鸿沟，为不同算力层级提供可量化的选型与设计指引，并展望端侧自适应与可持续Edge AI等未来方向。

Abstract: The rapid growth of deep learning has driven the deployment of computer vision directly on resource-constrained edge devices. However, the extreme heterogeneity of edge hardware architectures poses major challenges in selecting and designing suitable vision models. This survey bridges the gap between edge hardware constraints, model architectures, and deployment software toolchains. We establish a comprehensive taxonomy classifying edge hardware into three distinct tiers: mid-range devices (GPUs and dedicated NPUs or TPUs), dedicated embedded MPU or SoC processors, and microcontrollers (MCUs). We aggregate and analyze multi-source benchmark results for image classification and object detection across the computing spectrum, spanning from tens of watts down to milliwatt-scale bare-metal MCUs. Based on these empirical findings, we propose a two-tiered hardware-to-model mapping framework that provides concrete model selection matrices across performance priorities (Speed, Balanced, Accuracy) and actionable architectural design principles (peak SRAM budgeting, resolution-first scaling, and algorithm-compiler co-design). Finally, we highlight critical deployment challenges including toolchain fragmentation, operator incompatibility, and Vision Transformer feasibility, and outline promising future research directions in on-device adaptation and sustainable Edge AI.

</details>


### [30] [CoScan: Multi-Scale Content-Adaptive Space-Filling Scans for Causal State-Space Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23717)
*Wei Dong,Terry Ji,Yan Min,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoScan 提出一种内容自适应的扫描顺序设计，通过可微代理监督学习图像相关的空间填充遍历，并配合多尺度扫描构造与频率引导的起点选择，提升因果视觉 SSM 的复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型（SSM）在图像复原中潜力显著，但二维特征图需线性化为一维 token 序列以进行因果状态更新，导致性能高度依赖扫描顺序。现有确定性扫描虽遵循空间填充原则，却固定且与内容无关，在退化条件下易产生碎片化或语义不一致的上下文。

Method: 1) 通过由扫描质量目标驱动的可微代理监督，学习图像相关的空间填充遍历，目标鼓励短程连贯性与因果可预测性，同时保持空间邻接性；2) 提出基于细粒度最小生成树粗化的高效多尺度扫描构造，在多个特征分辨率上产生连贯扫描，以支撑分层复原骨干；3) 设计频率引导的起点选择策略，将信息丰富的结构置于序列前缀，强化因果上下文形成。

Result: 嵌入现有 Mamba 类骨干后，CoScan 在多个复原基准上取得一致性提升且开销极小，并在高层视觉任务上展现出良好前景。

Conclusion: 扫描顺序是因果视觉 SSM 的关键但被忽视的设计维度；内容自适应、多尺度一致且起点感知的扫描能够以极低代价显著改善复原性能，并具备向高层视觉任务迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Selective state-space models (SSMs) have recently shown strong potential for efficient image restoration. A central difficulty, however, is that a 2D feature map typically needs to be linearized into a 1D token sequence for causal state-space updates, making restoration performance highly sensitive to the imposed scan order. Existing deterministic scans follow space-filling principles but remain fixed and content-agnostic, and can yield fragmented or semantically inconsistent contexts under degradations. We propose \textbf{CoScan}, a \textbf{co}ntent-adaptive \textbf{scan}-order design for causal vision SSMs. CoScan learns image-dependent space-filling traversals via differentiable proxy supervision driven by \textbf{scan-quality objectives} that \textit{encourage short-range coherence and causal predictability, while preserving spatial adjacency}. To support hierarchical restoration backbones, we introduce an \textbf{efficient multi-scale scan construction by coarsening a fine-level minimum spanning tree}, producing coherent scans across feature resolutions. In addition, a \textbf{frequency-guided start selection strategy} is designed to \textit{place informative structures early in the sequence prefix to strengthen causal context formation}. Plugged into existing Mamba-based backbones, CoScan yields consistent improvements across several restoration benchmarks with minimal overhead and shows promising results on high-level vision tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/dongw22/CoScan

</details>


### [31] [Do 3D Point Representations Encode Architectural Functions? Probing Supervised and Pretrained Models on Urban Facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23720)
*Abdiaziz Muse,Jiju Poovvancheri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出"建筑功能可被3D点表示编码"这一问题,在22栋移动LiDAR建筑、每元素15个功能属性上跨建筑留出评估,发现冻结自监督点云Transformer特征(0.732 mAP)显著优于手工描述子(0.610)与从头训练网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义标签只说明建筑元素"是什么",而非"做什么"——外观相同的两扇窗可能一扇可开启、一扇固定,柱子可能承重也可能仅装饰。作者由此追问:3D点表示是否编码了这类建筑功能(功能属性)?

Method: 数据为22栋移动LiDAR建筑,每个元素标注15个功能属性,采用整栋建筑留出的泛化协议。对比三类特征:手工描述子、从头训练网络(PointNet至108M参数PTv3)、冻结自监督点云Transformer(Utonia)。描述子与冻结特征均接同一线性层读出,并设置同架构随机权重对照以分离预训练贡献。

Result: 在功能歧义元素上,手工描述子达0.610 mAP,任何从头训练网络(含108M的PTv3)均未明显超越;冻结Utonia达0.732。随机权重对照为0.649与0.652,归因于预训练增益+0.080(95%建筑区间[+0.035,+0.124])。预训练特征在任意标注建筑数量下均随数据增长改善,而描述子与随机特征趋于平台;在所测配置中微调会侵蚀这一收益。

Conclusion: 在当前数据规模下,建筑功能更适合从预训练表示中"读出",而非从头"学习";功能信息确实存在于自监督点云表示中,且其优势主要来自预训练而非容量或架构。

Abstract: Semantic labels say what a building element is, not what it does: two identical-looking windows on one facade may be operable or fixed, a column may carry load or only decorate. We ask whether 3D point representations encode such architectural function. On 22 mobile-LiDAR buildings with 15 functional attributes per element, we hold out entire buildings and compare hand-crafted descriptors, networks trained from scratch and frozen self-supervised point transformers. Descriptors and frozen features are read by the same linear layer. No network trained from scratch, from PointNet to a 108M-parameter PTv3, clearly exceeds the descriptors (0.610 mAP on functionally ambiguous elements), whereas frozen Utonia reaches 0.732. Random-weight controls of the same architectures (0.649 and 0.652) attribute the gain to pretraining (Utonia: +0.080, 95% building interval [+0.035, +0.124]). Pretrained features improve at every tested number of labelled buildings while descriptors and random features plateau, and, in the configurations we tested, fine-tuning erodes the benefit: at today's data scale, function is better read from pretrained representations than learned from scratch.

</details>


### [32] [GTA: Generative Tower of Agents for Scalable Holonic Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23727)
*Zeng Tao,Yunuo Chen,Xiang Feng,Yumeng He,Peihao Li,Nanxiang Jiang,Yaowei Guo,Fanlian Zeng,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GTA（Generative Tower of Agents）框架，将自适应场景层级中每个单元表示为"holon"（既是独立构建任务又是可组合场景单元），由并行智能体自上而下递归规划、自下而上生成与组装，实现可扩展的4D世界建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展世界建模需在有限时间与上下文预算下，协调场景级全局目标与细粒度物体、动态角色；现有方法难以兼顾整体布局结构与局部细节。

Method: 1）将场景组织为自适应层级，每层单元为holon，兼具构建任务与可组合单元双重身份；2）并行智能体自上而下递归规划层级并生成资产；3）自下而上组装与精修；4）统一接口向下传递场景级约束，指导局部构建以保证嵌套一致性；5）最终向装配环境填充由场景引导外观与运动的动态人物，形成4D世界。

Result: 通过高吞吐并行执行加速世界建模，同时保持结构化布局与细粒度资产细节，并生成带动态人物的4D场景。

Conclusion: 层级化holon抽象与双向（自上而下规划/自下而上组装）并行智能体流水线，可在有限预算下兼顾全局一致性与局部细节，为可扩展4D世界建模提供可行范式。

Abstract: Scalable world modeling requires coordinating scene-wide goals with fine-grained objects and dynamic characters under limited time and context budgets. We introduce GTA (Generative Tower of Agents), which represents every unit at every level of an adaptive scene hierarchy as a holon, both a self-contained construction task and a composable scene unit. Parallel agents recursively plan the hierarchy top-down, generate assets, and assemble and refine them bottom-up. Uniform interfaces carry scene-wide requirements down the hierarchy, guiding local construction so that each holon fits into the larger one. GTA accelerates scalable world modeling through high-throughput parallel execution while preserving well-structured layouts and fine-grained asset details. GTA then populates the assembled environments with dynamic human characters whose appearance and motion are guided by the scene, yielding realized 4D worlds. Project page: https://zt-zeng-tao.github.io/GTA/

</details>


### [33] [GaussLearner: 3D Gaussian Primitives as Self-Regulating Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23736)
*Gaoxiang Linghu,Haonan Zhou,Xuanhe Liu,Weili Xu,Kaiyue Zhou,Jun Xiao,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussLearner 将每个 3D Gaussian 基元视为可自我调节的持续学习单元，构建"观测—自调节—适应"闭环，在持续学习 3D 高斯场中兼顾场景变化适应与旧内容保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新观测上直接微调已有高斯场会导致对已重建内容的灾难性遗忘；现有的选择性局部优化、回放与参数正则化方法难以在"适应场景变化"与"保留仍然有效的内容"之间取得平衡。

Method: 核心洞察是把 3D Gaussian 基元本身当作自调节的持续学习者。在每个新状态，先将当前观测中的逐像素不一致归因到贡献该像素的高斯；每个高斯基元通过一个学习权重进行自调节，该权重在当前观测与跨状态可靠性之间做平衡，并同时控制高斯参数更新与优化器记忆刷新；随着不一致被消除，适应结果反过来更新该高斯的可靠性，供未来状态使用。最终场是由局部自调节学习器全局耦合而成的群体。

Result: 在 CL-Splats、WAT 及自建的 CL-3D 数据集各场景上，平均 PSNR 相比最强基线提升 3.41 dB；在 CL-3D 上于变化视角与未变化视角均取得最佳重建质量。

Conclusion: 以基元级闭环自调节替代全局选择性优化/回放/正则化，可有效缓解持续学习 3D 高斯场中的灾难性遗忘，并同时提升对变化与未变化区域的重建性能。

Abstract: Continual learning of 3D Gaussian fields aims to maintain faithful reconstructions of the evolving world. However, directly fine-tuning an existing Gaussian field on new observations can cause catastrophic forgetting of previously reconstructed content. To address this challenge, existing methods employ selective local optimization, replay, or parameter regularization, yet still struggle to balance adaptation to scene changes with preservation of still-valid content. Our key insight is to view 3D Gaussian primitives themselves as self-regulating continual learners. Based on this insight, we introduce GaussLearner, which equips each Gaussian with a closed continual-learning loop connecting observation, self-regulation, and adaptation at the primitive level. At each novel state, GaussLearner first attributes pixel-wise inconsistencies in current observations to their contributing Gaussians. Each Gaussian then self-regulates through a learning weight that balances current observations against cross-state reliability. This weight governs both Gaussian parameter adaptation and optimizer memory refresh. As current inconsistencies are resolved, the adaptation outcome renews each Gaussian's reliability for future states. The resulting field is a globally coupled population of locally self-regulating learners. Across scenes from CL-Splats, WAT, and our CL-3D dataset, GaussLearner improves average PSNR by 3.41 dB over the strongest baseline. On CL-3D, it achieves the best reconstruction quality on both changed and unchanged views.

</details>


### [34] [ACUS: An Angular Calibrated Uncertainty Score for Incomplete-View CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23752)
*Zihan Deng,Chuanzhi Xu,Quankeng Huang,Yujie Liu,Zhenzhen Gong,Junjie Huang,Tianye Niu,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出角度校准不确定性分数（ACUS），以Winkler区间分数衡量稀疏视角CT中预测可靠性随与已采视角角度距离变化的关系，揭示点估计PSNR无法反映的间隙相关过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角与有限弧CT常被视为投影补全问题，以与单一真值图像的相似度排序；当查询角度远离所有已采测量时，学习先验可能"编造"扫描从未约束的解剖结构，导致高分假象。现有评估协议无法回答"预测可靠性如何随角度间隙变化"这一基本问题。

Method: 将ACUS定义为：在留出投影上构造名义90%预测区间，计算Winkler区间分数，并按到最近已采视角的距离分箱统计。该分数同时计入区间宽度，并对留出像素落到区间外施加大幅惩罚，因而窄区间只有在覆盖率成立时才有益；同时以在已采角度上的"塌缩"作为预测器的必要一致性检查。

Result: 在胸部与牙科锥束轨迹上，区间宽度随角度间隙增大而增长；经验覆盖率处于77%–92%区间；点估计的PSNR随宽度上升而下降。最近邻、插值及点补全方法在仅给定单一残差尺度时，远端覆盖率报告为零，并产生区间外距离惩罚。

Conclusion: ACUS能揭示依赖角度间隙的过度自信，而这正是单靠点估计PSNR无法度量的；它可作为稀疏/有限弧CT预测可靠性评估的校准化指标。

Abstract: Sparse-view and limited-arc computed tomography (CT) are commonly treated as projection completion: a network fills an unmeasured view, and the result is ranked by similarity to one ground-truth image. That score can be high even when the query angle is far from every acquired measurement, because a learned prior may invent anatomy the scan never constrained. Existing protocols therefore leave a basic question unanswered: how does predictive reliability change with distance from the acquired views? We propose the Angular Calibrated Uncertainty Score (ACUS) to answer that question. ACUS is the Winkler interval score of a nominal 90% predictive interval on held-out projections, binned by distance to the nearest acquired view. It charges interval width and adds a large miss penalty when a held-out pixel falls outside the interval, so a narrow band helps only when coverage holds. We use collapse on acquired angles as a necessary consistency check on the predictor. On chest and dental cone-beam orbits, interval width grows with the angular gap. Empirical coverage ranges from 77% to 92%, and the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of the point estimate falls as the width rises. Nearest-neighbor, interpolation, and point completion, each given one residual scale, have zero reported far coverage and incur outside-distance penalties. ACUS exposes gap-dependent overconfidence that a point-estimate PSNR alone does not measure.

</details>


### [35] [Detect What You Need: Chain-of-Causal Reasoning for 3D Intent Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23754)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.CV
