TL;DR: 提出链式因果推理框架CoCR，将抽象意图分解为有序功能需求，构建意图-功能-对象显式推理链与功能依赖图，实现3D意图定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D意图定位(3D-IG)中的意图抽象且非描述性，需从语言中推断潜在功能需求并对齐物体级3D表示；现有方法多依赖隐式意图-对象匹配，存在逻辑断层，可解释性、鲁棒性与泛化性不足。

Method: 1) 渐进式将复杂意图分解为有序功能需求，形成显式"意图-功能-对象"推理链；2) 构建因果启发的功能依赖图建模需求-属性关系；3) 引入因果-视觉对齐模块，将功能感知表示与点云几何-语义特征对齐，实现结构化功能推理与视觉证据的双向验证。

Result: 在3D意图定位与3D视觉定位基准上的大量实验表明，该方法提升了意图感知的物体定位性能；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 显式功能依赖推理可有效弥合抽象意图与3D物体之间的语义鸿沟，提升3D意图定位的可解释性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurately matching human intentions in 3D space is an important goal of artificial intelligence. Recently, 3D Intention Grounding (3D-IG) has emerged, aiming to localize target 3D objects given a natural-language intent. Unlike conventional visual grounding with descriptive referring expressions, 3D-IG intents are abstract and non-descriptive, making object localization substantially more challenging. This requires inferring latent functional requirements from non-descriptive intents and aligning them with object-level 3D representations. However, existing methods largely rely on implicit intent-object matching, leading to logical gaps and limited interpretability, robustness, and generalization. To address these challenges, we propose Chain-of-Causal Reasoning (CoCR), a causality-inspired functional dependency reasoning framework that explicitly bridges abstract intentions and candidate objects through intermediate functional requirements. Specifically, CoCR progressively decomposes complex intents into ordered functional requirements, thereby forming an explicit intent-function-object reasoning chain that links abstract intentions to object suitability. Building on this chain, we construct a causality-inspired functional dependency graph to model requirement-attribute relationships and introduce a causal-visual alignment module that aligns function-aware representations with the geometric-semantic features of 3D point clouds, enabling bidirectional verification between structured functional reasoning and visual evidence. Extensive experiments on 3D Intention Grounding and 3D Visual Grounding demonstrate that our method enhances intent-aware object localization. Our code is available at https://2490o.github.io/CoCR.

</details>


### [36] [GramStatTexNet: Efficient, Interpretable, and Neuro-Inspired Texture Analysis-by-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23755)
*Vasha DuTell,Zuyu Yun,Anne Harrington,Michael Y-S Fang,Mark Hamilton,Christian Kövesdi,Edward H. Adelson,William T. Freeman,Ruth Rosenholtz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GramStatTexNet：将经典多尺度 Gabor 金字塔与 Gramian 相关性统计结合的混合「分析-合成」纹理建模框架，兼具可解释性、紧凑性与竞争性合成质量，并可自然扩展到时间、空间与外周视野。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络纹理合成真实感强，但表征不透明、过参数化，与生物视觉关联弱；神经科学启发的金字塔模型虽可解释、紧凑、生物合理，却依赖少量手工挑选的统计量，难以泛化或扩展。

Method: 多尺度 Gabor 滤波器结构（沿用经典模型）+ 基于 Gramian 的相关性统计，并按结构化滤波器族组织；每条统计量具有可命名的滤波器族身份，据此将合成保真度分解为各族贡献，并学习保留感知纹理内容的低维可解释嵌入；通过空间池化扩展到外周纹理，通过时空滤波器组扩展到动态纹理。

Result: 在多类纹理与多个感知指标上，与经典及深度合成基线比较，显示出紧凑性与可解释性优势，同时合成质量具竞争力；可实现按滤波器族分解保真度并得到低维感知嵌入。

Conclusion: GramStatTexNet 提供了统一、可解释的视觉信息分析与建模框架，可自然延伸至空间、时间与偏心度维度。

Abstract: Deep networks achieve high realism in texture synthesis but often rely on opaque, over-parameterized representations that have limited direct connection to biological vision. Neuroscience-informed pyramid models are interpretable, compact, and biologically inspired, but rely on small, hand-curated statistics sets that are difficult to generalize or extend. We introduce GramStatTexNet, a hybrid analysis-by-synthesis framework that combines the multi-scale Gabor filter structure of classical models with the flexibility of Gramian-based correlations, organized into structured filter families. This structured representation enables analyses that are challenging for deep-feature methods: each statistic carries a named filter-family identity, allowing us to decompose synthesis fidelity into per-family contributions and to learn an interpretable, low-dimensional embedding that preserves perceptual texture content. We compare our framework to classical and deep texture-synthesis baselines across diverse texture categories, and on multiple perceptual metrics, highlighting the compactness and interpretability advantages of this hybrid representation, alongside competitive synthesis quality. This same framework extends naturally to peripheral textures via spatial pooling, and to dynamic textures using a spatiotemporal filter bank. GramStatTexNet provides a unified, interpretable framework for analyzing and modeling visual information with natural extensions across space, time, and eccentricity. Project page: https://vdutell.github.io/gstn

</details>


### [37] [Mean Curvature Minimizing Foliations Of A Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23765)
*Tabish A. Syed,Marc Theveneau,Benedikt Wirth,Rustum Choksi,Kaleem Siddiqi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从单一方向向量场恢复局部光滑"叶状结构"(foliation)的数学模型与算法，通过引入与之正交的第二向量场并要求二者Lie括号为零，结合平均曲率正则化平衡似然与低表面积，推导能量一阶变分并给出可并行数值实现，在白质纤维束与心肌组织数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然中广泛存在流线型结构（如脑白质纤维束、心肌细胞排列），普遍认为其组织成局部光滑曲面以优化功能；但如何从仅与局部片层方向对齐的单一向量场中恢复这种叶状结构仍是未解问题。

Method: 构建"片层"的数学模型：给定输入向量场，估计与其正交的第二向量场，并约束二者Lie括号为零（保证可积性/叶状结构存在）；同时最小化恢复曲面的平均曲率以平衡片层似然与低表面积。推导相应能量泛函的一阶变分，设计支持高效并行计算的数值方法。

Result: 在合成数据与生物医学示例上验证：脑图谱白质纤维束、透明化心脏壁组织的共聚焦显微图像；后者以微米级前所未有尺度重建了与局部心肌细胞方向对齐的片层。

Conclusion: 该方法为从单方向场推断局部光滑叶状结构提供了有理论依据且可并行求解的框架，适用于医学成像中纤维与细胞尺度结构的曲面重建。

Abstract: Streamline patterns are ubiquitous in nature. Prominent examples include white matter fibre tracts in the mammalian brain and arrangements of elongated cells, such as cardiomyocytes in the heart wall. It has been posited that such organisations foliate into locally smooth surfaces to optimise function. However, the recovery of these foliations from a single vector field aligned with the local orientation of the underlying sheets remains an open problem. We address this challenge by introducing a mathematical model of a sheet and developing an algorithm for sheet inference, from measurements of the oriented structures. Given an input vector field, our model estimates a second vector field that is orthonormal to it, while also requiring the Lie bracket of the two to vanish. To balance sheet likelihood against low surface area, we also reduce the mean curvature of the recovered local surface. We compute the first variation of an associated energy and provide a numerical method which admits an efficient, parallel implementation for sheet estimation. We demonstrate our approach on both synthetic and biological examples, motivated by applications to medical imaging. These include white matter fibre tracts from a brain atlas and confocal microscopy images of cleared heart wall tissue. For the latter we are able to reconstruct sheets aligned with local cardiomyocyte directions at unprecedented (micrometre) scale.

</details>


### [38] [Keep the Reasoning, Read the Image: On-Demand Visual KV for Multi-Turn Reasoning VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23769)
*Ziyi Wang,Xiyue Zhang,Bing Hu,Guangmin Song,Chenyi Zhang,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 推理型VLM的KV cache保留策略在共享预算下会让增长的推理内容挤占视觉条目，本文提出免训练方法ShelfKV保护图像，仅在需要时将高相关视觉条目换入注意力槽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理VLM在回答前会进行长推理，KV cache保留策略通过剪枝控制显存，但图像与推理共享同一预算，导致图像在多轮对话中被挤出：同一图表问题首轮仅损失8分，在推理轮之后提问则损失24分。实验表明从驱逐中保护一小块视觉保留区可挽回大部分损失，而把同样槽位加入共享预算则效果明显较差，说明图像缺的是保护而非容量。

Method: ShelfKV是一种免训练方法，可叠加在现有保留策略之上：将所有视觉条目常驻CPU内存；在每个解码步，把当前查询打分最高的视觉条目换入被其打分最低的文本条目所占的注意力槽位。图像永不被驱逐，但仅在被读取时占用注意力槽。

Result: 在四个基准（其中三个为对话式）、两种保留策略、两种推理VLM上评估：对话场景中，在相同GPU显存下较最强现有策略DBTrimKV最高提升20.3分；即使DBTrimKV使用两倍显存，仍领先8.7分；并稳定优于同等显存的固定视觉保留区，同时不损害独立问题的准确率。

Conclusion: 图像应当受到保护而不被推理内容驱逐，但应仅按需读取——这是ShelfKV的核心设计原则，并被实验结果支持。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) think for thousands of tokens before they answer, and retention policies keep the KV cache affordable by pruning it as the reasoning grows. We find that this pruning sacrifices the image in multi-turn conversations: the image and the reasoning share one budget, so the growing reasoning crowds the visual entries out. A chart question that loses 8 points to the full cache when asked first loses 24 points when asked after a round of reasoning. Protecting a small visual reserve from eviction recovers most of this loss, while adding the same slots to the shared budget recovers considerably less. The image therefore lacks protection, not capacity. Based on this finding, we propose ShelfKV, a training-free method that works on top of existing retention policies. It keeps every visual entry in CPU memory and, at each decoding step, swaps the visual entries that the current query scores highest into the attention slots of the text entries it scores lowest. The image is never evicted, yet it occupies attention slots only while it is read. We evaluate ShelfKV on four benchmarks, three of them conversational, with two retention policies and two reasoning VLMs. In conversations, it improves the strongest existing policy, DBTrimKV, by up to 20.3 points at matched GPU memory, and still leads by 8.7 points when DBTrimKV uses twice the memory. It also consistently outperforms a fixed visual reserve of matched GPU memory in these settings, without hurting accuracy on standalone questions. These results show that the image should be protected from the reasoning but read only on demand.

</details>


### [39] [DetectViT: Test-time Backdoor Detection for Vision Transformers via Inter-Head Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23770)
*Siquan Huang,Yijiang Li,Xingfu Yan,Ningzhi Gao,Leyu Shi,Ying Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DetectViT，一种无需训练数据、模型输出与触发器先验的 ViT 测试时后门检测方法，仅利用推理中已计算的注意力权重，通过"头间一致性分数"与"头间熵方差分数"量化注意力劫持现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 已成为多数多模态模型视觉编码器的核心，但依赖第三方预训练权重使其面临后门攻击风险。现有测试时检测方法依赖预测标签或中间嵌入，任务适用性受限且计算开销大。

Method: 分析后门触发器对多头注意力的影响，识别两种异常模式：完全注意力劫持（头间空间重叠异常高）与部分注意力劫持（注意力分布集中度方差异常大）。据此设计两个指标——头间一致性分数与头间熵方差分数，并用来自任意来源的小规模 OOD 校准集估计阈值。

Result: 在四种代表性攻击、多种架构（DeiT、CLIP、LLaVA）及下游任务（图像分类、图像描述）上显著优于全部基线；仅需 64–128 个 OOD 校准样本，在 BadCLIP 上达到 100% 真阳性率与 0.17% 假阳性率；仅依赖推理已有注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 注意力层面的头间统计特征是 ViT 后门检测的高效信号，DetectViT 兼具高检出率、低误报、跨任务与跨架构泛化和极低开销的优势。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) now underpin the visual encoders of most multimodal models, but this success comes at a cost: relying on third-party pretrained checkpoints leaves these systems open to backdoor attacks. Existing test-time detection methods rely on either the predicted label or intermediate embeddings, limiting their applicability across diverse tasks and incurring substantial computational overhead. In this work, we study how backdoor triggers affect the multi-head attention mechanism of ViTs and identify two distinct anomalous patterns: $\textit{complete attention hijacking}$, causing the attention to exhibit abnormally high inter-head spatial overlap, and $\textit{partial attention hijacking}$, causing an abnormally large variance in the concentration of the attention distribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{DetectViT}$, a test-time backdoor detection method that requires neither training data, model outputs, nor any prior knowledge of the trigger. DetectViT quantifies the two hijacking phenomena via an inter-head consistency score and an inter-head entropy variance score, with thresholds estimated from a small out-of-distribution (OOD) calibration set drawn from an arbitrary source. Across four representative attacks, diverse architectures (DeiT, CLIP, and LLaVA), and downstream tasks (image classification and captioning), DetectViT substantially outperforms all baselines with as few as 64--128 OOD calibration samples, achieving a true positive rate of $100\%$ and a false positive rate of $0.17\%$ on BadCLIP. Notably, it introduces negligible additional overhead by relying solely on attention weights already computed during inference. Code is available at: https://github.com/siquanhuang/DetectViT.

</details>


### [40] [When accuracy hides risk: risk-controlled confirmation of the critical view of safety under expert disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23781)
*Bowen Xiang,Xiao Yu,Mingze Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示自动化CVS判定中"固定平均误确认率"阈值规则的结构性缺陷——误差集中于争议视频，且识别器越准误差越集中；提出基于多重检验的"认证式确认"(certified confirmation)风险控制监控规则，在总体、争议子集、厂商/国家分组及CVS可见前均以概率1-δ将误确认率控制在α以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署型CVS自动判定需在打第一枚夹子前决定是否确认CVS。标准做法是校准阈值使平均误确认率低，但"平均准确"可能掩盖风险在特定亚群（争议视频）上的集中；作者怀疑这种失败是结构性的，需要显式的风险控制而非平均校准。

Method: 理论分析：当参考标签为从更大专家组抽样的小组投票时，证明在固定平均误确认率下最大化敏感性的规则同时最大化对争议视频的误确认，且对噪声更低（更准）的识别器该率随准确率上升。据此提出certified confirmation：以多重检验选阈值，使总误确认≤α、争议视频≤α、厂商内≤α、国家内≤α、CVS不���见前≤α均以≥1-δ概率成立；争议标签仅用于校准。在SAGES CVS Challenge 300个测试视频（18国）上做预注册、哈希锁定评估。

Result: 标准校准在39个争议视频中误确认8个（0.21；单侧p=0.037，对α=0.10），certified confirmation仅误确认2个（单侧95%上界0.15）；开发数据上对未参与校准的厂商与国家分组保护仍成立。代价是及时确认充分视频的比例由35%降至10%。

Conclusion: 平均准确率掩盖了争议病例上的集中风险，且该失败是结构性的、会随模型变强而恶化；必须在争议亚群上显式施加风险控制，"认证式确认"提供了可证的、分组的误确认上界。

Abstract: Automated assessment of the critical view of safety (CVS) in laparoscopic cholecystectomy has advanced rapidly, but a deployed check must decide, before the first clip, whether to confirm CVS. The standard rule calibrates a threshold so that false confirmations are rare on average. We show that this rule concentrates its errors on disputed videos, which one of three expert raters judged sufficient, and that the failure is structural. When reference labels are votes of a panel sampled from a larger expert pool, the rule that maximizes sensitivity at a fixed average false-confirmation rate also maximizes false confirmation of disputed videos, and for recognizers that recover expert judgement with less noise this rate rises with accuracy. We propose certified confirmation, a risk-controlled monitoring rule whose threshold is selected by multiple testing so that, with probability at least 1 - δ, false confirmation is at most α overall, among disputed videos, within vendor and country groups, and before CVS is visible, using disputed labels only to calibrate. In a pre-specified, hash-locked evaluation on the 300 test videos (18 countries) of the SAGES CVS Challenge, standard calibration falsely confirmed 8 of 39 disputed videos (0.21; one-sided p = 0.037 against α = 0.10), whereas certified confirmation confirmed 2 (one-sided 95% upper bound 0.15). On development data, protection held for vendor and country groups left out of calibration. The cost was confirming 10% rather than 35% of sufficient videos on time. Average accuracy hides this risk; disputed cases must be protected explicitly.

</details>


### [41] [ROBIN++: Unified Copyright Protection and Tamper Localization for Diffusion Models via Dual-Domain Synergistic Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23793)
*Huayang Huang,Siqi Zeng,Qian Wang,Bo Du,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROBIN++ 提出双域协同范式：注入时将鲁棒性（频域版权水印）与脆弱性（空域定位水印）解耦到不同域，验证时再耦合互增强，缓解版权鲁棒性与篡改定位敏感性之间的固有权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成内容水印方法在单一域内混合相互冲突的信号，导致版权鲁棒性与篡改定位敏感性之间存在固有权衡，难以同时兼顾。

Method: 1) 对抗优化算法在生成过程中注入频域版权水印，最大化提取鲁棒性且无视觉伪影；2) 内容感知扰动生成器在潜空间注入空域定位水印，对空间篡改敏感且对版权水印干扰极小；3) 验证阶段双分支协同检测器：频率到空间协同（鲁棒频谱先验作为全局锚点引导局部异常检测）与空间到频率精炼（用预测篡改掩码修正受损潜特征）。

Result: 大量实验表明 ROBIN++ 在版权恢复与篡改定位精度上均显著优于现有方法，有效缓解了两任务间长期存在的权衡问题。

Conclusion: 通过注入解耦、验证耦合的双域协同设计，可同时实现高鲁棒版权溯源与高灵敏篡改定位，突破单域纠缠带来的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Digital watermarking provides a promising solution for verifying the provenance and integrity of generated content. However, existing methods often suffer from an inherent trade-off between copyright robustness and localization sensitivity due to the entanglement of conflicting signals within a single domain. In this paper, we propose ROBIN++, a paradigm that shifts from single-domain entanglement to a Dual-Domain Synergistic design. Our key insight is to explicitly decouple robustness and fragility during injection by assigning them to distinct domains, while re-coupling them for mutual reinforcement during verification. Specifically, we introduce an Adversarial Optimization Algorithm to inject a frequency-domain copyright watermark during generation, maximizing extraction robustness without visual artifacts. Complementarily, a Content-Aware Perturbation generator is integrated into the latent space to inject the spatial-domain localization watermark, ensuring sensitivity to spatial tampering with minimal interference to the copyright watermark. During verification, we propose a dual-branch synergistic detector that facilitates Frequency-to-Spatial Synergy, where robust spectral priors act as global anchors for local anomaly detection. Conversely, a Spatial-to-Frequency Refinement strategy is employed to rectify corrupted latent features using predicted tamper masks. Extensive experiments show that ROBIN++ significantly outperforms existing approaches in both copyright recovery and tamper localization accuracy, effectively mitigating the long-standing trade-off between the two tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/Hannah1102/ROBIN.

</details>


### [42] [Sightline: Long-Window Speculative Decoding for Fast SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23807)
*Yanchao Li,Ben Gao,Jiaqing Xie,Yusu Zhang,Beilun Wang,Yanbo Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Nanqing Dong,Tianfan Fu,Zhongyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sightline：面向矢量图形生成的投机解码方法，采用与草稿器视界等长的验证窗口和足够宽的草稿树，在文本到SVG和图像到SVG任务上均为最快的无损解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG生成模型逐token自回归写代码，绘制速度慢。投机解码可无损加速，且块式草稿器每轮可猜多个token；但在SVG上，许多轮次会整块接受草稿、仅在验证窗口处终止，说明其失配稀少且成簇，且在一个块内深处的准确率远高于自然语言场景，因此存在"更长的验证窗口"未被利用的加速空间。

Method: 提出游程长度恒等式与三条命题，论证当候选足够宽时，延长验证窗口至草稿器视界上限即可获得收益；据此设计Sightline，将验证窗口设为与草稿器视界等长，并配以足够宽的草稿树来填满该窗口。

Result: 在MMSVG、MMSVG-2M、SArena的六个基准、三个模型上覆盖文本到SVG与图像到SVG任务，Sightline在所有设置下均为最快的无损解码器；在SArena上即使各解码器共享同一循环控制仍最快；在另外四个家族的六个模型（输出SVG代码、压缩SVG token或Lottie JSON）的几乎全部设置中也领先。分析表明优势来自短窗口被截断的轮次，且在无循环与含循环内容中均成立；投机解码亦能加速按渲染选样的过程，且优于按似然选样。

Conclusion: 矢量图形上的投机解码应"验证到草稿器能看到的深度"，即验证窗口与草稿器视界对齐；这一简单原则带来稳定且普适的无损加速，并可与渲染式选样结合。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) generation models write a graphic as code with one forward pass per token, so drawing is slow. Speculative decoding speeds them up losslessly, and block drafters guess many tokens per pass. On SVG, many of their rounds accept the whole draft and end only at the verification window. We find that their misses are rare and clumped, and that they stay accurate far deeper into a block than on natural language. A run-length identity and three propositions show that a longer window pays once the candidates are wide, up to the drafter's horizon. We propose Sightline, which pairs a window as long as the drafter's horizon with a draft tree wide enough to fill it. Experiments cover text-to-SVG and image-to-SVG with three models on six benchmarks from MMSVG, MMSVG-2M and SArena. In every setting, Sightline is the fastest of the compared lossless decoders. On SArena, it stays the fastest when every decoder shares one loop control. It also leads in almost every setting of six models from four other families, which write SVG code, compressed SVG tokens or Lottie JSON. Analyses trace the lead to rounds that shorter windows cut off, both in loop-free content and in loops. Speculative decoding also saves time for choosing samples by rendering, which beats choosing by likelihood. These results suggest that speculative decoding on vector graphics should verify as far as the drafter sees. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/Sightline.

</details>


### [43] [PReMind: When Personal Memory Meets Visual Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23808)
*Yeongtak Oh,Donghyeok Lee,Joonhee Oh,Jaewon Jeong,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PReMind-Bench基准与两阶段强化学习框架PReMind，联合训练个性化视觉助手的记忆调用与危险响应能力，在4B模型上将依赖性子集联合成功率从12.9%提升至28.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视觉语言助手需结合视觉观察与个人记忆给出建议；当场景含物理危险时，过敏、疾病、行动受限等个人语境会改变合适的回应。因此需同时识别可见危险并调用相关个人信息，而现有模型常只满足其中一项。

Method: 构建诊断性基准PReMind-Bench，覆盖多样危险与个人记忆条件，分别度量记忆访问、grounding、弃答（abstention）与危险响应，并在相互依赖案例上评估联合成功率；提出两阶段强化学习框架PReMind，结合按需调用与强制调用（总是检索记忆）两类交互，联合训练grounded记忆使用与危险响应。

Result: 在4B规模下，依赖性子集联合成功率由12.9%升至28.6%；主测试集上危险响应充分性与正确弃答均有提升；联合成功增益在强制调用评估、第二个LLM裁判及第二阶段三个随机种子下保持，且种子平均在未见危险组上亦提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明需在评估与训练中显式连接视觉证据与相关者语境的必要性；仅针对安全或个性化训练会留下联合能力缺口。

Abstract: Personalized vision-language assistants use visual observations and personal memories to provide advice tailored to the user. When the observed scene contains a physical hazard, personal context, such as allergies, medical conditions, or mobility constraints, can change what help is appropriate. Responding appropriately therefore requires recognizing the visible hazard and applying the relevant person's information. We introduce PReMind-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that evaluates this combination across diverse hazards and personal-memory conditions. It separately measures memory access, grounding, abstention, and hazard response, and assesses joint success on interdependency cases. Existing models often satisfy one requirement while failing the other, and training only for safety or personalization leaves a gap in joint success. We therefore propose PReMind, a two-phase reinforcement learning framework that combines on-demand and forced-call interactions (memory always retrieved) to jointly train grounded memory use and hazard response. At 4B, PReMind raises joint success from 12.9% to 28.6% over its base on the interdependency set and improves hazard adequacy and correct abstention on the main test. Joint-success gains persist under forced-call evaluation and under a second LLM judge across three Phase II seeds; averaged over these seeds, joint success also improves on unseen hazard groups. These findings highlight the need to evaluate and train personal assistants to connect visual evidence with the relevant person's context.

</details>


### [44] [SWIFT: Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely Action for Asynchronous Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23813)
*Jiazhe Wang,Tianyi Yang,Ruihang Li,Fan Lu,Xianming Zeng,Jianyun Xu,Xingliang Liu,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIFT 是一种面向部署的异步 world-action 系统，将世界模型的更新移出"观测—动作"关键路径：后台 GPU 用慢速 Video DiT 流式生成只读预测世界上下文，前台 GPU 用轻量新鲜度载体编码当前环视图像，使快速 Action DiT 无需等待即可结合实时视觉反馈与最新世界上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景在模型计算过程中持续变化，使得"观测新鲜度"与"动作交付时延"成为 world-action 规划的核心问题；而保留丰富生成式世界先验通常与实时控制相冲突，需要在不牺牲世界模型能力的前提下避免阻塞实时决策。

Method: 采用前后台异构异步架构：后台 GPU 上慢速 Video DiT 将环视历史流式处理为只读预测世界上下文，并用覆盖"更新间隔 + 后台世界计算"的预测跨度进行训练；前台 GPU 上轻量新鲜度载体每步将当前环视图像编码为门控 key/value bank，快速 Action DiT 融合实时视觉反馈与最近完成的世界上下文，无需等待后台更新。

Result: 开环 NAVSIM 上配合轨迹评分器达到 94.2 PDMS，单次决策 198.4 ms；闭环 Bench2Drive 上 Driving Score 80.30，前台时延 109.9 ms；迁移至 Waymo E2E 隐藏测试获 8.0050 Rater Feedback Score，nuScenes 上平均终点误差 0.54 m、平均碰撞率 0.03%。四个基准上均优于对比的 world-action 方法。

Conclusion: 通过在时间维度上解耦"世界更新"与"动作生成"，SWIFT 在不牺牲世界先验丰富度的条件下实现了实时控制，建立了较优的精度—时延权衡，并具备跨数据集泛化能力，体现了面向部署的可行性。

Abstract: Driving scenes keep changing during model computation, making both observation freshness and action-delivery time central to world-action planning. To retain rich generative world priors in autonomous driving without stalling real-time control, we present SWIFT (Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely action), a deployment-oriented asynchronous world-action system that moves world updates off the observation-to-action critical path. On a background GPU, a slow Video DiT streams surround-view history into read-only predictive world context, trained with a predictive span that covers both the update interval and background world computation. On a foreground GPU, a lightweight freshness carrier encodes current surround-view images into a gated key/value bank at every step, letting a fast Action DiT combine live visual feedback with the latest completed world context without waiting. On open-loop NAVSIM, SWIFT reaches 94.2 PDMS at 198.4 ms per decision with a trajectory scorer; on closed-loop Bench2Drive, it achieves an 80.30 Driving Score at 109.9 ms foreground latency. SWIFT further transfers to the Waymo E2E hidden test (8.0050 Rater Feedback Score) and nuScenes (0.54 m mean endpoint error, 0.03% mean collision rate). Across all four benchmarks, SWIFT achieves the best results among compared world-action methods, establishing a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [45] [Mapping the Design Space of Feature Sampling for Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23822)
*Liping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE用逐像素各向异性高斯期望替代固定半径/单点相关采样，以正定精度矩阵学习采样支撑的尺度与方向，仅增1.5% MACs即在Sintel上EPE降24.2%、KITTI F1-all降11.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法(如RAFT)在cost volume上做固定半径查找，或WAFT做单点索引，对所有位置采用相同的相关特征采样方案。但不同位置所在区域的特性不同，统一采样要么冗余要么不足，忽略了区域特性，缺乏按位置自适应的采样设计。

Method: 提出SAGE(各向异性高斯期望采样)，将单点采样替换为以当前光流估计为中心的固定候选网格上的逐像素各向异性高斯期望；用正定精度矩阵控制采样支撑的尺度、各向异性和方向，单点采样为其极限情形，使采样支撑成为显式可分析的设计变量。

Result: 仅增加1.5% MACs，有效提升零样本跨数据集泛化。仅在FlyingChairs和FlyingThings训练时，Sintel clean/final的EPE分别降低24.2%和16.4%，KITTI F1-epe和F1-all分别降低7.2%和11.6%。

Conclusion: 采样支撑(尺度、方向)应针对每个查询位置单独学习；SAGE以极小的计算开销将固定采样推广为可学习、可分析的各向异性高斯采样，显著改善对应学习与跨域泛化。

Abstract: The computation of correlation features is crucial for optical flow estimation. Existing methods (e.g., RAFT) perform fixed-radius lookups on a cost volume, yielding a set of correlation features for each position; alternatively, WAFT performs single-point feature indexing, yielding one correlation feature vector per position. None of them designs a suitable correlation feature sampling scheme for each position according to regional characteristics. Our insight is that not every position requires the same sampling scheme. Applying an identical sampling scheme to every position is either redundant or insufficient and ignores the specific characteristics of the corresponding region. This paper rethinks the design of feature sampling and investigates the following question: should the support of feature sampling---i.e., the extent and directions over which evidence is aggregated---be learned separately for each query position? To study this question, we propose SAGE (Sampling with Anisotropic Gaussian Expectation). This method replaces single-point sampling with a per-pixel anisotropic Gaussian expectation on a fixed candidate grid centered on the current optical flow estimate. A positive-definite precision matrix controls the scale, anisotropy, and orientation of the sampling support, with single-point sampling included as its limiting case. Consequently, the sampling support becomes an explicit and analyzable design variable and provides a principled way to analyze how directional feature aggregation affects correspondence learning. Extensive experiments show that SAGE, while adding only 1.5% multiply-accumulate operations (MACs), effectively improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization. When trained only on FlyingChairs and FlyingThings, SAGE reduces the end-point error (EPE) by 24.2% and 16.4% on Sintel clean and final, and reduces KITTI F1-epe and F1-all by 7.2% and 11.6%.

</details>


### [46] [TOGBench: Benchmarking and Improving Tiny-Object Generation Fidelity in Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23840)
*Chanhwi Jeong,Dongmin You,Seungwook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TOGBench，一个面向航空影像小目标生成保真度的定量、对象级基准，并给出两种简单训练/推理技巧提升布局到图像生成器渲染小目标的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空影像因俯视、广域特性以小目标（车辆、船舶、飞机等，仅占数像素）为主；现有生成模型在小目标上表现显著劣于大目标，而此前生成评估工作侧重提示词遵循或人类偏好，忽视了小目标是否被忠实渲染。这一局限长期被忽视。

Method: 构建TOGBench——面向航空影像小目标生成保真度的定量、对象级基准；并针对布局到图像（layout-to-image）生成器提出两种简单的训练期/推理期技巧，以提升其渲染小目标的能力。

Result: 两种技巧提升了布局到图像生成器渲染小目标的性能，并在TOGBench上取得改进。

Conclusion: 弥补了生成模型评估中小目标保真度缺位的问题，为航空影像小目标生成提供了可量化评估手段，并展示了通过轻量训练/推理改动即可改善该能力。

Abstract: Advances in deep generative models now enable the synthesis of realistic imagery. However, aerial imagery is dominated by tiny objects, such as vehicles, ships, and aircraft, which occupy only a few pixels owing to the overhead, wide-area nature of aerial imaging. Existing generative models struggle substantially more with tiny objects than with larger ones. This limitation has been overlooked, as prior work on generative evaluation emphasizes prompt following or human preference rather than whether small objects are rendered faithfully. To address this gap, we introduce TOGBench, a quantitative, object-level benchmark for evaluating tiny-object generation fidelity in aerial imagery. We further propose two simple train-/inference-time techniques that improve the ability of layout-to-image generators to render small objects, raising their performance on TOGBench.

</details>


### [47] [ReflectScene:MLLM-Guided Closed-Loop Diffusion for 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23845)
*Jiawei Shen,Jie Feng,Junpeng Zhang,Guanbin Li,Wanyi Chen,Weisheng Dong,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReflectScene——一个由 MLLM 引导的闭环扩散框架，用于文本条件 3D 室内场景生成，通过层次先验场景理解、语义奖励评估与决策条件轨迹一致扩散，在去噪过程中实现对象级纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散方法主要依赖静态文本条件，难以评估演化中的场景并将语义差异转化为可执行的对象级修正；MLLM 的事后精修虽提供语义反馈，但与去噪过程解耦，可能破坏场景连贯性。

Method: 1）层次化先验锚定场景理解：将结构化布局与多视图视觉证据对齐，借助冻结的多模态大语言模型解读对象特定上下文与场景全局语义；2）MLLM 引导的语义奖励评估：将 MLLM 对候选修正的诊断与评估蒸馏为轻量级 Reward Predictor，降低决策计算开销；3）决策条件轨迹一致扩散：通过瞬态条件与持续干净估计投影整合选定修正，在去噪全程纠正目标对象布局并保持非目标布局不变。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上实验表明，功能一致性与语义对齐得到提升，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 将 MLLM 语义反馈闭环嵌入扩散去噪过程，可在保证轨迹一致性的前提下实现对象级布局纠错，弥补静态文本条件与事后精修的不足。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation requires layouts satisfying geometric, functional, and semantic constraints. However, existing diffusion methods rely primarily on static textual conditions, limiting their ability to assess evolving scenes and translate semantic discrepancies into executable object-level corrections. MLLM-based post-hoc refinement provides semantic feedback but remains decoupled from denoising, potentially disrupting scene coherence.We propose ReflectScene, an MLLM-guided closed-loop diffusion framework. First, we introduce Hierarchical Prior-Grounded Scene Understanding that aligns structured layouts with multi-view visual evidence, to interpret object-specific context and scene global semantics via a frozen multi-modal large language model (MLLM). Second, we propose MLLM-Guided Semantic Reward Evaluation to distill MLLM's diagnoses and assessments on possible candidate corrections into a lightweight Reward Predictor, enabling correction decision making with reduced computation burden. Finally, Decision-Conditioned Trajectory-Consistent Diffusion is proposed to integrate selected corrections through transient conditioning and persistent clean-estimate projection, correcting object layout in the generated scene while preserving non-target arrangements throughout denoising.Experiments on 3D-FRONT demonstrate improved functional consistency and semantic alignment while maintaining competitive generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [48] [Temporal Image Representations for Mouse Dynamics Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23859)
*Junyi Li,Charles Devlen,Daqing Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对鼠标动态认证中仅用空间轨迹图像、丢失时间信息的问题，提出在图像表示中融入速度/时间信息，系统比较XYPlot与Pairwise Distance两种表示及位置感知/无位置设定，在多个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往鼠标动态认证依赖手工特征或时序表示以同时刻画空间与时间特性；近期将鼠标数据转为图像以复用预训练视觉模型，但多采用仅空间的轨迹图表示，丢弃了有助于区分用户的时间特征。

Method: 提出包含速度与时间信息的图像表示：XYPlot（直观显示移动路径）与Pairwise Distance (PWD)（利用整幅画布编码鼠标事件间关系）；在位置感知与无位置两种设定下，分别用预训练2D-CNN与ViT架构评估。

Result: 引入时间信息优于纯空间表示；位置感知变体总体优于无位置变体；在Balabit与TWOS上达到SOTA并扩展至另两个数据集。总体上，位置感知PWD+时间编码+2D-CNN效果最佳：Balabit Test-1/Test-2平均EER为5.21%/12.70%，TWOS 4.94%，ChaoShen 6.16%，DFL 0.59%。

Conclusion: 将时间（速度）信息嵌入鼠标轨迹的图像表示可有效提升认证性能，其中位置感知的PWD表示配合2D-CNN为最优组合，并在多个数据集上验证了泛化性。

Abstract: Mouse dynamics authentication has traditionally relied on handcrafted features or time-series representations that capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of mouse movements. More recent approaches have modeled mouse data as images, enabling the use of powerful pretrained computer vision models. However, prior work primarily focuses on spatial-only representations where the cursor coordinates are plotted as a trajectory image. These representations discard the temporal characteristics which can help discriminate users. In this work, we propose image representations that incorporate speed and velocity information for mouse dynamics authentication. We investigate two mouse data representations, a XYPlot representation that intuitively shows the path of movement, and a Pairwise Distance (PWD) representation that utilizes the entire image canvas to encode relationships between mouse events. Both representations are evaluated under both location-aware and location-free settings using pretrained 2D-CNN and ViT architectures. We found that the incorporation of temporal information improved performance over spatial representations alone, and that the location-aware variants generally outperformed the location-free. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on two datasets, Balabit and TWOS, and extend our evaluation to two other datasets. We found that in general, location-aware PWD with temporal encoding combined with the 2D-CNN architecture provided the best results, with mean EERs of 5.21% and 12.70% on the Balabit Test-1 and Test-2 sets, respectively, 4.94% on TWOS, 6.16% on ChaoShen, and 0.59% on DFL.

</details>


### [49] [MobileWeave: Program-First Synthesis of Dependency-Linked Tasks for Training Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23860)
*Ziyi He,Xian Wu,Qiyuan Chen,Haitao Li,Cehao Yang,Yinghao Zhu,Xichen Zhang,Xiao Chen,Yushi Feng,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileWeave将移动GUI智能体任务合成为可执行程序：把App抽象为带跨App共享语义类型的类型化能力图，通过显式值/对象依赖组合原子程序为单App与跨App复合任务，并以原生执行验证，指令与完成校验同源于该程序；训练后MobileWeave-8B在MobileGym提升26.7分，在AndroidWorld/AndroidLab/MobileWorld分别提升8.6/21.3/17.6分，4B规模亦有相当增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生移动GUI智能体训练需要覆盖单App功能与App内/跨App多目标工作流的大规模轨迹，自动任务合成是扩展数据的关键途径。但现有合成流程把任务生成为自由文本，子目标之间的依赖关系隐式化；而"组合"本身是一项独立技能——策略可能分别完成每个子任务，却无法把它们串成完整工作流。

Method: 以可控模拟器的App实现为依据，把每个任务合成为可执行程序。具体为：(1) 将每个App抽象为类型化能力图，语义类型跨App共享；(2) 通过显式的值依赖与对象依赖，把原子程序组合为单App与跨App复合程序；(3) 通过原生执行验证每个程序，并由同一程序派生指令与完成校验；(4) 为这些任务采集教师轨迹，并在策略自身停滞处加入恢复监督进行训练。

Result: MobileWeave-8B相对基座模型在MobileGym提升26.7分，在AndroidWorld、AndroidLab、MobileWorld分别提升8.6、21.3、17.6分；4B规模取得可比增益。

Conclusion: 组合能力可从任务结构中习得：子目标间的显式依赖作为训练信号，教会策略组合其已掌握的技能。作者公开数据、模型与代码。

Abstract: Native mobile GUI agents require large-scale training trajectories covering both individual app functions and multi-goal workflows within and across apps, which makes automatic task synthesis a promising way to scale training data. Existing synthesis pipelines, however, produce each task as free-form text, leaving implicit how its subgoals depend on one another. This matters because composition is a skill in its own right: a policy may complete each subtask of a workflow on its own, yet fail to chain these subtasks into the complete workflow. We present MobileWeave, which instead synthesizes every task as an executable program derived from the app implementations of a controllable simulator. MobileWeave abstracts each app into a typed capability graph with semantic types shared across apps, composes atomic programs into single- and cross-app composite programs through explicit value and object dependencies, and verifies each program by native execution, deriving both its instruction and its completion check from the same program. Trained on teacher trajectories collected for these tasks and on recovery supervision at the policy's own stalls, MobileWeave-8B improves its base model by 26.7 points on MobileGym and by 8.6, 21.3, and 17.6 points on AndroidWorld, AndroidLab, and MobileWorld, with comparable gains at 4B. Beyond these gains, we find that composition is learnable from task structure: explicit dependencies between subgoals act as a training signal that teaches a policy to compose skills it already masters. We release data, model, and code at https://maxxnotmarx.github.io/MobileWeave/ .

</details>


### [50] [Grounding Radiology Reports in 3D Chest CT: Results of the ReXGrounding Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23862)
*Mohammed Baharoon,Mahmoud Hussain Alabbad,Sung Eun Kim,Xiaoman Zhang,Lucas Bijnens,Zirong Chen,Jiayu Wang,Xi Ouyang,Mingyang Yu,Weiqiu Jin,Xiaodan Ye,Dijia Wu,Kwang-Hyun Uhm,Inhwa Son,Rachel van 't Hull,Tim Veenboer,Cees Snoek,Kevin Groot Lipman,Daesung Kim,Junha Im,Youngjun Jun,Sangwon Lee,Katelyn Morrison,Adam Perer,Mijin Yun,Ali Piracha,Reem Alwasiah,Talal Yahyan,Abdulrahman Alamoudi,Mashael Alshehri,Rayan Alsager,Hannah Hancock,Anna M. Hunkemöller,Peter Youssef,Mona A. Alhammad,Afnan Aljumayan,Brecht Van Berkel,Eric Bijnens,Saud Alhussain,Maarten Steyvers,Rolf Symons,Bryan Eelen,Ayed Alaklubi,Maha Buraykan Alshammari,Amirah Fahad Alshammeri,Tomas Gilvydis,Ibrahim AlTurki,Monerah Almohideb,Ali Alburkani,Shahad Alhomaiani,Fatima Jabali,Maximilian Rokuss,Moritz Langenberg,Yannick Kirchhoff,Benjamin Hamm,Klaus Maier-Hein,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci,Sezgin Er,Michael Moritz,Hassan R. Alomaish,Luyang Luo,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍首个面向"报告发现逐条3D分割"的挑战赛ReXGrounding Challenge，最佳方法Dice达0.375、命中率0.77，与两组放射科医师间的一致性相当，但非局灶性发现、多实例发现及文本解剖位置对齐仍是开放难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将放射学报告中每条发现逐一分割，可支持发现的测量、随访追踪以及可核验的AI报告生成；然而现有AI模型在该任务上表现很差，且此前缺乏专门基准与评测平台。

Method: 在MICCAI 2026组织ReXGrounding Challenge：输入平扫胸部CT与对应报告中的发现集合，要求输出相应的3D分割集合。使用ReXGroundingCT数据集的2,992例训练与200例验证扫描，在由认证放射科医师穷尽标注的300例扫描、582条发现的独立测试集上评测；共48支队伍、15个国家、760次提交。并以100例赛前基准与既往最佳模型VoxTell对比。

Result: 最佳方法Dice 0.375、命中率0.77，与两组独立放射科医师的阅片一致性（50例子集上Dice 0.32、命中率0.76）持平；赛前基准100例上冠军Dice 0.390，显著优于VoxTell的0.285，前六名中五支队伍对VoxTell进行了微调。非局灶性发现分割仍明显难于局灶性发现，实例F1（0.34）低于阅片者间一致性（0.47），且模型存在"分割到正确异常但位置错误"的现象。

Conclusion: 顶级方法在Dice上已达到放射科医师间一致性水平，表明任务可行且社区进展显著；但非局灶性发现、多实例发现以及遵循文本所述解剖位置仍是未解决问题，构成后续研究方向。

Abstract: Segmenting each reported finding in a radiology scan would allow findings to be measured and tracked over time and let AI systems generate reports that are verifiable, but so far AI models have performed poorly at this task. To advance this task, we organized the ReXGrounding Challenge at MICCAI 2026, the first challenge dedicated to it. Given a non-contrast chest CT scan and the set of findings from its report, participants trained models to produce the corresponding set of 3D segmentations. The challenge used 2,992 training and 200 validation scans from the ReXGroundingCT dataset, and evaluated methods on a held-out test set of 300 scans with 582 findings, exhaustively annotated by board-certified radiologists. In total, 48 teams from 15 countries made 760 submissions, and the best method reached a Dice of 0.375 and a hit rate of 0.77, matching agreement between two independent radiologist groups (Dice 0.32, hit rate 0.76, on a 50-scan subset). On the 100 scans of the pre-challenge benchmark, the winner reached a Dice of 0.390, against 0.285 for VoxTell, the best model before the challenge, which five of the six best teams fine-tuned. Non-focal findings remained far harder to segment than focal ones, instance F1 stayed below inter-reader agreement (0.34 vs. 0.47), and models sometimes segmented the right abnormality in the wrong location. Top methods thus match radiologist agreement in Dice, but non-focal findings, findings with multiple instances, and adherence to the anatomical location described in the text remain open problems.

</details>


### [51] [On Demand, Not Uniformity: Adaptive Expert Orchestration for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23865)
*Mengxin Qin,Kun Wei,Weitao An,Jiahua Li,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIRA-MoE框架，通过"覆盖率校准的专家注入"与"残差仲裁"解决多模态持续指令微调中"何处需要新增容量"与"新专家对每个token贡献多强"两个问题，在UCIT和CoIN上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MoE的方法通过冻结历史专家、新增专家来缓解遗忘，但忽略了不同任务在适应需求上的差异。探测实验表明任务在逐层梯度压力和逐token预测难度上存在显著异质性，说明"额外容量应加在哪些层"以及"新专家应以多大强度参与单个token的预测"这两个决策尚未解决。

Method: CIRA-MoE由两部分构成：(1) 覆盖率校准的专家注入：以可学习阈值路由产生"专家接受边际"，作为统一的覆盖率信号；利用历史接受证据判断覆盖不足的层，并将新专家选择性地注入这些层。(2) 残差仲裁：依据历史专家与当前专家之间的相对证据调节token级残差贡献，并配合"仲裁一致性"正则化以促进专家专化。

Result: 在UCIT与CoIN两个基准上的综合实验表明，CIRA-MoE在MCIT任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以路由接受边际作为统一的覆盖率信号，可同时指导层级的专家选择性注入与token级的贡献调节，从而在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时提升对新任务的适应能力；该机制在标准MCIT基准上验证有效。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to adapt to sequential instruction data while retaining previously acquired knowledge. While recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based methods mitigate forgetting by freezing historical experts and introducing new ones, they largely overlook the varying adaptation demands across tasks. Probing incoming tasks reveals pronounced heterogeneity in layer-wise gradient pressure and token-wise prediction difficulty. This observation highlights two unresolved decisions: where additional capacity is needed and how strongly new experts should contribute to individual tokens. To address both questions, we propose the Coverage-Calibrated Expert Injection and Residual Arbitration framework (CIRA-MoE), which derives expert acceptance margins via learnable threshold routing to serve as a unified coverage signal. Specifically, historical acceptance evidence guides the selective injection of new experts into insufficiently covered layers. Meanwhile, relative evidence between historical and current experts modulates token-wise residual contributions, coupled with arbitration-consistent regularization to promote expert specialization. Comprehensive experiments on UCIT and CoIN demonstrate that CIRA-MoE outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in MCIT.

</details>


### [52] [HOForce: Motion and Force Reconstruction of Hand-Object Interaction Using a Single-View RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23871)
*Jiahui Wang,Wanqing Du,Yuanbin Zou,Zhefeng Xu,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HOForce，从单视图RGB-D视频与已知物体属性中联合跟踪手-物并恢复交互力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单相机捕获手-物交互可支撑大规模数据采集，但严重遮挡使重建困难；且现有工作多关注运动而忽略交互力。

Method: 联合建模双手指尖力，并在双物体场景中建模物体间接触力；将重建的力作为遮挡区域的位姿约束，反哺姿态跟踪。

Result: 在DexYCB与TACO上手/物运动跟踪更准确，DexYCB上穿模更少；在自建触觉力数据集上法向力MAE较最强基线降低24.5%。

Conclusion: 恢复的力可在MuJoCo中复现物体运动，并经适配迁移至其他物理引擎。

Abstract: Reconstructing hand-object interactions is an important task, and achieving it with a single camera makes large-scale capture practical but challenging due to heavy occlusions. We introduce HOForce to track hands and objects, and further recover interaction forces from single-view RGB-D video and known object properties. For two-hand manipulation, we jointly model fingertip forces of both hands and the contact forces between objects when two objects are presented. The reconstructed forces also improve motion tracking by giving pose constraints in the occluded regions. HOForce recovers hand and object motion more accurately on DexYCB and TACO and produces less hand-object penetration than interaction baselines on DexYCB. On our dataset of fingertip forces recorded with tactile sensors, HOForce reduces normal-force MAE by 24.5% relative to the strongest baseline. The recovered forces reproduce object motion in MuJoCo and transfer to other engines after adaptation.

</details>


### [53] [Keep the Image in Mind: A Visual Board for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23872)
*Haiming Zhu,Zhiwei Chen,Ding Cao,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以"Visual Board"为核心的推理框架，将视觉观察、不确定解释与派生推理显式分离到结构化、可修订的工作区中，无需任务特定训练即在多步视觉推理基准上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步视觉推理中，早期视觉误读会作为错误前提持续传播。作者认为视觉证据"如何被表征"与"收集多少证据"同样重要，而把观察与推理不断追加到无限增长的对话中会使前提隐含、难以审计。

Method: 构建容量受限、可修订的结构化工作区（Visual Board），把视觉观察、不确定解释和派生推理分开存放，使每个前提显式且可审计；即使仅用文本critic也能检测"所见"与"所推"之间的冲突，并触发对原图的定向重检。在相同工具、critic与交互预算下与自由形式笔记、无Board重试进行对照，并额外进行基于harness轨迹的监督微调。

Result: 在Euclid30K、SolidGeo与ChartQA柱状图上，4B、8B、Qwen3-VL-Flash、32B配置均一致超越独立模型，最高分别提升11.68、18.42、24.82个百分点；结构化解板较共享自由笔记高6.67–13.00分，较无Board重试高7.35–18.80分，同时节省7.9%–38.2%的token；在harness轨迹上SFT可进一步改善直接回答与无critic的工具使用，并正向迁移到图表推理。

Conclusion: 视觉证据的结构化表征而非单纯证据量是缓解多步视觉推理中错误前提累积的关键；显式、可审计、可修订的Board机制能以更低token开销带来显著且跨模型规模一致的性能提升，并可作为SFT数据来源产生可迁移收益。

Abstract: Multi-step visual reasoning suffers when early visual misinterpretations persist as incorrect premises in subsequent reasoning. We argue that how visual evidence is represented during reasoning matters as much as how much of it is gathered. Rather than appending observations and inferences to an ever-growing dialogue, we introduce a Visual Board-centered harness that separates visual observations, uncertain interpretations, and derived reasoning within a structured, capacity-bounded, and revisable workspace. This separation makes each premise explicit and auditable, so that conflicts between what is seen and what is derived become detectable even by a text-only critic and can trigger targeted reinspection of the original image. Without task-specific training, our harness consistently outperforms standalone models across 4B, 8B, Qwen3-VL-Flash, and 32B configurations on Euclid30K, SolidGeo, and ChartQA bar charts, with gains of up to 11.68, 18.42, and 24.82 percentage points, respectively. Under identical tools, critic, and interaction limits, the structured Board outperforms shared free-form notes by 6.67 to 13.00 points and no-Board retries by 7.35 to 18.80 points while using 7.9\% to 38.2\% fewer tokens. Supervised fine-tuning on harness trajectories further improves direct answering and critic-free tool use, with positive transfer to chart reasoning.

</details>


### [54] [NullTok: empirical-null token selection for training-free long video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23881)
*Zhang Ziyue,Juwei Jian,Baiyang Song,Yunshan Zhong,Jun Peng,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 NULLTOK，用经验零分布校准查询-视觉相关性，联合解决长视频中帧选择与token剪枝的证据丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频导致MLLM视觉token数量激增；现有帧选择与token选择两范式简单串联时，噪声化的帧级相关性估计会造成不可逆的证据丢失。

Method: 从查询相关证据的时空分布出发：对每帧每个patch，用64个分组匹配的无关控制查询构建经验零分布，刻画无目标证据时的期望得分；将目标亲和度校准为同位置右尾蒙特卡洛p值（条件可交换假设下离散超均匀）；再经校准筛选与高召回语义守卫生成候选token，做确定性视觉去重与联合时空预算分配，全程免训练。

Result: 在冻结LLaVA-Video-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B上，64 FFE预算、无字幕无音频条件下，Video-MME分别达65.81%与68.85%，均优于同预算下所有对比基线。

Conclusion: 以统计显著性校准替代启发式相关性打分，可在有限视觉token预算下有效提升长视频理解性能。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), mainly due to the large number of visual tokens accumulated in long videos. Currently, frame selection and token selection are two main paradigms for addressing this issue, both aiming to select relevant visual information to form a compact input for MLLMs. A natural approach combines these two paradigms by selecting frames before pruning tokens, but noisy frame-level relevance estimates can cause irreversible evidence loss. In this paper, we study this problem from the perspective of assessing the spatiotemporal distribution of query-specific evidence and propose a novel and training-free approach termed NULLTOK. For every patch across all frames, we construct an empirical-null distribution from 64 group-matched irrelevant control queries, capturing the scores expected in the absence of target-specific evidence. Target affinities are calibrated against these same-position references into right-tail Monte Carlo p-values, which are discrete super-uniform under a conditional exchangeability assumption. Calibrated screening and a high-recall semantic guard produce candidate tokens for deterministic visual deduplication and joint spatiotemporal budget allocation. To validate NullTok, we apply it to two representative MLLMs and conduct extensive experiments on a set of video benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness under a bounded visual token budget. For example, it achieves 65.81% and 68.85% on Video-MME using frozen LLaVA-Video-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones, respectively, at a cap of 64 full-frame equivalents (FFE), without subtitles or audio. These results outperform all evaluated baselines on Video-MME under the same budget for both backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/potato-kitty/NullTok.

</details>


### [55] [Can AI Understand Indonesian Children's Book Illustrations? Compliance, Safety, and the Cultural Alignment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23887)
*Rian Adam Rajagede,Rochana Prih Hastuti,Fitri Hasanah Amhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用印尼政府发布的《图书分级指南》作为评估框架，考察视觉语言模型（VLM）能否作为儿童绘本插画的评估者与生成者满足合规性、安全性与文化对齐三项标准。结果显示VLM在显性标准上可靠，但在文化对齐上系统性失效，AI生成插画也存在同样的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童绘本插画承担发展、教育与文化责任，常被正式写入指南。随着AI系统进入儿童出版流程（既作评估者也作生成者），需回答其能否可靠满足结构、安全与文化标准。现有研究缺少在有政府背书的真实指南框架下、同时检验VLM评判与生成两类角色的系统证据。

Method: 以印尼《图书分级指南》中针对四个阅读等级的显性视觉标准为政府评估框架，围绕合规性、安全性、文化对齐三标准展开。先让VLM评估真实出版的儿童绘本页面，并与人类基线评估对比；再将VLM作为评判者对两个生成模型产出的插画打分，并与部分人类插画师评分交叉验证。

Result: VLM在合规性、安全性等显性标准上与人类判断较为一致，可作为可靠评判者；但在文化对齐上表现不足，系统性遗漏人类评分者标记为文化不当的内容。AI生成插画同样满足结构与安全标准，却无法捕捉人类评分者一致识别的隐性文化细节。

Conclusion: VLM与AI生成模型目前只能胜任可显式编码的标准，对隐性文化适宜性存在系统性盲区，不宜单独承担儿童出版的质量把关。需在流程中保留人类文化审查环节，并推动指南把隐性文化标准显性化、可操作化。

Abstract: Children's book illustrations carry developmental, pedagogical, and cultural responsibilities that are often formally codified in guidelines. As AI systems begin entering children's publishing pipelines, both as evaluators and as generators, a natural question arises: can they reliably meet the structural, safety, and cultural standards required? We investigate this across three criteria: guideline compliance, safety, and cultural alignment, using Indonesia's Book Leveling Guideline as a government-issued evaluation framework with explicit visual criteria for four reading levels. To understand how well VLMs can identify and apply these guideline criteria, we first evaluate them on real published children's book pages and compare their assessments against a human baseline. To then examine whether AI-generated illustrations meet the same standards, we apply the VLM judge to images produced by two generation models and cross-check them against a subset of human illustrator raters. We find that VLMs are reliable on explicit criteria such as compliance and safety, but fall short on cultural alignment, systematically missing content that human raters flag as culturally inappropriate. AI-generated illustrations show the same blind spot: they satisfy structural and safety criteria but fail to capture implicit cultural details that human raters consistently identify.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Additive Composition: Interventional Modeling of State-Dependent Cross-Degradation Responses for Composite Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23889)
*Zongda Tang,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Mingtao Feng,Yuping Liang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PROBE介入式框架，显式建模复合退化间的状态依赖交叉响应，量化非加性偏差以联合引导复原，并构建含15种组合的SCD-15基准，在CDD-11、AWMM-100K、SCD-15上取得SOTA且模型紧凑、显存需求适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复合图像复原方法将复合退化分解为单一因子，依赖孤立先验分别引导复原；但共存退化本质上是相互依赖的——某一退化的表现形式会随其他退化状态动态变化，产生非加性交互，因子分解范式无法刻画这种交互。

Method: 1) 形式化“状态依赖的跨退化响应”；2) 通过对齐视觉与文本的退化-干净偏移构建统一退化轴；3) 在退化轴上用标定坐标头估计当前退化状态，并注入双向特征扰动以探测当前状态下的跨坐标响应；4) 将响应与强度匹配的单退化参考对比，量化内在非加性偏差，得到响应描述子与状态坐标共同引导复原；5) 构建SCD-15基准，覆盖四类退化因子在三种严重度下的全部15种非空组合。

Result: 在CDD-11、AWMM-100K与SCD-15三个复合复原数据集上达到SOTA，且模型参数量紧凑、GPU显存需求适中。

Conclusion: 以介入探测显式刻画跨退化非加性交互，优于因子分解范式；统一退化轴+状态坐标+响应描述子的设计为复合退化复原提供了可解释且高效的建模范式。

Abstract: Composite image restoration aims to recover images corrupted by multiple coexisting degradations. Existing methods predominantly decompose composite corruptions into individual factors, relying on these isolated priors to guide restoration. However, coexisting degradations are inherently interdependent: the manifestation of one degradation dynamically shifts with the states of others, inducing non-additive interactions that factor-wise paradigms fail to capture. To bridge this gap, we formulate state-dependent cross-degradation responses and propose PROBE, an interventional framework that explicitly models how coexisting degradations influence one another. Specifically, we first construct unified degradation axes by aligning visual and textual degraded-clean offsets. Along these axes, a calibrated coordinate head estimates current degradation states, while bidirectional feature perturbations are injected to probe cross-coordinate responses under the current states. Contrasting these responses with corresponding intensity-matched single-degradation references quantifies intrinsic non-additive deviations, allowing the resulting response descriptors and state coordinates to jointly guide restoration. For comprehensive evaluation, we introduce SCD-15, a new benchmark covering all 15 nonempty combinations of four degradation factors at three severity levels. Experiments demonstrate that PROBE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the CDD-11, AWMM-100K, and SCD-15 composite restoration datasets using a compact model with modest GPU memory requirements.

</details>


### [57] [SixthView: A Benchmark for Hidden-View Reconstruction and 3D Assembly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23891)
*Pooya Khosravi,Jen-Feng Chang,Fnu Pramono*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SixthView 基准：由五个正交视图与部件形状信息预测第六个正交视图，需输出稠密部件标签网格，用于检验三维装配的遮挡与符号推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推断三维装配的不可见一侧需要推理部件位置与遮挡；不同装配可匹配相同观测视图却产生不同隐藏视图，导致目标本身存在歧义。现有基准缺乏对该歧义的控制与细粒度评估。

Method: 构建 602 个合成 polycube 装配体（2–10 个部件）；对每一项穷举所有与精确部件几何及观测标签网格一致的合法装配，并验证其目标视图唯一，从而消除歧义。任务要求为缺失视图输出稠密部件标签网格，支持精确视图与逐单元（cell-level）评估。设置纯图像变体以额外考察遮挡下的形状恢复；在四种输入条件下评测三个视觉语言模型（图像、精确文本及其组合、单图面板 vs 合并拼图）。

Result: 在 552 个完整评测条目上，图像+精确文本的精确网格准确率为 33.2%–85.4%。在分离图像上加入精确文本提升 19.7–63.0 个百分点；反之在精确文本上加入图像仅变化 −0.5 至 +2.1 个百分点。分离图像面板优于合并拼图 6.7–11.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 性能高度依赖对精确符号信息的获取；几何被明确给出后，图像带来的额外增益很小。基准为装配级符号推理与遮挡感知的形状恢复提供了严谨的歧义控制与细粒度评测。

Abstract: Inferring the unseen side of a 3D assembly requires reasoning about part placement and occlusion. Different assemblies can match the observed views while producing different hidden views, leaving the target ambiguous. We introduce SixthView, a benchmark in which models predict a sixth orthographic view from five observed views and information about the component shapes. The benchmark contains 602 synthetic polycube assemblies with 2--10 parts and requires a dense grid of part labels for the missing view, allowing both exact-view and cell-level evaluation. For each item, we exhaustively verify that all legal assemblies consistent with the exact component geometry and observed label grids yield the same target view. With these inputs, the task tests symbolic assembly and coordinate reasoning. The image-only variants additionally require shape recovery from rendered views, where occlusion can make different 3D shapes look identical. We evaluate three vision-language models under four input conditions. Across 552 items with complete evaluations, exact-grid accuracy with images plus exact text ranges from 33.2% to 85.4%. Adding exact text to separate images improves accuracy by 19.7--63.0 percentage points, whereas adding images to exact text changes it by only $-0.5$ to $+2.1$ points. Separate image panels outperform a merged sheet by 6.7--11.1 points. The results show that performance depends strongly on access to exact symbolic information, with little further improvement from images once the geometry is specified. GitHub: https://github.com/facebookresearch/sixthview_benchmark

</details>


### [58] [ProxySearch: Decoupled Inference-Time Scaling for Diffusion Models via Asymmetric Noise-Rank Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23929)
*Seungwook Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文表征了蒸馏少步模型在噪声搜索中作为代理的潜力，发现代理与教师在图像统计量上排序高度一致、但在语义打分器上不一致，并据此提出PROXYSEARCH实现单次教师轨迹下的统计约束生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理期噪声搜索能提升文本到图像扩散，但每个查询需要大量多步教师rollout，代价高；蒸馏少步模型虽已普及，却缺乏其作为噪声搜索代理的系统刻画。

Method: 在三种蒸馏范式与三种骨干（DiT、MMDiT、UNet）上测量代理-教师排序一致性，并用配对零对照定位排序来源；据此提出PROXYSEARCH：代理单步按统计匹配筛选候选，教师仅返回语义奖励最高的幸存者，以联合约束（语义奖励定内容、数值目标定统计）避免两目标间的权重权衡。

Result: 排序呈显著不对称：闭式图像统计量可从代理迁移至教师（Spearman ρ最高0.80），学习式语义打分器不可迁移（ρ≤0.12）；粗粒度统计的一致性源自第一步去噪，细粒度统计源自蒸馏。在FLUX-1024上，同等教师轨迹数下统计目标收紧21–23%，优化奖励无明显损失。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏模型是统计类目标的可靠代理而非语义类目标代理；利用该不对称性可在不增加教师rollout的前提下显著提升统计约束的满足度。

Abstract: Noise search at inference time improves text-to-image diffusion but requires many multi-step teacher rollouts per query. Distilled few-step models are now routinely released, yet their potential as proxies inside noise search is uncharacterized. We measure proxy-teacher rank agreement across three distillation regimes and three backbones (DiT, MMDiT, UNet) and find a sharp asymmetry. On our main pool, closed-form image statistics transfer from the proxy to the teacher (Spearman $ρ$ up to 0.80), while learned semantic scorers do not ($ρ\le$ 0.12). Paired null controls trace this rank to the first denoising step for coarse statistics and to distillation for finer ones. We use the asymmetry for noise search under a joint constraint: a semantic reward specifies content, and a numeric target specifies image statistics. In PROXYSEARCH, the proxy filters candidates by statistical match in 1 step, and the teacher returns the survivor with the highest semantic reward. No weight trades one objective against the other. On FLUX-1024, PROXYSEARCH tightens the statistical target by 21-23% at the same number of teacher trajectories, with no detected loss on the optimized reward.

</details>


### [59] [TRoPE: Topology-aware Relative Positional Encoding for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23933)
*Chengzhuo Gui,Zhenglin Zhou,Fan Ma,Zongxin Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视频到4D网格生成中的“粘连伪影”（空间接近但测地距离远的顶点错误同步运动），提出拓扑感知相对位置编码TRoPE与顶点级尾部风险目标，并构建MeshSticking-4D基准，显著降低最坏顶点误差与分离失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式视频到4D网格方法易出现粘连伪影：当测地距离远但空间上接近的顶点被赋予相似运动信息时，会出现跨部件运动泄漏。作者将该失败归因于运动解码无法区分空间邻近性与网格连通性，且缺乏针对该失败模式的评测基准。

Method: 提出TRoPE：从图拉普拉斯谱中提取谱特征，并基于多尺度扩散特征间距离构建成对拓扑关系，通过旋转相位调制顶点—锚点注意力，使网格连通性在交互中显式化；同时引入顶点级尾部风险目标，重点惩罚持续高误差顶点；并构建专门基准MeshSticking-4D用于刻画与量化此类失败。

Result: 在MeshSticking-4D上，TRoPE在抑制粘连伪影的同时提升整体运动精度：相较基线最坏顶点误差降低39.4%，分离失败率由49.35%降至34.90%。在Motion-80与Motion324-Subset上对多样物体取得更低的尾部误差；迁移至Mesh4D表明其跨骨干网络适用。

Conclusion: 通过将网格拓扑显式注入运动解码的注意力交互，并结合尾部风险优化与专用基准评测，可有效缓解跨部件运动泄漏导致的粘连伪影，且方法具备跨数据集与跨骨干网络的泛化性。

Abstract: Video-to-4D mesh generation has made substantial progress in recovering dynamic 3D geometry and motion from monocular videos. However, commonly used feed-forward approaches remain prone to sticking artifacts, where geodesically distant vertices move together incorrectly when they become spatially close. We attribute this failure to cross-part motion leakage: spatially nearby yet structurally distant vertices may retrieve similar motion information when motion decoding fails to distinguish spatial proximity from mesh connectivity. To address this issue, we introduce TRoPE, a topology-aware relative positional encoding that makes mesh connectivity explicit in the interaction between vertex queries and mesh-supported motion sources. TRoPE derives spectral features from the graph Laplacian and constructs pairwise topological relations from distances between multi-scale diffusion features, using these relations to modulate vertex--anchor attention through rotary phases. We further introduce a vertex-wise tail-risk objective that emphasizes persistently high-error vertices to explicitly suppress sticking artifacts. To better characterize and quantify these failures, we introduce MeshSticking-4D, a dedicated benchmark for this failure mode. Experiments on MeshSticking-4D show that TRoPE suppresses sticking artifacts while improving overall motion accuracy. Compared with the baseline, it reduces the worst-vertex error by 39.4% and lowers the separation failure rate from 49.35% to 34.90%. Additional evaluations on Motion-80 and Motion324-Subset show lower tail errors on diverse objects, while transfer to Mesh4D demonstrates applicability across backbones.

</details>


### [60] [What Right-Angle Corners Reveal About an Uncalibrated Camera: Convex Cones, Certified Bounds and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23934)
*Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究图像中"三垂直棱相交"的箱角/墙角三叉点（junction）对所揭示的无标定相机内参的约束：每个三叉点对应一个显式凸的相机集合，仅焦距未知时为闭式区间，多三叉点标定转化为凸可行性问题，并刻画下界、上界与精确确定焦距的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 箱角或墙角处三条相垂直的棱在图像中表现为一点相交的三条线段，这类结构在室内与人工场景中极为常见。作者希望回答：仅凭这些三叉点本身（无需标定物、无需先验），能对无标定相机的内参施加何种几何约束，从而以图像自身信息完成标定。

Method: 将每个三叉点表示为与之相容的相机集合（内参空间中的显式凸集）；在只未知焦距的假设下，推导出该集合为闭式表达的区间。集合完全由图像信息计算，因而多点标定被表述为凸可行性问题；进一步分析单点何时给出焦距下界、何时还给出上界，以及多点何时唯一确定焦距，并对已知顶点的情形给出在有界测量误差下可认证的界。

Result: 几乎每个三叉点都能给出焦距的下界；论文刻画了额外给出上界的条件，以及多个三叉点精确确定焦距的条件。当顶点已知时，所得界可在测量棱方向存在任意有界误差的情况下被认证。

Conclusion: 图像中的三垂直棱三叉点含有可观的相机内参信息，可用凸集/凸可行性框架统一、鲁棒地利用，实现基于纯图像几何的（部分）自标定与界估计。

Abstract: The corner of a box or a room, where three perpendicular edges meet, appears in an image as three segments meeting at a point. We ask what such junctions reveal about an uncalibrated camera's intrinsic parameters. Each junction is consistent with an explicit convex set of cameras; when only the focal length is unknown, this set is an interval in closed form. These sets are computed from the image alone, so calibrating from many junctions is a convex feasibility problem. Almost every junction bounds the focal length from below; we characterize when one also bounds it from above, and when many junctions determine it exactly. For a known vertex, the bounds can be certified against any bounded error in the measured edge directions.

</details>


### [61] [Angular Conditional Distillation: Overcoming Classifier Geometry Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23941)
*Xuefei Zhang,Ruoqi Wang,Lei Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出角度条件蒸馏（ACD）：用角度坐标接口（ACI）重建学生分类器以消除幅值与偏置带来的几何失配，并用尺度不变条件蒸馏（SICD）仅迁移条件性非目标关系，从而在无特征适配器、无额外推理开销下取得SOTA并可作为即插即用模块提升现有KD流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器输出是知识蒸馏中最基础、普适可用的知识接口，但标准logit目标把可迁移的语义关系与依赖架构的参数化伪影纠缠在一起，导致师生分类器几何失配，直接迁移不可靠。

Method: ACD由两部分组成：1) 角度坐标接口（ACI），用角度坐标重构学生分类器，抑制幅度与偏置效应，构建几何兼容空间；2) 尺度不变条件蒸馏（SICD），对输出做标准化并只迁移条件性非目标关系，过滤不可迁移的绝对置信度。ACI界定"在哪里迁移"，SICD界定"迁移什么"。

Result: 大量实验表明ACD优于现有KD方法，达到SOTA；作为通用即插即用替代方案，可持续提升现有蒸馏流程，且无需特征适配器、不增加推理时开销。

Conclusion: 通过将迁移空间与迁移内容解耦（几何对齐+尺度不变的条件关系迁移），ACD有效缓解了分类器几何失配问题，为logit-based KD提供了一种高效、通用的改进范式。

Abstract: Classifier outputs provide a fundamental and univer- sally available knowledge interface for knowledge distil- lation (KD). However, standard logit objectives entangle transferable semantic relations with architecture-dependent parameterization artifacts, causing a classifier geometry mismatch that makes direct knowledge transfer unreliable. To address this, we propose Angular Conditional Distilla- tion (ACD). First, the Angular Coordinate Interface (ACI) reconstructs the student classifier using angular coordi- nates, suppressing magnitude and bias effects to provide a geometry-compatible space. Then, Scale-Invariant Condi- tional Distillation (SICD) standardizes outputs and exclu- sively transfers conditional non-target relations, filtering out untransferable absolute confidence. Together, ACI de- fines where to transfer, while SICD defines what to transfer. Extensive experiments show that ACD achieves state-of-the- art performance over existing KD methods. Moreover, as a universal drop-in replacement, it consistently improves existing distillation pipelines while requiring no feature adapters or additional inference-time cost. Code is avail- able at https://github.com/laniusnan/ACD.

</details>


### [62] [SIGNTOK: A Text-Aligned Continuous Pose Tokenizer for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23944)
*Nabeela Khan,Gaurav Rai,Bowen Wu,Sihan Tan,Benjamin Yen,Takeshi Ashizawa,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SIGNTOK——一个连续姿态分词器，通过三个仅训练时使用的预测头（运动学、句子级、词级）重塑潜空间，使其按语言内容而非签名者身份组织，从而提升文本条件手语生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件手语生成（SLG）直接在姿态空间、量化码空间或为重建训练的潜空间中生成，但这些空间主要由签名者身份而非语言内容组织：在 CSL-Daily 上，原始姿态空间中测试片段的最近邻仅为"同一句子由他人签署"的比例仅 2%，说明表示不具备语言对齐性。

Method: SIGNTOK 是一个连续姿态分词器，潜空间由三个仅在训练阶段使用的头共同塑造：运动学头预测运动动态；句子级头将手语与整体文本对齐；词级头将短运动窗口与内容词对齐。跨签名者同句最近邻率因此提升至 82%。

Result: 同一生成器在 SIGNTOK 潜空间而非姿态空间中训练时，在三个手语基准的六个"基准-评分器"设置中有五个将回译 BLEU-4 提升，关节精度相当；且词级对齐在每个基准上都优于句子级对齐。

Conclusion: 生成空间的组织方式与生成器同样重要；以语言对齐目标塑造的连续潜空间可有效解耦签名者身份、提升手语生成质量，且细粒度（词级）对齐收益更大。

Abstract: Text-conditioned sign language generation (SLG) depends not only on the generator but also on the space in which signing is generated. Existing approaches generate directly in pose space, in quantized code spaces, or in learned latent spaces trained for reconstruction. We find that these spaces are organized largely by signer identity rather than linguistic content: on CSL-Daily, a test clip's nearest neighbor in raw pose space is the same sentence signed by another person in only 2% of cases. To address this, we propose SIGNTOK, a continuous pose tokenizer whose latent space is shaped by three training-only heads: a kinematics head that predicts motion dynamics, a sentence-level head that aligns signing with the overall text, and a word-level head that aligns short motion windows with content words. Together, these objectives raise the same-sentence cross-signer nearest-neighbor rate to 82%. On three sign language benchmarks, the same generator trained in the SIGNTOK latent instead of pose space raises back-translation BLEU-4 in five of six benchmark-scorer settings at comparable joint accuracy, and word-level alignment gives higher back-translation scores than sentence-level alignment on every benchmark.

</details>


### [63] [How Complete Must an Environment Be to Train a Capable GUI Agent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23946)
*Jingxing Wang,Teng Wang,Xingyu Lou,Zihan Guo,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MobileGym 基准，沿奖励覆盖、数据覆盖、特征覆盖三个维度分别调控 GUI 环境完整度并训练 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct；发现奖励/数据覆盖不全会降性能，而剪除无关特征反而提升成功率达 56.13%，据此构建的 MobileGym-Lite 以更低成本超越完整环境（55.48% vs 51.61%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体在可执行环境中训练，但为降低构建成本、批量搭建环境并减少人工，各工作都在环境构造上做取舍，且这些取舍仅基于工程直觉，未量化其对训练效果的影响。核心问题是：训练一个有能力智能体所需的环境完整度到底要多高，以及应在哪些维度上保持完整。

Method: 提出 MobileGym，将环境构造取舍归纳为三个维度：奖励覆盖（验证器奖励的达成目标轨迹）、数据覆盖（任务读写之外的暴露数据）、特征覆盖（任务使用之外的应用功能）。在 MobileGym 中分别独立调整每一维度，训练 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct，并在原始完整环境中评估各策略；进一步分析 episode 加权方式的影响。

Result: 仅奖励单条参考路径的验证器使成功率下降 10.53 分；移除冗余记录数据亦降低成功率；剪除无关特征则使成功率从 51.61% 升至 56.13%，且增益完全来自特征未被改动任务的迁移。当每 episode 等权加权时增益反转，仅在剪枝任务上平衡 episode 时增益消失；未剪枝时长度加权会强化长绕路的成功轨迹，损害其他任务技能。

Conclusion: 据此构建 MobileGym-Lite，保持奖励与数据覆盖完整、剪除无关特征，在完整环境中以更低构建成本取得 55.48% 成功率，优于完整环境 MobileGym 的 51.61%，表明环境完整度应在维度上有选择地保留而非一味求全。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are widely trained in executable GUI environments. To lower cost, build environments in batches, and reduce manual effort, each work trades away part of the environment through its construction choices. We describe these choices along three dimensions: reward coverage, the goal-reaching trajectories the verifier rewards; data coverage, the application data the agent is exposed to beyond what the task reads or writes; and feature coverage, the application features beyond what the task uses. Existing works set each dimension on engineering grounds, but they do not measure its effect on training. How complete must an environment be to train a capable agent, and along which dimensions? In MobileGym, we adjust each dimension separately, train Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct, and evaluate every policy in the original full environment. A verifier that rewards only one reference path lowers success by 10.53 points, and removing unneeded records also lowers success. Pruning unneeded features instead raises success from 51.61% to 56.13%, entirely on tasks whose environments it left unchanged. The gain reverses when each episode is weighted equally and vanishes when only episodes on the pruned tasks are balanced. Without pruning, length weighting there reinforces long detour successes that erode skills on other tasks. Following these findings, we build MobileGym-Lite, which keeps reward and data coverage complete and prunes unneeded features. It outperforms Full in the full environment (55.48% versus 51.61% success) with lower construction cost.

</details>


### [64] [VOL-Agent: Programming Your Film via Video Orchestration Language Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23950)
*Hongji Yang,Yucheng Zhou,Lingting Zhu,Shengju Qian,Xinye Li,Huan Zheng,Dongyue Lu,Wei Chow,Xin Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可编程视频智能体 VOL-Agent，基于视频编排语言 VOL 实现分钟级、故事连贯的长视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型只能生成高质量但时长有限的片段，将其编排为分钟级、叙事连贯的影片仍是开放问题；现有视频智能体高度依赖自然语言规划或预定义工作流，导致制作过程难以编程、定制与扩展，且跨片段一致性差。

Method: 构建视频编排语言 VOL；采用静态编译与验证加运行时动态加载，将结构可靠性与执行灵活性解耦；技能驱动执行器从开放世界仓库动态解析工具与技能并支持自然语言用户自定义技能；具备从学得知识中构造新导演技能并通过反馈自我改进的能力；引入结构化记忆机制记录与召回世界及实体状态以维持跨镜头一致；结合评论家反馈进行局部修订并复用制作知识。

Result: 在三个基准上的实验表明，VOL-Agent 能以较低成本生成整体质量较高的分钟级视频。

Conclusion: 通过语言化编排与可编程智能体的结合，兼顾长视频生成的结构可靠性与执行灵活性，并借助结构化记忆与反馈机制提升跨片段一致性及经验积累能力。

Abstract: Recent video foundation models generate high-quality and limited-length clips, but orchestrating them into minute-level, story-coherent films remains an open problem. Existing video agents highly rely on natural-language planning or predefined workflows, which make their production hard to program, customize, and extend. Therefore, we introduce VOL-Agent, a programmable video agent built on a Video Orchestration Language (VOL). With static compilation and verification, followed by runtime dynamic loading, VOL-Agent decouples structural reliability from execution flexibility. Through the skill-driven executor, VOL-Agent dynamically resolves tools and skills from an open-world repository and supports user-defined skills in natural language. Moreover, VOL-Agent is capable of crafting new director skills from learned knowledge and improves itself through feedback to accumulate filmmaking knowledge. To overcome the disconnect between different segments, we apply a structural memory mechanism to record and recall world and entity states, sustaining entity and scene consistency across different shots. Critic feedback supports local revision, while reusable filmmaking knowledge and experiences can be retained for subsequent tasks. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VOL-Agent generates minute-scale videos with strong overall quality and limited cost.

</details>


### [65] [MitoWorld: A Stochastic World Model of the Single-Cell Cycle under Genetic Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23953)
*Ting Long,Xiaojing Sun,Cheng Han,Zhicheng Ji,Yingnian Wu,Jiebo Luo,Heng Fan,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MitoWorld：一个随机世界模型，联合建模基因敲低后单细胞外观的连续演化与分裂时间，在隐空间生成轨迹并以分裂风险终止、经条件扩散渲染为图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法只能预测终点或群体水平变化，无法同时建模单细胞轨迹与分裂时序；而基因敲低后的细胞未来外观演化与分裂时刻本身具有不确定性。

Method: 以高斯参考隐空间中的连续随机演化建模外观，普通转移可证明保持高斯参考分布（噪声与动力学绑定）；配对观测可辨识平稳边际无法辨识的离散转移；分裂用风险函数（hazard）建模为生存似然，再由条件扩散渲染器转回图像。

Result: 在覆盖17种敲低与1个对照的325条MitoCheck留出轨迹上，FID降低34.6%、视频指标JEDi降低45.6%；隐空间预测区间90%覆盖率接近名义值，且比自由拟合噪声的模型窄35%；风险函数给出的细胞周期长度分布KS距离接近基于模型的有限样本参考。

Conclusion: 可检验的单细胞随机动力学为延时扰动筛选提供了生成式工具。

Abstract: A cell observed after a gene knockdown has an uncertain future, both in how its appearance will evolve and when it will divide. Existing approaches predict endpoints or population-level changes without jointly modeling individual trajectories and division timing. In this work, we present MitoWorld, a stochastic world model that generates these future appearances as continuous evolution in a Gaussian reference latent, terminated by a division hazard and converted into images by a conditional diffusion renderer. The ordinary transition provably preserves its Gaussian reference, tying its noise to its dynamics; paired observations identify its discrete transition where stationary marginals cannot. The hazard turns division timing into a survival likelihood that can be tested against real cycle lengths. On 325 held-out MitoCheck tracks spanning 17 knockdowns and a control, MitoWorld lowers FID by 34.6\% and the video metric JEDi by 45.6\% relative to the strongest baselines. Its latent-space prediction intervals achieve near-nominal 90\% coverage, are 35\% narrower than those of a model with freely fitted noise. The hazard also yields cell-cycle length distributions with KS distance close to the model-based finite-sample reference. Testable single-cell stochastic dynamics thus offer a generative tool for time-lapse perturbation screens.

</details>


### [66] [SimplePro: A Simple Baseline for Proactive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23954)
*Yilin Wang,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的主动视频理解基线SIMPLEPRO，仅靠显式规则与结构化流式上下文，使冻结VLM在主动流式场景下超越专用系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长离线长视频理解，但在主动流式场景中难以判断"何时该回答、何时应保持沉默"；已有工作依赖任务特定强化学习或辅助触发模块，代价高。

Method: 无需训练：每个决策步融合近期高分辨率帧、有界历史观测与既往答案记录，判断新证据是否值得响应，需要时生成简洁答案；再对连续重复答案过滤，同时保留原始输出作为后续决策上下文。无需参数更新与辅助触发器。

Result: 以4B骨干在OVO-Bench、StreamingBench-PO、ProactiveVideoQA上一致优于专用主动系统；性能随骨干从2B增至32B整体提升。

Conclusion: 简单有效的免训练方法可作为主动视频理解的强基线，作者将开源代码与推理流程。

Abstract: Proactive video understanding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to decide not only what to answer as a video unfolds, but also when newly arrived evidence warrants a response and when to remain silent. Although modern VLMs excel at offline long-video understanding, they struggle in proactive streaming settings, leading prior work to introduce task-specific reinforcement learning or auxiliary triggering modules. We find that explicit reporting rules and structured streaming context substantially improve a frozen VLM's proactive response performance. We exploit this observation to build SIMPLEPRO, a training-free baseline for proactive video understanding. At each decision step, the model combines recent high-resolution frames, bounded historical observations, and prior answer records to determine whether new information warrants a response and, when needed, generate a concise answer. The system then filters consecutive duplicate answers while retaining the raw outputs as context for subsequent decisions. Without any parameter updates or auxiliary triggers, SIMPLEPRO with a 4B backbone consistently outperforms specialized proactive systems across OVO-Bench, StreamingBench-PO, and ProactiveVideoQA. SIMPLEPRO shows overall performance gains as backbone size increases from 2B to 32B. We hope this simple yet effective method will serve as a strong baseline for proactive video understanding, and we will release our code and inference pipeline to support future research.

</details>


### [67] [DORMS: Image Segmentation via Depth-Ordered Region Merge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23958)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DORMS：基于单目深度图的深度序区域合并分割，无需神经网络或学习参数，在Cityscapes上超过所有零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不依赖学习分割模型的纯算法几何推理用于图像分割的可行性，以单目深度估计器输出的深度图为输入。

Method: 利用现成单目深度估计器提供深度图；通过确定性并查集扫描构建深度序合并树；以区域面积、深度持久性和空间接触约束指导区域合并；用阈值搜索按目标区域数量确定分割粒度；并用全局深度去趋势提升局部几何结构可分性。

Result: 在COCO Panoptic、Cityscapes和ADE20K上相比经典分割基线持续提升，与零样本神经分割模型性能相当；在Cityscapes上优于所有评估的零样本模型，在COCO和ADE20K上具竞争力。

Conclusion: 纯算法几何推理在无学习分割模型情况下具备竞争力，验证了深度序区域合并在图像分割中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce DORMS (Depth-Ordered Region Merging for Segmentation), a purely algorithmic approach to image segmentation from monocular depth maps, requiring no neural networks or learned parameters in the segmentation process. An off-the-shelf monocular depth estimator provides the input depth maps. Our method constructs depth-ordered merge trees through a deterministic union-find sweep, combining component area, depth persistence, and spatial contact constraints to guide region merging. A threshold search determines the segmentation granularity according to a specified target region count, while global depth detrending improves the separation of local geometric structures. Extensive experiments on COCO Panoptic, Cityscapes, and ADE20K demonstrate consistent improvements over classical segmentation baselines and competitive performance against zero-shot neural segmentation models. Notably, our method outperforms all evaluated zero-shot models on Cityscapes, while remaining competitive with several approaches on COCO and ADE20K. These results highlight the potential of purely algorithmic geometric reasoning for image segmentation without learned segmentation models.

</details>


### [68] [SEE: Unlocking the Hidden Perception of Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23962)
*Alin-Ionut Popa,Tuo Zhang,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 SEE：当 VLM 初始回答置信度低时，让冻结模型自指向区域、在放大裁剪上重新作答，并按置信度加权投票聚合答案；在五类骨干上小目标基准提升 2.3–13.1 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 能把握图像整体内容，却常忽略问题所依赖的细节（大图中的小目标、密集信息图中的数字）；但即便答错，其指向的区域往往已包含证据，说明模型具备"指对但看不清"的可利用信号。

Method: Self-Elicited Evidence（SEE）：训练无关，冻结 VLM。初始答案置信度低时，提示模型自行提出可能含细节的区域，对放大裁剪重新回答，并与原始预测做置信度加权投票；同时让模型以自己的转写文本作答以应对文本密集图像；无需检测器或 OCR 引擎。

Result: 在五种骨干网络上，SEE 使每个小目标基准提升 2.3–13.1 分，并在小目标任务上优于五个免训练基线；配合 SEE，Qwen3.5-4B 超过 Qwen3.5-9B 的直接作答，并与体积大 6 倍的 Qwen3.5-27B 持平。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身"指向即含证据"的特性做局部重看，是一种零额外模块、可即插即用的有效增益手段，能以极低成本弥合小模型与更大模型在细节感知上的差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) grasp the overall content of an image but often miss the fine details a question hinges on, such as a small object in a large photo or a number in a dense infographic. Yet even when their answer is wrong, the region they point to often contains the evidence. We introduce Self-Elicited Evidence (SEE), a training-free method that lets a frozen VLM look again where it points. When the initial answer has low confidence, SEE prompts the model to propose regions where the details may appear, answers the question on these enlarged crops, and aggregates the answers with the original prediction through confidence-weighted voting. The model also answers with its own transcript, which helps on text-rich images. SEE needs no detector or OCR engine. Across five backbones, SEE improves every small-object benchmark by 2.3-13.1 points and outperforms five training-free baselines on small objects. With SEE, Qwen3.5-4B surpasses the direct answer of Qwen3.5-9B and matches the 6 times larger Qwen3.5-27B.

</details>


### [69] [Dual-Manifold Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23964)
*Hongxu Chen,Chun Wang,Yanghao Wang,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Zhixiang Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双流形VAE（DM-VAE），用内部低维表示作为拓扑正则器约束高维潜空间，无需外部基础模型即在ImageNet-1K上取得SOTA重建与生成FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型中扩大VAE潜维度可提升重建上限，但缺乏结构约束会使潜流形崎岖病态，生成时潜变量漂移至流形外（off-manifold问题）。现有工作用DINOv2等外部基础模型做拓扑正则，但冻结的外部先验受预训练分布与语义偏置限制，在分布外域难以刻画真实视觉细节，本质上限制了泛化性。

Method: 观察到低维潜变量天然形成更平滑、利于生成的流形，提出DM-VAE：并行设置高维重建分支与提取干净拓扑骨架的低维分支，将高维流形锚定到该光滑子空间，实现内部表示正则。并引入拓扑表示对齐（TRA）在训练时监督生成器中间特征，以及内部骨架引导（IG_TS）在推理时引导结构成分并保留高频细节。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K 256×256上，无基础模型的DM-VAE取得SOTA的rFID 0.12，生成FID50K为1.28（无CFG）与1.20（有CFG）；与DINOv2正交结合后进一步达到1.11。

Conclusion: 内部低维表示可作为有效且更通用的拓扑正则器替代外部冻结先验，缓解off-manifold问题；TRA与IG_TS分别从训练监督与推理引导层面强化该框架，且可与外部先验正交叠加获得进一步收益。

Abstract: In latent diffusion models, expanding the latent dimension of variational autoencoders (VAEs) is essential to push the upper bound of reconstruction quality. However, without explicit structural constraints, such unregularized expansion easily yields a rugged and ill-conditioned latent manifold, causing generated latents to drift off the manifold during generation (i.e., off-manifold problem). Recent works smooth this manifold by using external foundation models (e.g., DINOv2) as a topological regularizer.
