However, we argue that tying the latent space to frozen external priors inherently restricts generalization: bound by their pre-training distributions and semantic biases, they may fail to capture faithful visual details on out-of-distribution domains. Observing that low-dimensional latents naturally form a smoother, generation-friendly manifold, we propose dual-manifold VAE (DM-VAE) to leverage internal representations as a topological regularizer. Specifically, DM-VAE pairs a high-dimensional reconstructive branch with a low-dimensional branch that extracts a clean topological skeleton, anchoring the high-dimensional manifold to this smooth subspace. Building upon this framework, we further introduce topological representation alignment (TRA) to supervise intermediate generator features during training, and internal guidance with topological skeleton (IG_TS) to guide structural components while preserving high-frequency details at inference. On ImageNet-1K (256x256), our foundation-model-free DM-VAE achieves a state-of-the-art rFID of 0.12 and a generation FID50K of 1.28 (w/o CFG) and 1.20 (w/CFG), and further reaches 1.11 when orthogonally combined with DINOv2.

</details>


### [70] [TOMAS: Topology-Aware Operation Modeling and Adaptive Staging for Egocentric Hand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23970)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Erhang Zhang,Chenyang Xu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TOMAS框架，用于第一人称视频中的手部未来运动预测，通过拓扑感知的手-物推理与自适应分阶段机制实现操作意图条件下的轨迹预测，在多个公开基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使操作同一物体，不同操作意图（manipulation intention）也会导致显著不同的手部轨迹；现有方法未充分挖掘关节化手部运动与物体语义之间的内在关联，难以捕捉这种“操作依赖”的运动模式。

Method: 1) 拓扑感知的手-物推理机制：将手部骨架结构与关节运动动力学（articulation dynamics）同物体语义、空间关系关联，联合推断潜在交互目标与隐含操作类型，形成物体-操作表示作为交互条件；2) 自适应分阶段机制：将未来手部运动组织为带可学习时间边界的有序潜在阶段，并引入阶段特定的几何子目标引导轨迹细化；3) 监督信号按各数据集可用标注自适应调整。

Result: 在多个公开基准上，TOMAS在多维度、多目标的手部运动预测任务中取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模手部拓扑结构与物体语义的耦合关系、并按操作阶段分解未来运动，是提升第一人称手部轨迹预测的有效途径。

Abstract: Forecasting future hand movements from egocentric videos plays an important role in understanding human intentions and hand-object interactions. However, different manipulation intentions can lead to substantially different hand trajectories even when interacting with the same object. Existing methods have not sufficiently explored the intrinsic relationships between articulated hand movements and object semantics to capture such operation-dependent motion patterns. Therefore, we propose TOMAS, a topology-aware operation modeling and adaptive staging framework for egocentric hand forecasting. Specifically, we develop a topology-aware hand-object reasoning mechanism that associates hand skeletal structures and articulation dynamics with object semantics and spatial relationships to jointly infer potential interaction targets and latent manipulation operations. The resulting object-operation representations provide interaction conditions for future hand trajectory prediction, with supervision adapted to the annotations available in each dataset. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive staging mechanism that organizes future hand movements into ordered latent stages with learnable temporal boundaries. Stage-specific geometric subgoals are then incorporated to guide trajectory refinement according to the underlying manipulation patterns. Experimental results on multiple publicly available benchmarks demonstrate that TOMAS achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-dimensional and multi-target hand motion forecasting.

</details>


### [71] [Agreement Is Not Accuracy: LiDAR Elevation Errors Hidden in LiDAR-Camera Extrinsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23972)
*Yeon Kun Lee,Dongseok Yang,Deok-Kyeong Jang,Choongho Chung,Junyoung Lee,Hyeongseok So*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出LiDAR与相机观测一致性并不能证明传感器内部几何正确：系统性LiDAR高程偏差可被吸收进外参估计。作者利用同步的LiDAR强度与相机估计的棋盘格位姿，联合估计跨录制共享的高程修正与逐录制的位姿，在六个同型平台将平移参考均值误差从7.57 mm降至3.04 mm，并改善面间角度与图像边缘对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LiDAR-相机标定中，通常以观测一致性作为正确性依据，但两者一致性并不能保证任一传感器内部几何（如LiDAR高程/测距偏差）正确。此类系统性高程偏差可与外参耦合，被外参吸收而掩盖，因此需要一种能分离并检验内部几何误差的方法。

Method: 采用同步LiDAR强度与相机估计的棋盘格位姿，联合优化：一个跨所有录制共享的传感器特定高程修正，以及每条录制单独的外参。用射线-平面求交定位棋盘格图案，而不使用LiDAR上报的距离，避免测距误差直接影响查表位置。在匹配的强度与修正模型下，该查表方式比使用实测点查表产生更小的相机一致性残差。

Result: 在六个同设计、同型号传感器的平台上，排除录制后的修正将到平移参考的平均距离从7.57 mm降至3.04 mm；在独立结构上，与独立密集相机扫描参考相比降低面间角度误差，并改善图像边缘对齐。滚动平台时，角度改善最大的平面对发生转移，符合仅高程修正模型的预测。而在射线-平面模型内，标靶投影与闭环一致性残差变化很小。

Conclusion: 外参标定后的一致性并不足以证明LiDAR内部几何正确；高程偏差可被外参吸收。利用对熟悉棋盘格的同步观测，可在无需专用大型标定场的情况下实用地估计并检验高程修正。作者计划公开代码与数据以复现主要结果。

Abstract: LiDAR and cameras provide complementary measurements for 3D vision, yet agreement between their observations does not establish the accuracy of either sensor's internal geometry. We show that systematic LiDAR elevation biases can be absorbed into the estimated extrinsics. Using synchronized LiDAR intensities and camera-estimated checkerboard poses, we jointly estimate a sensor-specific elevation correction shared across recordings and extrinsics for each recording. Ray-plane intersections locate the checker pattern without using reported ranges, preventing range errors from directly moving the lookup. Under matched intensity and correction models, this lookup gives smaller camera-consistency residuals than measured-point lookup. Across six identically designed rigs using the same sensor models, recording-excluded corrections reduce the mean translation-reference distance from 7.57 to 3.04 mm. On separate structures, they reduce inter-plane angle errors against references from independent dense camera scans and improve image-edge alignment. Rolling the rigs shifts the plane pairs exhibiting the largest angular improvements, as predicted for an elevation-only correction. Within the ray-plane model, calibration-target projections and loop-consistency residuals change little despite these geometric improvements. These findings show why agreement after extrinsic calibration need not establish correct internal LiDAR geometry. Synchronized observations of a familiar checkerboard provide a practical route to estimating and testing elevation corrections without a dedicated large calibration field. We plan to release code and data for reproducing the main results.

</details>


### [72] [SIRAZ: Sparse Inter-Layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-Shot Visual Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23991)
*Neha,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIRAZ框架，通过模拟人眼层级视觉通路（DoG变换、多深度中间表征、残差门控适配、方向感知多尺度时序注意力）对齐脑信号与视觉表征，实现零样本图像检索，在THINGS-EEG上达92.5% top-1、99.27% top-5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口需要解读脑信号；神经视觉解码旨在从神经活动中推断视觉信息。生物视觉系统沿层级通路处理视觉信息，而现有方法学到的神经表征与生物视觉系统存在本质差异，因此需要一种模仿人类视觉层级结构、并对齐神经表征的框架。

Method: SIRAZ框架包含三部分：(1) 借鉴中央凹与巨细胞通路的Difference-of-Gaussians变换，联合建模早期视觉过程；(2) 提取图像在多个深度的中间表征，经Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism处理，以对齐语义与神经信息；(3) 引入Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder刻画神经信号的时间动态，将学得的神经表征与最终视觉表征对齐。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG数据集被试内设置下取得92.5% top-1与99.27% top-5准确率；在THINGS-MEG数据集上取得50.25% top-1与75% top-5准确率。

Conclusion: 将生物启发的视觉表征与神经动力学的结构化模态相结合，可改善跨模态对齐与零样本图像检索性能。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces aim to establish direct communication between neural activity and external systems, making interpretation of brain signals an important problem. In this context, neural visual decoding seeks to infer visual information from neural activity by learning a mapping between brain responses and the corresponding visual stimuli. It investigates how visual information emerges from neural signals and relates to perceptual processing while enabling the reconstruction or retrieval of visual stimuli from neural signals. Our brain primarily follows a hierarchical pathway to process visual information captured by the eye. However, neural representations learned by existing methods differ fundamentally from those produced by the biological visual system. To bridge this gap and mimic the human vision hierarchy, we introduce Sparse Inter-layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-shot (SIRAZ). SIRAZ is a framework that aligns neural visual representations by jointly modeling early visual processes via fovea- and magnocellular-pathway-inspired Difference-of-Gaussians transformations. To align the semantic information with neural information, it extracts multiple intermediate representations of the image at multiple depths and passes them through the Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism. To study the temporal dynamics of neural signals, the Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder is introduced to align the learned neural representation with the final visual representation. The proposed framework achieves 92.5% top-1 and 99.27% top-5 accuracy in the intra-subject setting on the THINGS-EEG dataset, and 50.25% and 75% top-1 and top-5 accuracy in the THINGS-MEG dataset. The results indicate that combining biologically motivated visual representations with the structured modalities of neural dynamics improves cross-modal alignment and zero-shot image retrieval.

</details>


### [73] [WaveView: The Novel View Is in the Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23994)
*Kaiwen Wang,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Yinzhe Shen,Frank Bieder,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveView将显式warp后的源图像注入预训练生成器初始噪声的相位，通过DT-CWPT实现局部各向异性相位注入，在保持网络结构、开销可忽略的前提下提升新视角合成质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式新视角合成中，显式warp方法依赖视频扩散模型重投影源图，隐式方法编码相机参数或3D线索，二者均带来额外推理开销，且生成布局常偏离真实几何。作者希望在不改动架构、低开销的条件下引入几何约束。

Method: 提出WaveView：对源视图做warp后，将其内容注入预训练扩散生成器初始噪声的相位中；采用Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform（DT-CWPT）实现局部、各向异性的相位注入，使生成器在遮挡/去遮挡区域与极线方向上的约束更宽松。

Result: 在DL3DV基准上每个重叠分箱均优于所有基线；最困难分箱中共视区域PSNR较共享生成器的MetaView提升0.84 dB，且速度为其2.1倍。动态NVS中，同一噪声方案微调AnyView，使其在AnyViewBench零样本PSNR从11.58提升至12.35 dB。

Conclusion: 以相位注入方式嵌入warp几何是高效低开销的NVS改进途径，兼具精度与速度优势，并可迁移至动态场景生成模型。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis (NVS) employs a diffusion model to render a static or dynamic scene from a target camera view. Explicit warp methods complete a reprojection of the source with video diffusion models, and implicit methods encode the camera parameters or 3D cues. Both add inference overhead, and the generated layout often drifts from the ground truth. We present WaveView, which injects the warped source into the phase of the initial noise of a pretrained generator. The Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform (DT-CWPT) allows local and anisotropic phase injection. The generator is thus constrained less in disoccluded regions and along the epipolar line. WaveView keeps the architecture and adds negligible overhead. On the DL3DV benchmark, it outperforms all baselines in every overlap bin. In the hardest bin, it gains 0.84 dB PSNR on the covisible region over MetaView, which shares its generator, while running 2.1 times faster. For dynamic NVS, fine-tuning AnyView with the same noise raises its zero-shot PSNR on AnyViewBench from 11.58 to 12.35 dB.

</details>


### [74] [ReSynth: Constructing Synthetic Supervision for Multi-Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23996)
*Yilin Wang,Xin Yu,Xinyi Guo,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReSynth 将单图感知与可控图像生成结合，自动合成跨图像关系监督数据；仅用约44万合成问答做 GRPO 训练（训练中无真实图像），即可在 BLINK、MUIRBench、VLM2-Bench 上显著提升 VLM 的跨图像关系理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类常通过比较同一对象或场景的不同视图来理解世界，但 VLM 将这些视图作为独立图像输入，难以建立跨图像关联。核心瓶颈是数据：训练需要"关系已知的图像组"（同一对象的多视角、同一场景的前后变化），这类样本难以从现有照片库大规模采集。

Method: 提出 ReSynth 数据合成框架，把单图感知转化为跨图像关系监督：局部编辑生成前后对比对，视角生成产生同一实例的多视图，经核验的场景内容支持计数与属性比较。实现上结合 Qwen3-VL-8B 的captioning、grounding 与计数能力，以及 Qwen-Image-2.1 的生成与编辑能力；先核验单图事实（物体计数、属性），再将事实组合构建多图 QA。最终在约 440K ReSynth 问题上用 GRPO 训练。

Result: 训练全程未使用真实图像，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 Qwen3-VL-8B 在 BLINK、MUIRBench、VLM2-Bench 上平均分别提升 17.73 与 6.10 分；Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的单图性能总体保持，Qwen3-VL-8B 在四个评测基准上全部提升。尤为突出的是，该监督使 8B 感知模型自身在 BLINK 与 MUIRBench 上超过 Qwen3-VL-235B。

Conclusion: 以可控生成合成跨图像关系监督是可行且高效的路径：无需真实图像即可获得超越真实数据规模的训练信号，小模型亦可通过该监督在跨图像理解任务上反超更大模型，说明数据构造方式对 VLM 关系推理能力具有决定性作用。

Abstract: People often understand the world by comparing different views of it. Vision-language models (VLMs), however, receive these views as separate images and still struggle to relate them. One problem is data. Training on these tasks requires groups of images with a known relationship: the same object from different viewpoints, or the same scene before and after a change. Such examples are hard to collect at scale from existing photo collections. We introduce ReSynth, which turns single-image perception and controllable image generation into cross-image relational supervision. Localized editing produces before-and-after pairs, viewpoint generation produces multiple views of the same instance, and verified scene contents support comparisons of counts and attributes. ReSynth combines Qwen3-VL-8B's captioning, grounding, and counting capabilities with Qwen-Image-2.1's generation and editing capabilities. It verifies single-image facts such as object counts and attributes, then combines these facts to construct multi-image QA. Using GRPO on approximately 440K ReSynth questions, with no real images during training, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B improve by 17.73 and 6.10 average points on BLINK, MUIRBench, and VLM2-Bench, while single-image performance is broadly preserved on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and improves across all four evaluated benchmarks on Qwen3-VL-8B. Notably, this supervision enables the 8B perception model itself to outperform Qwen3-VL-235B on BLINK and MUIRBench.

</details>


### [75] [ProtoBridge-CLIP: Observation-Masked Finding Prototypes for Medical Image--Report Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24002)
*Jinrui Wu,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将报告中"确诊发现/明确否定/未提及"三种语义状态显式区分的医学图文表示学习框架，在3个CT与5个胸片基准上取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局图文对比学习将整份报告压缩为单一研究级目标，混淆了确诊发现、明确否定与未提及概念等不同证据状态，导致监督信号含噪、定位能力弱。

Method: 对称全局图文对齐 + 观测掩码的发现级监督 + 确诊报告概念上的软关系目标；文本导出的发现原型与局部视觉token对齐；不确定与未提及概念被排除在发现标签之外。模型名以\model{}占位，需回填。

Result: 宏平均AUROC：CT-RATE 73.6%、RAD-ChestCT 66.5%、INSPECT 68.8%；ChestXray14 80.2%、CheXpert 91.4%、PadChest 78.5%、RSNA 86.5%、SIIM-ACR 90.8%。相对匹配的仅全局对照，发现目标与关系目标在全部数据集上带来一致增益；并辅以组件消融、配对/临床检索、定位、扰动鲁棒性、校准与逐发现分析。

Conclusion: 在医学图文表示学习中区别对待已观测与未观测报告概念具有实际价值，可提升多基准判别性能与可解释性相关指标。

Abstract: Radiology reports distinguish affirmed findings, explicit negations, and unmentioned concepts, but conventional global image--report contrastive learning often compresses these different evidence states into a single study-level target. We introduce \model{}, which combines symmetric global image--report alignment with observation-masked finding supervision and a soft relation objective over affirmed report concepts. Text-derived finding prototypes are aligned with local visual tokens, while uncertain and unmentioned concepts are excluded from direct finding labels. We evaluate the framework on three CT benchmarks and five chest-radiograph benchmarks, together with component ablations, paired and clinical retrieval, localization, perturbation robustness, calibration, and per-finding analyses. \model{} achieves macro-AUROC values of 73.6%, 66.5\%, and 68.8% on CT-RATE, RAD-ChestCT, and INSPECT, and 80.2%, 91.4%, 78.5%, 86.5%, and 90.8% on ChestXray14, CheXpert, PadChest, RSNA, and SIIM-ACR, respectively. Relative to the matched global-only controls, the findings and relation objectives yield consistent gains across the evaluated datasets. These results demonstrate the practical value of treating observed and unobserved report concepts differently during medical image--text representation learning.

</details>


### [76] [Choosing Supervised Post-Processing for Frozen Person Detectors: Information, Object Changes, and Complete Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24003)
*Tianyi Zhou,Zijun Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了冻结检测器输出端多种候选读出（评分、类别分布、几何、原生集合摘要、邻域元分类）在统一候选、监督与工作点条件后的检测效果与计算开销，发现引入原生集合上下文的高维读出能显著增加正确匹配，且优化后时延与元分类相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结检测器可通过监督式后处理提升最终输出质量，但选择哪种读出取决于"哪些可用信息真正改善检测"以及"其处理成本"，而现有做法常以特征维度或净检测增益作为采纳依据，缺乏在统一条件下的可比证据。

Method: 在相同的候选生成、监督信号与工作点条件下，比较五类候选读出：候选评分、类别分布、几何特征、原生集合（native-set）摘要、以及适配后的邻域元分类（MetaDetect 风格）；采用执行等价（execution-equivalent）优化后测量每请求时延，并在 CrowdHuman、MOT17Det、MIAP 上做源冻结迁移评估。

Result: CrowdHuman 320 张留出图像上，向 97 维读出加入原生集合上下文后多出 275 个正确匹配、96 个未匹配预测；另一 319 张队列中 Full100 匹配 6004 个目标，优于源选择原始 WBF 的 5848 与 MetaDetect 风格的 6002，相对原始 WBF 增益含 300 新增、144 丢失；在固定 64 图负载下，Full100 与元统计读出每完整热请求约 95 ms；7 个序列 223 帧 MOT17Det 上源冻结迁移得到正向点结果，但 MIAP 主结果无提升。

Conclusion: 应使用信息量充分的输出读出，同时将优化后的元分类保留为有竞争力的选项；仅凭特征维度或净检测增益判断是否采纳并不可靠，需同时考虑信息含量与处理成本。

Abstract: A frozen detector computes hypotheses that can support better final outputs through supervised post-processing. Choosing a readout requires knowing which available information improves detection and what its processing costs. We compare candidate scores, class distributions, geometry, native-set summaries and adapted neighborhood meta classification under common candidate, supervision and operating-point conditions. On 320 held-out CrowdHuman images, adding native-set context to a 97-dimensional readout yields 275 more matches and 96 more unmatched predictions. On a separate 319-image cohort, Full100 matches 6004 objects,versus 5848 for source-selected raw WBF and 6002 for a MetaDetect-style adaptation. Its gain over raw contains 300 added and 144 lost discoveries. After execution-equivalent optimization, Full100 and the meta-statistics readout both take approximately 95 ms per complete warm request on a fixed 64-image workload. Source-frozen transfer gives positive point results on 223 MOT17Det frames from seven sequences, while the main MIAP result shows no improvement. The study supports using sufficiently informative output readouts while retaining optimized meta classification as a competitive option. It also shows why feature dimension and net detection gain alone are unreliable guides to adoption.

</details>


### [77] [Freeforward4d: Annotation-free feed-forward 4d reconstruction of driving scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24007)
*Luosong Guo,Xiang Yang,Chaoyang Li,Ao Gao,Jiangming Shi,Yanyun Qu,Zhizhong Zhang,Jingyu Gong,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FreeForward4D，利用预训练视觉模型（相机、深度、目标轨迹）经尺度校准与跨时间配准统一到单一度量坐标系，仅用 RGB 视频实现前馈式 4D 驾驶场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 4D 重建方法依赖数据集提供的相机参数、LiDAR 深度或 3D 框标注，难以用于无标注驾驶视频；而预训练视觉模型的几何先验彼此不一致（多视图预测为短窗口且尺度/帧独立，单目深度仅逐图度量），无法直接使用。

Method: 核心思路是将上述先验统一到单一度量坐标系以替代标注：1）以单目度量深度校准重叠的预测窗口，并将各窗口跨时间配准到同一坐标系；2）在此几何上于 3D 空间完成跨相机 2D 轨迹关联；3）采用分层运动建模——共享目标速度叠加局部高斯修正，使训练与推理均仅需 RGB 视频。

Result: 在 Waymo 上达到 27.66 dB PSNR、0.836 SSIM，且无需尺度对齐即可恢复度量尺度的相机与深度；全图 PSNR 超过依赖标注的 STORM、SLARM-F、UFO、DGGT、StreetForward，在 8 秒窗口取得最佳 RGB 保真度；仅用 RGB 微调即可适配 nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2。

Conclusion: 表明经校准与配准的预训练视觉先验可有效替代数据集标注，实现纯 RGB 驱动的前馈 4D 驾驶场景重建，并具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D reconstruction enables efficient creation of renderable driving scenes for autonomous driving simulation. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dataset-provided cameras, LiDAR depth, or 3D box annotations, limiting their applicability to unannotated driving videos. Pretrained visual models can predict cameras, depth, and object tracks from RGB alone, but their geometry is mutually inconsistent: multi-view predictions come in short windows with independent scales and frames, while monocular depth is metric only per image. Our key insight is that, once reconciled into a single metric frame, these priors can replace dataset annotations for feed-forward 4D reconstruction. We present \textbf{FreeForward4D}, which calibrates overlapping prediction windows against monocular metric depth and registers them into one frame across time. On this geometry, 2D tracks are associated across cameras in 3D, and objects move through hierarchical motion that combines a shared object velocity with local Gaussian corrections, so that both training and inference use only RGB videos. On Waymo, FreeForward4D achieves 27.66 dB PSNR and 0.836 SSIM while recovering metric-scale cameras and depth without scale alignment. It surpasses STORM, SLARM-F, UFO, DGGT, and StreetForward, which rely on dataset annotations, in full-image PSNR and attains the best RGB fidelity on 8-second windows. RGB-only fine-tuning further adapts the framework to nuScenes and Argoverse 2. Code will be released.

</details>


### [78] [HHW-Ego: DSLR-Quality Enhancement for Multi-Source Wearable Ego Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24010)
*Yunfei Tong,Qianqian Sun,Jixiang Luo,Dell Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文面向可穿戴第一人称影像画质提升，设计定制退化流水线生成配对样本，构建两阶段增强框架，并发布融合高光谱、HDR 与可穿戴自采集数据的 HHW-Ego 多源配对数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴智能眼镜及具身智能场景对第一人称图像需求旺盛，但其画质远逊于智能手机；根源在于缺乏配对的高质量参考图像及专用增强系统。

Method: 1) 设计定制化退化流水线，生成与可穿戴相机成像特征匹配的配对样本以提升增强性能上限；2) 构建两阶段增强框架：第一阶段在配对数据上微调高效模型以增强真实可穿戴图像，第二阶段利用高光谱数据修正色温以提升质量；3) 构建 HHW-Ego 数据集，融合高光谱、HDR 与可穿戴第一人称数据，含 5,000 组图像，覆盖室内/室外、白天/夜间场景。

Result: 在留出配对数据上，第一阶段微调使 Real-ESRGAN 与 PFT-SR 的 PSNR、SSIM、Lab 距离和 LPIPS 均有改善；在真实可穿戴图像上，通过无参考指标、人类偏好、色域分析及视觉对比评估了第二阶段的效果。

Conclusion: 定制退化配对数据可提升增强模型上限，两阶段框架结合高光谱色温修正能有效改善可穿戴第一人称图像质量，HHW-Ego 为相关研究提供了多源配对的基准数据支撑。

Abstract: Wearable ego-centric images are now in high demand for scenarios ranging from daily smart glass usage to embodied intelligence. But the image quality is far behind smart phone due to the lacking paired high-quality reference images and the dedicated enhancement systems. To solve this, a customized degradation pipeline is designed to generate paired samples matching wearable camera images, boosting the upper limit of enhancement performance. Besides a two-stage enhancement framework is further built: first, tuning an efficient model on the paired dataset to enhance real wearable images; second, using hyperspectral data to refine color temperature for better quality. Moreover we present HHW-Ego, a multi-source paired dataset integrating with Hyperspectral, High-dynamic range and Wearable Ego-centric data, which includes 5,000 image groups spanning indoor/outdoor and day/night scenes. On held-out paired data, Stage-1 fine-tuning improves PSNR, SSIM, Lab distance, and LPIPS for Real-ESRGAN and PFT-SR. On real wearable images, no-reference metrics, human preference, gamut analysis, and visual comparisons characterize the effects of Stage 2.

</details>


### [79] [FlyVQA: Seeing Video Frame Interpolation Quality through Fly Eyes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24012)
*Baoliang Chen,Lingyu Zhu,Zhiwei Zhong,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: I will analyze the abstract.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I will analyze the abstract.

Method: I will analyze the abstract.

Result: I will analyze the abstract.

Conclusion: I will analyze the abstract.

Abstract: The ultimate ambition of visual quality assessment is to reproduce the neural computations of human vision and thereby achieve full alignment with human perception. However, the immense complexity of human visual circuitry makes this goal difficult to realize. Recently released fly visual connectomes provide a tractable biological starting point. Despite differences between fly and human vision, the fly's well-characterized motion pathways motivate us to investigate their potential for assessing the perceptual impact of temporal distortions. In this paper, we use a shared, frozen fly visual circuit to perform full-reference quality assessment of frame-interpolated videos, whose artifacts can disrupt temporal consistency. Specifically, compound-eye sampling maps reference and distorted sequences into the circuit, while a connectome-aware readout quantifies discrepancies in cell-type-specific activity and excitatory--inhibitory synaptic dynamics. Reference-guided temporal pooling and a lightweight regression head integrate these differences into a quality score. Experiments across multiple interpolation methods demonstrate better agreement with human ratings than CNN, RNN, and Transformer baselines with comparable parameters, supporting a biologically grounded approach to perceptual quality modeling.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking Contrastive Targets in Radiology Language-Image Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24016)
*Yunhe Gao,Ashwin Kumar,Jiaming Liu,Chong Wang,Maya Varma,Fuying Wang,Stefania L. Moroianu,Louisa Fay,Sergios Gatidis,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP式图文对比预训练在放射影像中存在两个系统性失效（零样本性能先升后降、大batch训练反而学不好），根源在于"配对身份"目标的结构性偏置，而非噪声；作者构建合成测试床验证并用目标误设理论解释，提出无架构改动的兼容性修正CorLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射数据中每张图像仅配一份报告、批内其余报告一律视作负样本，但许多"未配对"报告与图像共享相同临床发现，导致CLIP的配对身份目标被系统性偏置。现有对比学习理论既无法解释"零样本性能在损失持续下降时先达峰后劣化"，也无法解释"训练无法从大batch中获益"这两个失效。

Method: 1) 提出假设：失效是结构性的而非放射领域特有；2) 构建CLEVR-Radiology合成测试床，仅保留"重叠事实"结构，用于验证该结构足以复现两个失效；3) 建立目标误设（target-misspecification）理论，用单一确定性梯度偏置解释两个失效，且该偏置随batch size放大；4) 据此提出最小化修正CorLIP：当文本的临床断言与图像一致即视为正样本，不改变模型架构。

Result: CLEVR-Radiology成功仅凭重叠事实结构复现两个失效；理论给出统一的梯度偏置解释。CorLIP在基准上超越原方法：基本消除峰值后性能劣化、使大batch训练由崩溃转为成功、显著放大数据量增加带来的收益，且是唯一在B=2048下仍稳定的配置。

Conclusion: CLIP在放射数据上的失效源于配对身份目标的结构性误设，可由随batch增大的确定性梯度偏置统一刻画；基于兼容性判断的轻量目标修正CorLIP即可消除该偏置，并从胸部X光迁移到3D CT模态，具有跨模态通用性。

Abstract: CLIP-style contrastive image-text pretraining is widely used in radiology data, where each image is paired with one report and every other report in the batch is treated as a negative. Under this recipe, two failures appear, neither of which is systematically addressed by existing contrastive learning theory: zero-shot evaluation peaks then degrades while training loss keeps decreasing, and training fails to learn from large batches. We hypothesize the cause is structural rather than radiology-specific: many unpaired reports share clinical findings with a given image, so CLIP's paired-identity target is systematically biased, not merely noisy. To test this, we construct CLEVR-Radiology, a synthetic testbed that preserves only this overlapping-fact structure and reproduces both failures. With evidence that the structure is sufficient to reproduce the failures, we develop a target-misspecification theory that explains both failures from a single deterministic gradient bias amplified by batch size, and use it to motivate a minimal compatibility-based correction, CorLIP: text is treated as positive whenever its clinical assertions agree with the image, with no architecture changes. Beyond benchmark improvements, CorLIP largely removes the post-peak degradation, makes large-batch training succeed where it previously collapsed, substantially amplifies the gains from more data, and is the only configuration that remains stable up to B=2048. The correction transfers from chest X-ray to 3D CT modalities.

</details>


### [81] [Semi-Supervised GAN-Based Segmentation of CTPA Scans for Pulmonary Emboli Boundary Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24018)
*Maria Thoma,Michalis Savelonas,Foteini Malli,Dimitris Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于GAN的半监督分割方法，利用大量无标注CTPA扫描检测肺栓塞，Dice达88.75%、HD95为1.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PE发病率与死亡率高，延迟诊断可致命；CTPA为临床金标准但人工判读耗时且易出错，且标注数据有限，需借助大量无标注扫描提升分割性能。

Method: 采用生成对抗网络的半监督分割框架，并用多项损失协同：Dice损失与交叉熵损失应对CTPA高度类别不平衡；特征匹配损失促使生成器输出与真值难以区分、解剖上合理的分割图；自训练损失强化对高置信伪标签样本的学习。

Result: 在公开数据集上，即使标注CTPA扫描数量有限仍保持准确分割，Dice系数88.75%、HD95为1.74，优于全监督基线。

Conclusion: 半监督GAN方法可有效利用无标注CTPA数据，缓解标注稀缺问题，并在PE分割任务上超越全监督基线。

Abstract: Pulmonary embolism (PE) is a pulmonary vascular disease associated with high morbidity and mortality, where delayed diagnosis can be fatal. While computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA) is the clinical gold standard for PE detection, interpreting these scans manually is time-consuming and error-prone. In this work, we propose a semi-supervised segmentation method, employing generative adversarial networks (GANs) to leverage large pools of unlabeled CTPA scans. The proposed method addresses more effectively the challenges of CTPA image segmentation than previous methods by employing the dice loss and the cross-entropy loss to tackle the highly imbalanced nature of CTPA scans, the feature matching loss to encourage the generator to produce anatomically plausible segmentation maps that are indistinguishable from ground truth, as well as the self-training loss to reinforce learning from highly-confident pseudo-labeled samples. The experimental evaluation on a publicly available dataset shows that the proposed method maintains accurate segmentation, despite a limited number of labeled CTPA scans. with a Dice coefficient of 88.75% and a 95th percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) of 1.74, outperforming fully supervised baselines.

</details>


### [82] [STAR++: A Physical-Aware Benchmark and Framework for Astronomical Super-Resolution across Multiple Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24023)
*Guohang Zhuang,Jiaze Li,Kuo-cheng Wu,Hao Du,Jinyang Huang,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模基准STAR++（约170万通量一致图像对、36个滤波器）与通量不变超分模型FISR++，实现跨分辨率通量保真的天文图像超分并可泛化到未见滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文超分（ASR）需为天体物理分析重建高分辨率星场，但存在两方面挑战：数据层面缺乏支持场级ASR（尤其在未见滤波器上）的大规模基准；方法层面现有ASR方法难以在跨空间分辨率时保持恒星通量，且需重建星点位置与轮廓等高频成分，问题比自然图像超分更病态。

Method: 构建STAR++基准：基于HST观测，采用通量保持的数据生成流程保证跨分辨率光度一致性，含约170万通量一致图像对、36个滤波器，并提出通量误差（FE）指标从物理角度评估。方法上提出FISR++：在通量保持模块基础上引入滤波器感知适配器（FAA）利用滤波器元数据适配不同光谱与光学条件的表征，以及空间歧义解耦损失（SADL），将空间歧义建模为像素位移不确定性，缓解条件均值重建的过度平滑。

Result: 大量实验表明FISR++在重建质量与通量精度上持续优于现有ASR方法，并能泛化到未见滤波器。

Conclusion: STAR++与FISR++共同构成光度保真的天文超分框架，验证了通量一致数据构建、滤波器感知建模与歧义解耦对ASR的有效性。

Abstract: Astronomical super-resolution (ASR) aims to reconstruct high-resolution star fields from low-resolution telescope observations across multiple filters for astrophysical analysis. However, developing a physics-aware ASR framework faces two challenges: 1) Data view: no large-scale benchmark supports field-level ASR, especially on unseen filters. 2) Method view: Existing ASR methods often fail to preserve stellar flux across spatial resolutions. Moreover, ASR requires reconstructing high-frequency components, including accurate star locations and profiles, making it more ill-posed than natural-image SR. To tackle these problems, we introduce STAR++, a large-scale benchmark comprising approximately 1.7 million flux-consistent image pairs across 36 filters, derived from Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations. STAR++ uses a flux-preserving data generation pipeline to ensure photometric consistency across resolutions and provides the Flux Error (FE) metric to evaluate ASR models from a physical perspective. To address method-level challenges, we propose a Flux-Invariant Super-Resolution Plus (FISR++) model that generalizes to unseen filters while accurately reconstructing flux-consistent high-resolution images. Beyond the flux-preserving modules, FISR++ introduces two novel components. Specifically, a Filter-Aware Adapter (FAA) incorporates filter metadata to adapt image representations to varying spectral and optical conditions. In addition, a Spatial Ambiguity Disentangle Loss (SADL) models spatial ambiguity as pixel-shift uncertainty, mitigating the smoothing of learned conditional mean reconstructions caused by this ambiguity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FISR++ consistently outperforms existing ASR methods in reconstruction quality and flux accuracy, while generalizing to unseen filters, highlighting its effectiveness for photometrically faithful ASR.

</details>


### [83] [Semantic-aware Contrasting with Explicitly Disentangled Representations for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24038)
*Jiaquan Yu,Jingyi Xie,Zeyu Zhang,Yunhang Qian,Xiaobin Hu,Jiawei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义对齐的AI生成视频检测数据集SAVGE（236万视频）及模型无关训练框架EDSC，通过显式语义-伪影解耦与组内对比学习抑制语义捷径，提升检测鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGV检测基准中真实与伪造视频来自不同语义分布，检测器易依赖对象、场景、动作等虚假相关而非生成模型内在伪影，导致性能虚高、泛化差。

Method: 构建SAVGE：以真实视频配对字幕作为文生视频提示，使真/假样本按语义分组，提供组级对应与文本语义锚点。提出EDSC：模型无关的两阶段训练框架，先提取视觉语义特征并与文本表示对齐，再在伪影分支中显式抑制语义信息，并在每个语义组内进行真假对比，结合字幕引导语义监督与组内对比学习。

Result: 在SAVGE与GenVideo上，EDSC提升多数骨干-指标组合，在困难评测设置下更鲁棒，并增强跨数据集泛化。

Conclusion: 语义对齐对AIGV检测至关重要，显式语义-伪影解耦是学习鲁棒检测器的有效策略。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated videos (AIGVs) has raised critical concerns about content authenticity. Existing benchmarks suffer from a fundamental limitation: real and fake videos are often collected or generated from different semantic distributions. As a result, detectors may rely on spurious correlations involving objects, scenes, or actions rather than intrinsic artifacts introduced by generative models. To this end, we introduce SAVGE, a large-scale semantically aligned AIGV detection dataset comprising 2,369,822 videos, including 424,115 real videos and 1,945,707 generated videos. SAVGE organizes samples into real-fake semantic groups by using captions paired with real videos as prompts for text-to-video generation, thereby providing both group-level correspondence and textual semantic anchors. Based on SAVGE, we further propose Explicit Disentangling and Semantic-aware Contrasting (EDSC), a model-agnostic training framework for learning artifact-focused representations with reduced semantic interference. EDSC first extracts and aligns visual semantic features with textual representations, and then explicitly suppresses semantic information in the artifact branch while contrasting real and fake samples within each semantic group. By integrating caption-guided semantic supervision with intra-group contrastive learning in a two-phase optimization strategy, EDSC encourages detectors to capture generation-related cues under matched semantic conditions. Extensive experiments on SAVGE and GenVideo demonstrate that EDSC improves most evaluated backbone-metric combinations, enhances robustness under challenging evaluation settings, and strengthens cross-dataset generalization. These results highlight the importance of semantic alignment in AIGV detection and validate explicit semantic-artifact disentanglement as an effective strategy for robust detector learning.

</details>


### [84] [CURVTON-205K: A Mask-Free Difficulty-Stratified Synthetic Dataset for Robust and Scalable Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24042)
*Dipan Bartaula,Anuj Shrestha,Ankit Belbase,Anish Bishwakarma,Saroj Poudel,Samip Ghimire,Shashank Bhatta,Eric Arazo,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展数据生成流水线，结合编辑/生成模型与VLM引导，无需解析掩码或稠密姿态标注即可合成三元组，构建205K合成数据集CURVTON-205K，按难度分级，覆盖长尾服饰与场景，提升多种VTON架构的保真度与跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D虚拟试衣研究多聚焦架构创新，但遮挡处理不准、姿态不受约束、 underrepresented 服饰与外观覆盖不足等失效模式，实际与数据集设计及长尾数据分布紧密耦合。

Method: 搭建规模化数据生成流水线，协同专用编辑模型与生成模型，并以视觉语言模型（VLM）引导，合成"源人物—目标服饰—试穿结果"三元组，无需人体解析掩码与稠密姿态标注；据此构建205K三元组数据集，显式扩展服饰、人物外观、姿态、遮挡与视觉条件覆盖，并划分为三个难度层级。

Result: 对数据集多样性进行分析，并在四种VTON架构上验证其可提升试穿保真度，同时展现跨数据集泛化能力；难度分层支持难度感知的受控评测与实验。

Conclusion: 数据集设计是VTON鲁棒性的关键变量；所提数据流水线与CURVTON-205K为长尾覆盖、难度分层评测及泛化研究提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 2D virtual try-on (VTON) have largely focused on architectural innovation, yet robust performance in diverse real-world conditions remains challenging. We argue that failure modes in VTON, such as inaccurate occlusion handling, unconstrained poses, and poor coverage of under-represented garments and appearance factors, are tightly coupled to dataset design choices and long-tail data regimes. We present a scalable data generation pipeline that coordinates specialized editing and generation models with vision-language model (VLM) guidance to synthesize triplets without requiring parsing masks or dense-pose annotations. Using this pipeline, we create CURVTON-205K, a curated synthetic dataset of 205K triplets: source person, target garment, and corresponding try-on image. CURVTON-205K is explicitly designed to broaden coverage across garments, person appearance, poses, occlusions, and visual conditions. Additionally, CURVTON-205K is organized into three difficulty tiers, enabling controlled evaluation under varying levels of difficulty and supporting difficulty-aware experimentation. We analyze the dataset diversity and demonstrate its potential to improve try-on fidelity across four VTON architectures and its cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [85] [R$^{3}$Sieve: Suppressing Unconditional Information from Attention to Answers for High-Resolution VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24060)
*Yifan Cao,Yawen Zhang,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang,Shangshu Yu,Xianbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架R³Sieve，通过三阶段反事实参考抑制干扰，提升高分辨率VQA中小目标证据的有效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率VQA需识别与问题相关的小目标并据其作答，要求细粒度感知。以往方法侧重获取更好的图像裁剪证据，但仅获取证据不够，如何有效利用证据同样关键。作者认为小目标证据会被无条件信息遮蔽——即便其所依赖的条件被移除，这些信息仍在后续处理中可见，从而妨碍小目标证据的有效利用。

Method: R³Sieve（Residual-Region-Response Sieve）将推理分为定位、裁剪、作答三阶段，并在每阶段引入不同的反事实参考以抑制干扰：定位阶段用共享子空间消除器（SSC），借助通用图像描述构建反事实参考，获得问题相关注意力图；裁剪阶段用独特贡献定位器（UCS），由视觉与非视觉特征构建反事实参考，识别具区分性、互补性的裁剪区域；作答阶段用内容对齐投票器（CAV），构建无图像分支以校准答案响应。整体为免训练框架。

Result: 在三个高分辨率VQA基准上，R³Sieve在多种设置下总体准确率均优于对比方法，相比基础模型最高提升21.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过逐阶段反事实参考抑制遮蔽小目标证据的无条件信息，可在不训练的情况下显著提升高分辨率VQA的细粒度感知与作答准确率。

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering (VQA) requires models to identify question-relevant small objects in high-resolution images and select the correct answer based on what they recognize, demanding fine-grained perception. Previous methods focus on obtaining better visual evidence (image crops), but obtaining it alone is insufficient, using it effectively is equally crucial. Visual evidence should therefore be utilized as effectively as possible throughout inference. We argue that small-target evidence is obscured by unconditional information that remains observable in subsequent processing even after the condition on which it should depend is removed. Such information hinders the effective use of small-target evidence. We propose R$^{3}$Sieve (Residual-Region-Response Sieve), a training-free framework. We divide inference into three stages---localization, cropping, and answering---and introduce distinct counterfactual references to suppress interference at each stage. During localization, the Shared-Subspace Canceller (SSC) uses a generic image description to construct a counterfactual reference and obtain question-relevant attention maps. During cropping, the Unique-Contribution Spotter (UCS) constructs counterfactual references from visual and non-visual features to identify distinctive, complementary crop regions. During answering, the Content-Aligned Voter (CAV) constructs an image-free branch to calibrate answer responses. Experiments on three high-resolution VQA benchmarks show that R$^{3}$Sieve achieves the highest overall accuracy among compared methods across diverse settings, improving base models by up to 21.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [86] [Benchmarking Reasoning Reliability,Prior_Evidence Contrast,and Spectral Discrimination in Remote-Sensing Geological Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24068)
*Jun Wang,Fengpeng Li,Wei Han,Yuxuan Li,Tianjin Huang,Jining Yan,Sheng Wang,Kan Sun,Lizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出高光谱地质解释诊断基准 HyGeoBench（1万题、三维度、十类任务）并发现多模态大模型在细粒度识别与证据支撑解释上的缺陷，进而提出自适应调用多种工具增强的 HyGeoAgent 框架，实验显示相对基座模型在各诊断维度上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠岩性解释需要基于视觉证据的地质识别与推理，而多模态大模型在遥感地质解释中的可靠性尚不明确，缺乏针对推理错误、地质先验与视觉证据冲突、跨波段不一致等问题的系统诊断手段。

Method: 构建基于高光谱影像的 HyGeoBench 诊断基准（10,000 题，覆盖推理可靠性、先验-证据对比、光谱可分性三个维度、十种任务类型，含选择题与开放题），并融合卫星影像、地质标注与经核验的证据；同时提出工具增强框架 HyGeoAgent，通过自适应工具调用整合区域观测、文本知识检索、同波段图像检索与原生 PCA 分析。

Result: 评测揭示现有多模态大模型在细粒度地质识别与证据支撑解释方面存在明显局限；HyGeoAgent 在对应基座模型上于各诊断维度均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: HyGeoBench 提供了可诊断、多维度的地质解释评测体系，HyGeoAgent 表明自适应工具增强是提升多模态模型地质图像解释可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Reliable lithology interpretation requires accurate geological recognition and reasoning grounded in visual evidence. Although Multimodal Large Language Models show promise in remote sensing, their reliability in geological interpretation remains unclear. We introduce HyGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 10,000 questions based on hyperspectral imagery across three dimensions: reasoning reliability, prior_evidence contrast, and spectral discrimination. It covers ten task types, with both multiple-choice and open-ended response formats. Integrating hyperspectral satellite imagery with geological annotations and verified evidence, HyGeoBench examines reasoning errors, conflicts between geological priors and visual evidence, and cross-band inconsistencies. Evaluations reveal limitations in fine-grained recognition and evidence-grounded interpretation. To address these challenges, we propose HyGeoAgent, a tool-augmented framework integrating region observation, text knowledge retrieval, same-band image retrieval, and native PCA analysis through adaptive tool use. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over the corresponding base models, improving geological image interpretation across diagnostic dimensions.

</details>


### [87] [AeroRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24077)
*Shiwei Wu,Yuwen Pan,Zhi Li,Yaowen Li,Yan Zhou,Xuemiao Xu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AeroRSI递归自我改进框架，让无人机VLA策略从自身失败中学习"修复动作"，三轮迭代将2B监督策略成功率从46.4%提升至67.5%、碰撞减半，无需额外人工标注或推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型使无人机能跟随自然语言指令，但在闭环飞行中仍频繁失败。纠正失败需要"修复"动作——即在策略自身到达的状态上避免失败的动作，而这类数据人类飞行员无法大规模标注，规则式规划器也无法可靠提供。

Method: 核心洞察：失败的策略应通过"从修复动作继续飞行"来评判修复的好坏。由此，采样动作乃至直接模仿会损害策略的规则式建议，都可成为可靠监督。由于一个回合在首次失败处终止，后续失败被掩盖，AeroRSI每轮重新生成监督：锚定当前策略的失败状态，用其后续飞行验证修复，并在保持其他区域行为的前提下进行蒸馏。

Result: 在TravelUAV上，三轮迭代使2B参数监督策略成功率由46.4%升至67.5%，碰撞率减半；在相同初始化下较此前最优方法高10.6个百分点，且无额外人工标注与推理成本。

Conclusion: 基于"续飞验证"的递归自我改进可高效转化为无人机闭环导航性能提升，为VLA策略的失败驱动自迭代提供可扩展范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural-language instructions, yet still fail frequently in closed-loop flight. Correcting these failures requires repairs, i.e., actions that avoid them at the states the policy itself reaches, which human pilots cannot label at scale and rule-based planners cannot supply reliably. In this paper, we present AeroRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework in which an aerial navigation policy iteratively learns from its own failures. Our key insight is that a failing policy should judge its own repairs by flying on from them. Judged in this way, its sampled actions and even rule-based proposals that hurt it when imitated directly become reliable supervision. Moreover, an episode terminates at its first failure, concealing subsequent failures until the earlier ones are repaired. AeroRSI thus renews its supervision in every round, anchoring the failures of the current policy, verifying repairs by its continuation, and distilling them while keeping its behavior elsewhere. On TravelUAV, three rounds raise the success rate of a supervised 2B-parameter policy from 46.4% to 67.5% and reduce collisions by half, surpassing the previous state of the art by 10.6 points from the same initialization without extra human labels or inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/starsholic/AeroRSI.

</details>


### [88] [APS: Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24079)
*Junxiang Wen,Yinxi Zhao,Jiawei Chen,Jingying Hu,Lijun Wang,Zhen Qin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的自适应分区帧选择框架 APS，将时间分区建模为复杂度正则化拟合问题并用动态规划联合优化边界与局部模型，据查询相关性分配帧预算，在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上稳定提升多种 LVLM 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解受限于大量视觉输入带来的高昂计算成本；现有基于区域的帧选择方法依赖固定或预定义的时间划分，易产生次优时间区域，进而影响后续预算分配与帧选择效果。

Method: APS 为免训练框架：将时间分区形式化为复杂度正则化拟合问题，通过动态规划同时优化分区边界与局部模型，使分区自适应于查询-帧相关性的局部变化；再依据查询相关性与时间跨度对自适应分区进行帧预算分配，在有限视觉预算下选取信息量高的帧。

Result: 在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上，APS 在多种 LVLM 主干上均一致提升性能，相对均匀采样的平均增益在对比方法中最高；进一步分析验证了自适应分区的有效性，并显示其在模型规模、相似度编码器与先进 LVLM 上具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 自适应时间分区优于固定分区，可作为即插即用、免训练的帧选择方案提升长视频理解效率与效果。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have significantly improved video understanding capabilities, yet processing long videos remains challenging due to the substantial computational cost of extensive visual inputs. Frame selection offers an efficient alternative, but many existing region-based methods rely on fixed or predefined temporal partitions, which may result in suboptimal temporal regions, compromising subsequent budget allocation and frame selection. In this work, we propose Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection (APS), a training-free framework that adapts temporal partitions to local variations in query-frame relevance. Specifically, APS formulates temporal partitioning as a complexity-regularized fitting problem, jointly optimizing partition boundaries and local models through dynamic programming. The resulting adaptive temporal partitions guide frame budget allocation based on query relevance and temporal extent, enabling informative frame selection under limited visual budgets. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench demonstrate that APS consistently improves performance across diverse LVLM backbones, achieving the highest average gains over uniform sampling among competing methods. Further analyses validate the effectiveness of adaptive partitioning and demonstrate its generalizability across model scales, similarity encoders, and advanced LVLMs.

</details>


### [89] [CausalDrive: Causal Decomposition for Data-Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24080)
*Xiaobao Wei,Jingxian Wu,Lirong Yang,Qing Han,Yuan Gao,Ziwei Yang,Shuai Yang,Weiwei Liu,Chang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CausalDrive 因果分解框架，将专家轨迹拆分为"意图"（去哪里）与"风格"（怎么去），分别用确定性意图规划器和基于扩散的概率风格策略建模，提升端到端驾驶的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶中，直接模仿专家轨迹会把 nominal intention（由导航指令、地图拓扑、自车状态决定的目标位置）与多模态 driving style（执行方式）纠缠在同一目标中；两者不同组合可产生相似轨迹，导致因果贡献被混淆，策略需从单一纠缠标签中同时学两者，限制了对专家数据的利用效率。

Method: 1) 由导航命令、地图拓扑与自车状态构造反事实 nominal 轨迹，剥离风格相关效应，为 Nominal Intention Planner 提供不依赖专家轨迹的独立监督；2) 以预测的 nominal 意图与周围 agent 为条件，用基于扩散的 Style Execution Policy 将多样风格建模为"专家轨迹与 nominal 轨迹残差"上的多模态分布；3) 该因果结构化监督减少常规场景中共享 nominal 行为的冗余学习。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 上取得 91.2 PDMS，在 NAVSIM v2 上取得 89.5 EPDMS，匹配或超越现有 SOTA；在有限专家数据条件下仍表现良好，并在挑战性交互场景中具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 将意图与风格进行因果解耦，并以反事实 nominal 轨迹提供独立监督、以扩散模型建模风格残差分布，可有效提升专家示范利用率、数据效率与交互场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Efficient use of expert demonstrations is essential for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, directly imitating expert trajectories couples nominal intention, which specifies where the vehicle should go, with multimodal driving styles, which determine how that intention is executed. Different combinations of these factors can yield similar expert trajectories, obscuring their respective causal contributions and forcing the policy to learn both from a single entangled target. This ambiguity limits how effectively the policy can exploit expert demonstrations. We propose CausalDrive, a causal decomposition framework for data-efficient driving policy learning that separates deterministic nominal intention prediction from probabilistic driving style modeling. Specifically, we construct counterfactual nominal trajectories from navigation command, map topology, and ego state by removing style dependent effects, providing a Nominal Intention Planner with supervision independent of expert trajectories. Conditioned on the predicted nominal intention and surrounding agents, a diffusion-based Style Execution Policy models diverse driving styles as a multimodal distribution over residuals between nominal and expert trajectories. This causally structured supervision reduces redundant learning of shared nominal behavior in routine scenarios, directing expert supervision toward informative execution variations and improving data utilization. CausalDrive achieves 91.2 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.5 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art performance. Further experiments demonstrate strong performance with limited expert data and clear advantages in challenging interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Learning Where and How Much to Sample: Volume-Adaptive k-Space Acquisition for Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24081)
*Reyhaneh Neshatavar,Mohsen Yavartanoo,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出体数据自适应的k空间采样框架，联合学习在整卷MRI中"采哪里"和"采多少"，仅用ACS数据实现切片间预算分配与信息性k空间行选择，并配合敏感度感知的跨切片重建网络，在膝/脑MRI多对比度、多加速倍数下验证了自适应采样与重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MRI通过k空间欠采样缩短采集时间，但传统方法依赖预定义采样模式和统一的逐切片采样预算，忽略了不同切片间的解剖差异与层间相关性，导致采样效率和信息利用率不足。

Method: 1）体数据自适应k空间采集框架：仅使用已采集的ACS（自动校准信号）测量，利用整卷解剖信息，联合决定各切片的采样预算分配与具体k空间行的选择；2）可学习的联合切片—频率细化模块（joint slice–frequency refinement），协调各切片采样决策，同时严格保持体数据级别的总采集预算不变；3）敏感度感知的同步跨切片重建网络（sensitivity-aware synchronous cross-slice reconstruction）：从ACS估计线圈敏感度，渐进式细化相邻切片重建，并施加硬性k空间数据一致性约束；4）两网络基于重建监督联合优化，推理阶段无需全采样k空间。

Result: 在膝关节与脑部MRI的多组对比度与多种加速倍数实验中，采集框架表现出符合解剖差异的自适应采样行为，并对其重建性能进行了评估（摘要未给出具体定量指标）。

Conclusion: 将采样决策从固定模式/均匀预算扩展为体数据级别的联合自适应优化，可在总预算约束下兼顾层间差异与层间关联，配合跨切片敏感度感知重建，为加速MRI提供了一种端到端联合优化的新范式。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is an essential medical imaging technique, but its high costs and long acquisition times can limit accessibility. Accelerated MRI reduces acquisition time through k-space undersampling, yet conventional methods often rely on predefined sampling patterns and uniform slice budgets, overlooking anatomical variations and inter-slice relationships. In this paper, we propose a volume-adaptive k-space acquisition framework that jointly learns where and how much to sample across an entire MRI volume. Using only acquired auto-calibration signal (ACS) measurements, our method exploits volume-wide anatomical information to adaptively allocate sampling budgets across slices while identifying informative k-space lines. A learned joint slice--frequency refinement module coordinates sampling decisions while preserving an exact total volume-level acquisition budget. To reconstruct the acquired measurements, we design a sensitivity-aware synchronous cross-slice reconstruction network that estimates coil sensitivities from ACS measurements and progressively refines neighboring-slice reconstructions while enforcing hard k-space data consistency. Both networks are jointly optimized using reconstruction-based supervision without requiring fully sampled k-space at inference. Experiments on knee and brain MRI across multiple contrasts and acceleration factors demonstrate the adaptive behavior of our acquisition framework and evaluate its reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [91] [From Restoration to Redrawing: Revitalizing Ancient Chinese Characters by SVG Code Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24084)
*Weiguang Zhang,Linhua Dong,Bin Dong,Shanshan Jiang,Maizhen Ning,Xiaoqiang Kang,Kun Zhang,Hao Xu,Xiaowei Huang,Wei Wang,Kaizhu Huang,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDraw，将汉代简牍残损字的修复重构为SVG代码生成与编辑，用两阶段视觉语言模型先忠实矢量化可观测笔画、再以骨架链式推理与结构感知强化学习补绘严重残缺笔画，并构建多层数据合成管线生成1.2M样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉简墨迹残损、笔画不完整；现有图像到图像修复方法可能改写已观测笔画，且对笔画几何形状控制有限，与专家手工摹绘的实践不符。

Method: 两阶段VLM：阶段一将可观测笔画几何忠实矢量化，或对轻度退化笔画做保守描摹；阶段二在骨架链式思维引导与结构感知奖励的强化学习下，先预测骨架再生成轮廓，通过追加路径补绘严重残缺笔画，同时保持已有SVG路径不变。数据方面构建解耦墨迹、材质与退化的多层合成管线，生成1.2M样本，并从320个标注真实字形派生最多6,400个适配对。

Result: 实验显示合成数据上的修复性能，以及对真实汉简字形的适应能力。

Conclusion: 以矢量代码生成替代像素级修复，可在不改动已观测笔画的前提下补全字形，为汉简文字修复提供可控、可编辑的新范式。

Abstract: Hanjian manuscripts written on Han Dynasty bamboo and wooden slips are invaluable heritage of ancient Chinese writing but suffer from damaged ink and incomplete strokes. Existing image-to-image restoration methods may alter observed strokes and offer limited control over stroke geometry. Inspired by experts' manual redrawing practices, we address these issues by introducing \textbf{ReDraw}, a two-stage vision-language model(VLM) that reformulates character restoration as Scalable Vector Graphics(SVG) code generation and editing. Stage one targets faithful vectorization of observed strokes geometry or conservative tracing of mildly degraded strokes. Guided by skeleton chain-of-thought reasoning and reinforcement learning with structure-aware rewards, stage two predicts skeletons before contours to redraw severely incomplete strokes by appending paths while keeping existing SVG paths unchanged. To support training, we develop a multi-layer data synthesis pipeline that disentangles ink, material, and degradation to generate 1.2M samples, and derive up to 6,400 adaptation pairs from 320 annotated real glyphs. Experiments demonstrate synthetic restoration performance and adaptability to real Hanjian glyphs. Code and demo will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [92] [Object Pose: The Link between Weak Perspective, Paraperspective, and Full Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24095)
*Radu Horaud,Fadi Dornaika,Bart Lamiroy,Stephane Christy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对DeMenthon与Davis的迭代位姿估计方法给出代数推导，指出其属于透视相机模型的零阶（弱透视）与一阶（平行透视）近似方法类，提出收敛性更优的迭代平行透视位姿计算方法，并分析标定误差敏感性与最优实验布置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeMenthon与Davis提出的方法仅以弱透视模型迭代逼近透视位姿，缺乏代数推导与系统性理论归类，其收敛性、旋转正交约束处理及对相机标定误差的敏感性均未被充分分析。

Method: 对DeMenthon-Davis方法作代数推导，将其纳入透视模型的零阶（弱透视）与一阶（平行透视）近似统一框架；详细给出针对非共面与共面物点的迭代平行透视位姿计算方法；分析各类方法的收敛性；引入处理旋转矩阵正交约束的简便方式；并分析对标定误差的敏感性以确定最优实验布置。

Result: 迭代平行透视法相比迭代弱透视法具有更好的收敛性质；给出正交约束的简便处理方案；明确相对于标定不确定性的最优实验布置；并与非线性优化方法的结果进行了对比。

Conclusion: 弱透视迭代法可推广为一类基于透视模型零阶/一阶近似的位姿估计方法，其中平行透视迭代法在收敛性上更优，配合正交约束处理与合理实验布置可获得与非线性优化相当的精度。

Abstract: Recently, DeMenthon and Davis [4,5] proposed a method for determining the pose of a 3-D object with respect to a camera from 3-D to 2-D point correspondences. The method consists of iteratively improving the pose computed with a weak perspective camera model to converge, at the limit, to a pose estimation computed with a perspective camera model. In this paper we give an algebraic derivation of DeMenthon and Davis' method and we show that it belongs to a larger class of methods where the perspective camera model is approximated either at zero order (weak perspective) or first order (paraperspective). We describe in detail an iterative paraperspective pose computation method for both non coplanar and coplanar object points. We analyse the convergence of these methods and we conclude that the iterative paraperspective method (proposed in this paper) has better convergence properties than the iterative weak perspective method. We introduce a simple way of taking into account the orthogonality constraint associated with the rotation matrix. We analyse the sensitivity to camera calibration errors and we define the optimal experimental setup with respect to imprecise camera calibration. We compare the results obtained with this method and with a non-linear optimization method.

</details>


### [93] [RoughCut-Bench: A Benchmark for Automatic Video Rough-Cut with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24098)
*Ruisi Zhao,You Li,Sihan Chen,Aosong Cheng,Chixiang Ma,Sen Pei,Zhen Yang,Hehe Fan,Dongliang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoughCut-Bench，首个面向"视频粗剪"的基准，覆盖视觉驱动（高光检测与选择、素材排序、用户需求遵循）与语音驱动（冗余词去除、表达去重、低价值内容裁剪）两类场景，并提供由 LVLM 驱动的智能体工作流把原始素材转换为结构化剪辑方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频粗剪是媒体生产流程的核心环节，但自动粗剪研究不足：一方面缺少统一评测基准，另一方面缺少可产出可执行剪辑方案（cut plan）的成熟流程；现有 LVLM 虽在视频任务上进展明显，却未被系统评估于该任务。

Method: 构建 RoughCut-Bench：视觉设置含 101 个案例、831 段原始视频、1,476 条高光标注；语音设置含 84 个案例，每个含带清洗区间标注的 ASR 转录。评测将剪辑方案与标注对比，并在视觉设置中引入与人类对齐的智能体评估器评判渲染成片质量。此外提出智能体工作流，引导 LVLM 生成结构化剪辑方案。

Result: 实验表明，当前 LVLM 在生成与专家对齐的剪辑方案方面仍存在明显局限，性能不足。

Conclusion: 自动视频粗剪缺乏基准与成熟流程的问题由 RoughCut-Bench 部分填补；基准、代码与提示词已公开，揭示 LVLM 在该任务上的能力缺口，为后续研究提供评测基础。

Abstract: Video rough-cut is a core stage in media production workflows, organizing raw media materials into an initial video cut. Despite recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) for video-related tasks, automatic rough-cut remains underexplored, without a comprehensive benchmark for evaluation or mature workflows for producing executable cut plans. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce RoughCut-Bench, which covers two practical settings: (1) a visually driven setting over video cases, covering highlight detection and selection, source-video ordering, and adherence to user requirements when provided; and (2) a speech-driven setting over automatic speech recognition (ASR) transcripts, covering filler removal, expression deduplication, and low-value content trimming. Across these settings, the benchmark contains 101 visually driven cases, including 831 raw videos with 1,476 highlight annotations. It also includes 84 speech-driven cases, each consisting of an ASR transcript with annotated cleanup spans. The evaluation compares cut plans with these annotations in both settings and additionally uses a human-aligned agentic evaluator to assess rendered-cut quality in the visual setting. To apply LVLMs to automatic rough-cut, we further propose agentic workflows that guide them to produce structured cut plans from input materials. Experiments show that current LVLMs remain limited in producing expert-aligned cut plans. Our benchmark, code, and prompts are released at https://whalesong-zrs.github.io/RoughCut-Bench-Project-Page.

</details>


### [94] [IDEA: Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit for Inference-Time Backdoor Detection in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24102)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Sizai Hou,Jian Yang,Ge Sun,Junyu Luo,Wenkai Zhang,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 IDEA（Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit），针对开源权重 GUI 智能体在推理阶段逐步检测后门激活：利用“意图—证据主导性反转”这一签名，结合指令反事实探测与区域因果审计，并用门控保形评分提供有限样本误报保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下载的开源权重 GUI 智能体可能被植入视觉触发后门——在干净数据上通过验收测试，仅在屏幕出现特定视觉触发时行为异常，形成供应链风险；而现有样本级后门检测器面向图像分类器与 CLIP 类编码器，缺乏面向 GUI 智能体的步骤级检测方法。

Method: 提出概念签名“意图—证据主导性反转”：干净步骤中指令主导动作，被未引用屏幕区域劫持时指令失去对决策状态的控制。据此设计 IDEA：(1) 意图反事实探测（ICP），度量被重定向指令对决策状态的位移；(2) 区域因果审计（RCA），度量消融候选区域对决策状态的位移；(3) 门控保形评分，在同等距离于干净流形的条件下比较两种位移，提供有限样本误报率保证。无需触发知识或攻击数据。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上跨五类攻击家族的实验表明：IDEA 在受控误报率下稳定检出后门激活，优于现有 SOTA 检测方法，每次报警均可定位触发区域，且推理开销适中。

Conclusion: IDEA 将后门检测从样本级扩展到 GUI 智能体的步骤级推理阶段，以因果归因的方式实现无触发先验的检测与区域定位，为开源权重智能体的部署安全提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy downloaded open-weight GUI agents. A backdoored agent passes acceptance tests on clean data and misbehaves only when a visual trigger appears on screen, turning an unvetted checkpoint into a supply-chain risk. However, existing sample-level detectors target image classifiers and CLIP-style encoders, leaving step-level backdoor detection for GUI agents unaddressed. On clean steps, the instruction drives the action; when an unreferenced screen region hijacks the step, the instruction loses control over the decision state or that region dictates it instead. We term this signature intent--evidence dominance inversion and propose IDEA (Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit) to detect backdoor activation at inference time for each step without trigger knowledge or attack data. Specifically, Intent Counterfactual Probing (ICP) measures how far redirected instructions move the decision state, and Region Causal Audit (RCA) quantifies how far ablating a candidate region moves it. A gated conformal score compares both displacements against clean steps at the same distance from the clean manifold, providing a finite-sample false-positive guarantee. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B across five attack families show that IDEA consistently detects backdoor activation under a controlled false-positive rate and outperforms state-of-the-art detection methods, while localizing the trigger region for every alarm and adding only modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [95] [SCOPE: Single-Cell Omics Prediction from Histology via RNA-Protein Prior Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24104)
*Leyang Tao,Xuan Gong,Luyuan Xie,Arjun Beniwal,Yuqi Tan,Kun-Hsing Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个统一框架，利用RNA与蛋白之间的生物学互补先验增强共享基因级表征，从常规H&E图像预测单细胞分辨率的RNA与蛋白含量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从H&E图像预测RNA与蛋白含量可将空间分子谱分析扩展到既有切片档案，但H&E-空间蛋白组与H&E-空间转录组配对数据集受限于标记数量有限、高质量细胞分辨率数据不足，单模态监督稀缺。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Single-Cell Omics Prediction via RNA-protein prior Enhancement）：引入生物学启发的标志物先验以增强共享基因级表征，通过基因-蛋白对应关系与分子关联实现RNA/蛋白模态间知识迁移，两任务共享富集跨组学生物学知识的骨干网络；其标志物相关性设计支持未见标记预测。

Result: 在覆盖16个器官的测试集上，RNA平均Pearson相关较最强基线提升8.8%；在两个外部蛋白队列上，已见标记平均Pearson相关提升9.7%，未见标记提升11.9%。

Conclusion: RNA与蛋白的生物学关系可作为共享知识弥补单组学监督不足，统一骨干网络与相关性设计能同时提升已见与未见标记的预测，拓展空间分子谱分析范围。

Abstract: Predicting the RNA and protein content of cells from routine H&E images could extend spatial molecular profiling to existing slide archives. However, both H&E-spatial proteomics and H&E-spatial transcriptomics paired datasets are limited by the number of available markers and the insufficient availability of high-quality, cell-resolved data. We present SCOPE (Single-Cell Omics Prediction via RNA-protein prior Enhancement), a unified framework that leverages biologically complementary information between RNA and proteins to address the limited availability of single-omics supervision. Our key insight is that, although RNA and proteins represent distinct molecular modalities, their biological relationships provide valuable shared knowledge that can benefit single-omics prediction tasks. To this end, SCOPE introduces biologically informed biomarker priors to enhance shared gene-level representations, enabling knowledge transfer across RNA and protein modalities through gene-protein correspondence and molecular associations. By exploiting these complementary relationships, SCOPE enables both prediction tasks to benefit from a shared backbone enriched with cross-omics biological knowledge. Furthermore, its biomarker correlation design facilitates the prediction of unseen markers, extending spatially resolved molecular profiling beyond directly supervised targets. Across test datasets spanning 16 organs, SCOPE improves the average RNA Pearson correlation over the strongest baseline by 8.8%. On two external protein cohorts, it improves the average Pearson correlation over the strongest baseline by 9.7% for seen markers and 11.9% for unseen markers.

</details>


### [96] [EVF-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for E-commerce Visual Effects in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24116)
*Run Ling,Ke Cao,Runze He,Jie Gao,Ao Ma,Wei Feng,Zhanjie Zhang,Hao Wang,Jinghe Hu,Guibing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出电商视觉特效基准 EVF-Bench（六维度评测）与配套数据集 EVF-32K（32K+ 图文视频三元组），系统评测代表性视频生成模型及其领域适配变体，发现无任何配置在六个维度上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商视频中的电商视觉特效（EVF）对吸引注意力和传递商品信息至关重要，但通用视觉质量与文本相关性指标无法判断生成视频是否保持商品外观、维持文字可读性、并产生连贯且情境合理的特效，缺乏针对性评测手段。

Method: 构建 EVF-Bench，涵盖通用维度（视觉质量、文本相关性）与 EVF 专属维度（运动、商品一致性、文本稳定性、创意合理性）共六项；并构建 EVF-32K 数据集（逾 32000 条面向电商场景的精选图文视频三元组）以支撑任务对齐的研究与模型适配；基于该套件对代表性视频生成模型及其领域适配变体进行系统评测。

Result: 各模型表现出互补优势，没有任何被评测的配置在全部六个维度上同时领先。

Conclusion: EVF-Bench 与 EVF-32K 共同提供了面向任务的电商视频生成评测框架与数据资源。

Abstract: E-commerce visual effects (EVF) are dynamic, product-centered visual presentations in e-commerce videos, often accompanied by promotional text. They play an important role in attracting viewer attention and conveying product information. However, general measures of visual quality and text relevance alone do not establish whether generated videos preserve product appearance, maintain readable text, and produce coherent, contextually appropriate effects. To address this issue, we introduce EVF-Bench, a dedicated benchmark spanning six evaluation dimensions: visual quality and text relevance as general dimensions, and motion, product consistency, text stability, and creative rationality as EVF-specific dimensions. To complement the benchmark and support task-aligned research and model adaptation, we further construct EVF-32K, a dataset of over 32000 curated image-text-video triples tailored to e-commerce scenarios. Using this suite, we systematically evaluate representative video generation models and their domain-adapted variants. The results reveal complementary strengths, with no evaluated configuration leading all six dimensions. Together, EVF-Bench and EVF-32K provide a task-oriented evaluation framework and a supporting data resource for e-commerce video generation.

</details>


### [97] [How Well Do Generalist CT Foundation Models Differentiate Liver Tumors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24122)
*Yosuke Yamagishi,Tomohiro Kikuchi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估通用CT基础模型（RADAR、Merlin）在肝脏肿瘤鉴别诊断中的能力：零样本表现有限，但仅用冻结特征的线性探针即可显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用CT基础模型能高灵敏度检出多种病灶，但其对已检出病灶进行鉴别诊断的能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 回顾性研究，使用公共单中心多期CT数据集（517例：肝细胞癌、肝内胆管癌、乳腺癌与结直肠癌肝转移、血管瘤）。以零样本方式（沿用模型原有输出）和线性探针方式（冻结图像特征，仅训练线性分类器，分层五折交叉验证重复五次）评估RADAR与Merlin的四分类鉴别性能。

Result: 零样本下，RADAR在门静脉期四分类准确率54.4%（95% CI: 49.9–58.8），κ=0.37，并将31%（129/421）的恶性肿瘤预测为血管瘤；采用四期特征线性探针后准确率升至79.9%（95% CI: 76.6–83.4），κ=0.72，恶性肿瘤误判为血管瘤降至1%（5/421）。Merlin准确率为60.9%（95% CI: 56.9–65.0）。

Conclusion: 通用CT基础模型的零样本肝脏肿瘤鉴别能力有限，但仅经线性探针即可获得实质性提升，提示其特征具备可迁移的鉴别信息。

Abstract: Generalist CT foundation models detect many findings with high performance, but their ability to differentiate detected lesions remains largely unevaluated. This retrospective study evaluated two generalist models, RADAR and Merlin, for differentiating liver tumors using a public single-institution multiphase CT dataset (517 cases: hepatocellular carcinoma, intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma, breast and colorectal cancer liver metastases, and hemangioma). The models were evaluated in a zero-shot setting using their existing outputs and with linear probing, in which only a linear classifier was trained on frozen image features (stratified fivefold cross-validation repeated five times). In the zero-shot setting, RADAR achieved an accuracy of 54.4% (95% CI: 49.9, 58.8) and a $κ$ of 0.37 for four-class differentiation at the portal venous phase and predicted 31% (129 of 421) of malignant tumors as hemangioma. With linear probing of four-phase features, its accuracy improved to 79.9% (95% CI: 76.6, 83.4) with a $κ$ of 0.72, and 1% (five of 421) of malignant tumors were predicted as hemangioma; Merlin achieved an accuracy of 60.9% (95% CI: 56.9, 65.0). Generalist CT foundation models showed limited zero-shot ability to differentiate liver tumors, which improved substantially with linear probing alone.

</details>


### [98] [PatchAdaNet: Unsupervised class incremental learning using local patches and global adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24137)
*Kamel Aouaidjia,Yanchao Duan,Zihui Zhen,Xin Li,Yongfei Lv,Chongsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PatchAdaNet：无监督类增量学习方法。将图像切分为 patch，在外部数据上训练独立自编码器，聚合其编码器层为全局冻结层；用可学习 adapter 重建整图，并以固定大小的全局特征质心（GFC）和 EWC 抑制表征漂移，最后在冻结潜表示上训练线性分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于回放的方法需存储旧任务样本或特征，内存缓冲随任务数增长，且会保留任务特定噪声、降低泛化能力；生物学习则以早期建立、基本稳定的可复用表征实现低干扰的增量学习。

Method: 1）利用图像共享的边缘、角点、形状等结构特征，将每张图像划分为 patch，在外部数据上为每个 patch 训练独立自编码器；2）将所有 patch 编码器层聚合为跨任务泛化且全程固定的全局冻结层；3）在其上学习 adapter 构建全局图像表示并重建整图；4）引入固定大小的 Global Feature Centroid（GFC），为 adapter 输出的滑动平均，每个任务后更新，用于惩罚表征漂移；5）对可学习层施加 EWC 约束重要权重；6）在冻结潜表示与 GFC 上训练线性分类器用于类增量评估。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、miniImageNet 和 5-Datasets 上，于 CNN 与 Vision Transformer 两种设置下进行评估，给出两种架构在相同类增量设定下的对比研究。

Conclusion: PatchAdaNet 以 patch 级自编码器 + 冻结全局层 + adapter 的无数据回放方案实现无监督类增量学习，并通过 GFC 与 EWC 缓解遗忘；实验覆盖 CNN 与 ViT，提供了架构对比视角。

Abstract: Biological learning systems acquire knowledge incrementally through broad, reusable representations established early and kept largely stable, while integrating new experience with minimal interference with previously learned knowledge. Replay-based methods approximate this by storing samples or features from previous tasks and mixing them into the current task. However, memory buffers grow with the number of tasks and may retain task-specific noise that reduces generalization. To address these limitations, we propose PatchAdaNet (Patch Adapter Network), an unsupervised class-incremental learning method for image classification that continually updates feature representations using autoencoders. Since images share structural features such as edges, corners, and shapes, we first divide each image into patches and train independent autoencoders on external data to reconstruct each patch. The layers of all patch encoders are aggregated into global frozen layers that generalize across tasks and remain fixed throughout learning. Learnable adapters then build global image representations from these features to reconstruct the entire image. To prevent feature drift without storing data, we introduce the Global Feature Centroid (GFC), a fixed-size running average of adapter outputs updated after each task to penalizes representation drift. Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is applied to the learnable layers to preserve previously learned features by constraining important weights. Finally, a linear classifier is trained on the frozen latent representation and GFC for class-incremental evaluation. We evaluate PatchAdaNet in both CNN and Vision Transformer settings on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, miniImageNet and 5-Datasets, providing a comparative study of the two architectures under identical class-incremental settings. Code is available.

</details>


### [99] [Inherited Adapter Experts for Replay-Free Domain-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24145)
*Gyutae Oh,Hyunmin Kong,Jitae Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Lamarck：在无回放的域增量学习中沿用免训练的 Mahalanobis 路由器，转而强化每域专家——用逐层 token 校正的适配器并让专家由前驱初始化，在 CDDB-Hard 上领先 12 个冻结骨干基线 5.2 点，DomainNet/OfficeHome 第二，平均精度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 per-domain 学习方法为每个新域训练额外专家（少量参数+分类头），测试时无域标签需路由器为图像选择专家。近期工作投入精力训练路由器，但分析显示免训练的 Mahalanobis 路由器相对"用真域专家"仅损失不到 2.7 点，说明瓶颈不在路由而在专家本身，因此应提升专家质量。

Method: 采用免训练 Mahalanobis 路由器；专家由小型可训练适配器构成，在每个深层 ViT block 之后对所有图像 token 进行校正；并将每个专家由其前驱专家初始化（零额外开销），利用各域共享同一类别集合的性质，使误路由到后续专家时损失更小。

Result: 适配器带来 +4.4 点最终精度（CDDB-Hard deepfake 基准）；在统一协议下对 12 个冻结骨干基线，CDDB-Hard 上领先 5.2 点排名第一，DomainNet 与 OfficeHome 上排名第二，平均精度最高。

Conclusion: 无回放域增量学习的关键改进方向是专家容量与初始化，而非路由训练；轻量逐层适配器加前驱初始化在多个基准上取得有竞争力的综合表现。

Abstract: Replay-free domain-incremental learning trains a model on the same classes in successive domains without keeping earlier images. Per-domain learners freeze a pre-trained ViT and train an additional expert for each new domain: the small set of parameters and the classification head learned on that domain. Test images carry no domain label, so a router selects one expert for each image. Recent work trains this router. However, a training-free Mahalanobis router costs the analyzed learners under 2.7 points of final accuracy relative to always selecting each image's own-domain expert. The experts, not this router, limit accuracy. Our learner, Lamarck, therefore uses this router and strengthens the experts. Its adapters, small trainable layers that correct every image token after each deep ViT block, add 4.4 points of final accuracy on the deepfake benchmark CDDB-Hard. In addition, each expert is initialized from its predecessor at no extra cost: the domains share their classes, so an image misrouted to a later expert loses less. Against twelve frozen-backbone baselines under one protocol, Lamarck is first on CDDB-Hard by 5.2 points, second on DomainNet and OfficeHome, and highest in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [100] [SEER: Semantic Evidence for Executable Region Propagation in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24152)
*Junyang Chen,Xiao Li,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练测试时修复层SEER，将OVSS后预测修复形式化为证据条件标签传播，在八个基准上平均mIoU达58.0（不更新OVSS权重）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）虽通过将CLIP类表示适配到稠密预测取得进展，但残余错误往往呈局部性、结构性，且与活跃标签空间相关；有效纠正需同时满足视觉合理性、目标词表合法性以及在分割分数场中可执行。

Method: SEER（Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation）：把后预测修复视为证据条件标签传播。OVSS分数空间提出紧凑候选标签；冻结VLM验证闭集区域证据；执行门（execution gate）认证证据是否在合法与语义层面可操作；被接受的标签由OVSS侧策略传播——高置信一致区域允许广泛传播，对象/边界风险情形采用保守的、分数接地的传播。

Result: 在八个标准OVSS基准上无需更新OVSS权重即达58.0平均mIoU；匹配输入对照实验隔离出修复机制的作用；资源测量报告完整冻结系统的成本。

Conclusion: 免训练、模块化的测试时修复可在不改动分割器权重的前提下稳定提升OVSS精度，且代价可测、机制可归因。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) has made strong progress by adapting CLIP-like representations to dense prediction, but its remaining errors are often local, structured, and tied to the active label space. A successful correction must be visually plausible, legal under the target vocabulary, and executable in the segmentation score field. We propose SEER, a training-free test-time repair layer for Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation. SEER casts post-prediction OVSS repair as evidence-conditioned label propagation. The OVSS score space proposes compact candidate labels, a frozen VLM verifies closed-set regional evidence, and an execution gate certifies whether the evidence is legally and semantically actionable. The accepted label is then propagated by an OVSS-side policy: coherent high-confidence regions allow broad propagation, while object- or boundary-risk cases use conservative score-grounded propagation. Across eight standard OVSS benchmarks, SEER reaches 58.0 average mIoU without updating OVSS weights. Matched-input controls isolate the repair mechanism, while resource measurements report the cost of the complete frozen system.

</details>


### [101] [Game2Player: Scaling Gameplay Experience for Visual Agents through Environment-Player Co-Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24154)
*Xirui Jiang,Yuxi Chen,Rui Yang,Feng Luo,Pengcheng Wang,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Zhao,Yushan Li,Han Wang,Manling Li,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 Game2Player 框架，让 Builder 智能体改造现有网页游戏并定义可验证任务，Coding Player 与 VLM Player 尝试任务并把问题反馈给 Builder 修复，从而把"游玩"同时作为环境验证与训练数据采集；共构建 568 个已验证游戏、3,436 个任务、15.7K 条轨迹（307 小时），留出 50 个游戏作为 PlayerBench，并用进度加权 SFT 训练 Game2Player-9B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏是视觉智能体的理想测试场，需感知、长程规划与精确控制；但人工采集游戏数据成本高，从视频学习需推断缺失动作，且现有数据集游戏覆盖窄、缺少显式任务与可验证结果。

Method: 构建"环境—玩家"协同演化闭环：Builder 智能体适配现有网页游戏、暴露内部状态、以基于状态的进度度量与成功判定定义任务；Coding Player 与 VLM Player 执行任务并向 Builder 报告缺陷以驱动环境修复，使游玩兼作环境验证与数据采集。基于此构建 568 个已验证网页游戏、3,436 个任务、15.7K 条轨迹，并留出 50 个游戏作为 PlayerBench；训练上采用进度加权 SFT，对推进任务进度的步骤赋予更高权重。

Result: Game2Player-9B 在 PlayerBench 上进度由 14.3% 提升至 31.2%，在外部 GameWorld 基准上由 30.0% 提升至 53.1%。

Conclusion: 通过构建、验证并从现有游戏世界中学习，可为视觉智能体规模化扩展游玩经验提供可行路径。

Abstract: Games are a rich testbed for visual agents, demanding perception, long-horizon planning, and precise control. However, collecting human gameplay is costly, and learning from gameplay videos requires inferring missing actions. Existing datasets also cover a limited range of games, often without explicit tasks or verifiable outcomes. To address these gaps, we introduce Game2Player, a framework that co-develops game environments and players to collect diverse and verifiable gameplay experience at scale. In the Game2Player loop, a Builder agent adapts existing web games, exposes their internal state, and defines tasks with state-based progress measures and success checks. A Coding Player and a VLM Player then attempt the tasks and report problems to the Builder for repair, so that play serves as both environment validation and training data collection. With Game2Player, we build 568 verified web games with 3,436 tasks, collect 15.7K trajectories (307 hours of gameplay), and hold out 50 games as the benchmark PlayerBench. To evaluate the collected experience as training data, we train VLM agents with progress-weighted SFT, assigning greater weight to steps that advance task progress. Game2Player-9B improves progress from 14.3% to 31.2% on PlayerBench and from 30.0% to 53.1% on the external GameWorld benchmark. More broadly, our work suggests a path for visual agents to scale their gameplay experience by building, verifying, and learning from existing game worlds.

</details>


### [102] [GenRF: Generative Registration-free Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24157)
*Linfeng Tang,Zhijun Fan,Yang Chen,Zizhuo Li,Hao Zhang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GenRF，利用预训练生成基础模型FLUX（经LoRA适配）在生成式先验与交叉注意力下，直接从失配且退化的源图像完成多模态图像融合，无需显式配准与预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中多模态采集存在几何失配（大视差、视场不一致导致部分区域无对应）与质量退化。现有方法依赖显式配准（大视差难以估计对应关系）、独立复原（未利用跨模态信息）、以及假设已对齐的复原-融合流程（对误配准鲁棒性差）。

Method: 重新审视失配与退化条件下的融合问题：交叉注意力可实现位置无关的互补信息聚合，生成式先验可支撑退化恢复与缺失区域推断。基于此，通过LoRA对预训练FLUX进行适配，联合执行退化增强、缺失跨模态上下文推断与互补信息融合，全程不显式估计形变场。

Result: 在真实红外与可见光数据上，GenRF无需预处理即可从失配、退化图像直接获得高质量融合结果，并在失配条件下提升下游任务性能；额外实验表明其可零任务特定微调地泛化到失配多曝光与多聚焦图像融合。

Conclusion: 将失配与退化融合统一到生成式基础模型框架中，绕开显式配准，验证了生成式先验在跨模态对齐无关融合与多任务泛化上的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion combines complementary observations to improve scene visibility and perception. However, in real-world acquisition, differences in sensing principles and configurations introduce geometric discrepancies, while degradations further compromise source quality. Existing methods typically rely on explicit registration, yet large parallax complicates correspondence estimation, while mismatched fields of view leave some regions without cross-modal counterparts. Meanwhile, independent restoration underuses cross-modal information, whereas restoration-fusion methods assuming alignment remain vulnerable to misregistration. This study revisits misaligned and degraded image fusion in the era of generative foundation models, in which cross-attention enables position-agnostic complementary information aggregation, while generative priors support degradation recovery and inference in missing regions. Building on this perspective, we propose GenRF, which adapts pretrained FLUX through LoRA to jointly enhance degraded observations, infer missing cross-modal context, and fuse complementary information without explicitly estimating deformation fields. Extensive experiments on real-world infrared and visible captures show that GenRF delivers high-quality fusion directly from unregistered, degraded images without preprocessing, while improving downstream task performance under misalignment. Additional experiments demonstrate that GenRF can generalize to misaligned multi-exposure and multi-focus image fusion without task-specific fine-tuning. Project: https://linfeng-tang.github.io/GenRF.

</details>


### [103] [AVTR: Adaptive Multimodal Reasoning with Visual Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24161)
*Xiaoqian Shen,Yunyang Xiong,Changsheng Zhao,Saksham Suri,Chong Zhou,Zechun Liu,Zhipeng Cai,Zhuang Liu,Qi Qian,Wei Wen,Hongyu Xu,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Yanbei Chen,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Lemeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应视觉-文本推理（AVTR），以生成式视觉反思作为文本推理的互补模态，并将推理模态选择建模为可学习路由问题，在16个基准上平均提升4.5分，推理延迟降低33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的文本推理范式存在两点局限：一是纯文本推理在视觉中心问题上限制感知能力，难以准确解读任务相关视觉证据；二是对所有输入统一施加文本推理，忽略了推理模态与推理深度随问题变化的差异，导致在无需复杂逻辑时产生冗长无用的推理链。

Method: 引入视觉想象作为文本思维的互补推理模态，使模型通过生成式视觉反思重新审视视觉观测并提炼显著证据；同时将推理模态选择形式化为可学习路由问题，由单一策略根据输入的感知与推理需求自适应调用视觉反思与文本推理。

Result: 在16个基准上，平均分数较Qwen3.5基线提升4.5分，同时推理延迟降低33%；方法可有效迁移至更小规模骨干，性能优于所评估的2B级基线。

Conclusion: 视觉反思与文本推理具互补性，按需自适应路由推理模态可在提升多模态任务性能的同时降低推理开销，并具备良好的规模可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models increasingly rely on textual reasoning to solve advanced multimodal tasks. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm faces two fundamental limitations. First, relying on textual reasoning alone can limit perceptual ability in vision-centric questions, where accurate answers depend on interpreting task-relevant visual evidence.
