Second, applying textual reasoning uniformly across inputs overlooks question-dependent variation in both reasoning modality and depth, often producing verbose and unnecessary traces when little logical inference is required. To this end, we propose Adaptive Visual-Textual Reasoning (AVTR), which introduces visual imagination as a complementary reasoning modality to textual thinking, enabling the model to revisit its visual observations and distill the salient evidence through generative visual reflection. Furthermore, we formulate reasoning-modality selection as a learned routing problem, allowing a single policy to adaptively harness the complementary strengths of visual reflection and textual reasoning in response to the perceptual and inferential demands of the input. Across 16 benchmarks, AVTR improves the average score by 4.5 points over Qwen3.5 baseline, while reducing inference latency by 33\%. It also scales effectively into a smaller size backbone, outperforming the evaluated 2B-scale baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Stabilizing Neural-to-Image Retrieval with Frequency-Domain Priors and Adaptive Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24163)
*YaoYang Liu,ZhuoShi Tang,Haopeng Lai,Weijun Sun,Xiaozhao Fang,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的神经-图像检索框架，通过在共享语义空间中嵌入双流适配、频域校准与相位引导温度调制，缓解EEG/MEG噪声与个体差异，在THINGS-EEG/MEG上跨被试内/被试间设置一致超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉神经解码可从脑活动中推断视觉刺激，但神经信号噪声大、试次间变异高且具被试依赖性，导致EEG/MEG与预训练视觉-语言嵌入的可靠对齐仍困难。

Method: 统一神经-图像检索框架：①双流适配，以相关性正则构建低维共享子空间，抑制冗余噪声、增强跨模态一致性；②频域校准，利用幅度与相位统计对神经表征做条件调制；③相位引导温度调制，按神经响应稳定性自适应调整对比学习监督强度。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG与THINGS-MEG数据集上，被试内与被试间两种设置下均一致优于强基线。

Conclusion: 异构神经条件下的鲁棒神经-图像检索得益于稳定的共享语义空间、频域信息驱动的表征校准与自适应监督；代码将开源。

Abstract: Visual neural decoding aims to infer perceived visual stimuli from brain activity, offering important insights into human visual cognition while enabling transformative applications in brain computer interfaces and artificial intelligence. Recent advances in pretrained vision-language models have provided a semantically rich representation space for this task. However, reliable alignment between EEG/MEG signals and visual embeddings remains challenging because neural responses are noisy, highly variable across trials, and subject dependent. To address this issue, we propose a unified neural-to-image retrieval framework that improves retrieval performance by aligning neural and image representations in a shared semantic space. The framework integrates dual-stream adaptation, frequency-domain calibration, and phase-guided temperature modulation to promote more robust cross-modal semantic alignment. Specifically, dual-stream adaptation models a low-dimensional shared subspace with correlation regularization to suppress redundant noise and enhance cross-modal consistency. Frequency-domain calibration conditionally modulates neural representations using amplitude and phase statistics. Phase-guided temperature modulation further adjusts the supervision strength in contrastive learning according to the stability of neural responses. Experiments on THINGS-EEG and THINGS-MEG under both intra-subject and inter-subject settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms strong baselines. These results suggest that robust neural-to-image retrieval under heterogeneous neural conditions benefits from a stable shared semantic space, frequency-informed representation calibration, and adaptive supervision. Code will be released at github.com/wanfkuabc/Geometry-Regularized-Brain-Embeddings.

</details>


### [105] [Gaussian Primitive Fusion for Any-Resolution Multimodal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24164)
*Baoshuo Cai,Xinrui Jiang,Bing Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出高斯基元融合（GPF），将异构分辨率多模态融合重构到共享的连续高斯空间，直接在原生分辨率上拟合观测并在连续表示中学习模态贡献，优化后按最细原生分辨率渲染，避免重采样伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法在统一离散网格上估计模态贡献，当源模态原生分辨率不同时需重采样构造该网格，易引入伪影并影响后续融合决策。

Method: GPF摒弃"先统一分辨率再融合"的流程：用共享的高斯几何与模态特定的内容表示直接拟合各源图像的原生分辨率观测，并在所得连续表示中学习模态贡献；优化完成后在最细原生分辨率上渲染融合表示，全程不对源图像重采样。

Result: 在异构分辨率输入下，GPF相比"重采样后融合"流程给出更清晰的目标轮廓与更细的纹理，模糊与块状伪影更少；在多个红外-可见光基准上定量优于配备插值或超分辨前端的SOTA融合方法。

Conclusion: 以连续高斯空间替代离散公共网格，是处理异构分辨率多模态融合的有效范式，可在避免重采样伪影的同时提升融合质量。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion integrates complementary observations from different modalities into a unified representation. Existing methods typically estimate modality contributions on a common discrete grid. When source modalities have different native resolutions, constructing such a grid generally involves resampling, which may introduce artifacts and affect subsequent fusion decisions. To address this problem, we propose Gaussian Primitive Fusion (GPF), which reformulates heterogeneous-resolution fusion in a shared continuous Gaussian space. Instead of first converting source images to a common resolution, GPF directly fits their native-resolution observations with shared Gaussian geometry and modality-specific content representations, and learns modality contributions within the resulting continuous representation. Once optimized, the fused representation is rendered at the finest native resolution without resampling the sources. With heterogeneous-resolution inputs, GPF produces clearer target contours and finer textures, with less blurring and blocking artifacts than resample-then-fuse pipelines. Quantitatively, it achieves the best results on multiple infrared-visible benchmarks against state-of-the-art fusion methods equipped with interpolation or super-resolution frontends.

</details>


### [106] [ViStep-RL: Stepwise Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24170)
*Junyang Chen,Yao Qin,Xiao Li,Ningchao Wang,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViStep-RL 将长时程视觉智能体的执行建模为依赖关联的证据子目标图，用图势能变化提供净进展奖励并配合主动记忆协议，在 ViInteract 上以 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 达到 60.0% 严格完成率（基线 10.0%，SFT 后 46.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法只教智能体"看哪里、怎么看"，而仅凭最终答案奖励无法指导"哪些观测满足有依赖关系的子目标、哪些证据应保留供后续使用"，缺乏过程性结构信号与证据留存机制。

Method: 提出 ViStep-RL：将任务执行表示为依赖链接的证据承载子目标图；验证器判定各子目标是否被有效证据支撑，并以图势能（graph potential）的变化构造净进展学习信号；引入主动记忆协议，跨轮保留证据并将检索与下游使用显式绑定；配合强化学习训练策略。

Result: ViInteract 上 ViStep-RL 达 60.0% 严格完成率，基线 10.0%，SFT 46.5%；消融显示增量任务状态奖励比累积奖励高 3.2 分，主动证据写入比自动写入提升 7.75 分；统一交互框架下答案准确率 59.5%，高出六个通用参考模型中最强者 24.0 分；留出回合在两种 Qwen 规模上验证增益，并迁移至三个外部视觉推理基准。

Conclusion: 以证据子目标图为核心的逐步强化学习信号与主动记忆机制，能显著提升长时程视觉智能体的任务完成度与证据可复用性，且增益具有跨模型规模与跨基准的泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon visual agents must do more than inspect images: they must turn observations into evidence that remains usable as a task unfolds. Existing active-perception methods teach agents where and how to look, while a final-answer reward gives little structure for deciding which observations satisfy dependent subgoals or should be retained for later use. We present ViStep-RL (Visual Stepwise Reinforcement Learning), which models execution as a dependency-linked graph of evidence-bearing subgoals. A verifier tracks which subgoals are supported by valid evidence, and changes in graph potential provide a net-progress learning signal; an active memory protocol preserves evidence across turns and links retrieval to downstream use. On ViInteract, ViStep-RL reaches 60.0% strict completion with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, compared with 10.0% for the base model and 46.5% after supervised fine-tuning. Controlled studies show that incremental task-state rewards outperform cumulative rewards by 3.2 points, while active evidence writing improves strict completion by 7.75 points over automatic writing. Under a common interaction framework, the task-trained ViStep-RL policy achieves 59.5% answer accuracy, 24.0 points above the strongest of six general-purpose reference models. Held-out episodes confirm the gain at both Qwen scales, and gains extend to three external visual reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [107] [ART: Bridging the Embodiment Gap in 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24182)
*Zichen Zhang,Wei Zhai,Xuji Zhang,Yang Cao,Yu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出3D人-机器人可供性迁移新任务，构建首个双标注大规模数据集HPAD（2,137物体/80类/13动作原语，27,000+对），并提出ART网络，通过可靠性门控过滤与分层重建+图扩散精修，将人类先验重构为机器人可执行分布，真实6-DoF机械臂实验中达86.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性方法直接将人类先验作为参考，忽视了可供性并非物体内在属性、而与智能体具身结构耦合；人手与机器人末端执行器在结构与形态上的差异导致人类先验直接迁移时出现严重不兼容与可供性错位。

Method: 提出HPAD数据集：每物体同时具备人类先验标注与仿真验证的机器人标注，覆盖2,137物体、80类别、13种原子动作原语、27,000+物体-动作对。提出ART网络：先以可靠性门控流（reliability gating stream）滤除人类先验中的几何误报（false positives），再经分层重建模块与图扩散精修（graph-diffusion refinement）缓解人-机可供性拓扑不匹配，将先验重构为机器人可操作的分布。

Result: 在HPAD上实验验证了任务设定与ART的有效性；在6-DoF机械臂配平行夹爪的真实平台上，于12个物体、4类动作、共2,400次执行的基准中，ART在其480次试验上取得最佳成功率86.5%。

Conclusion: 可供性迁移需显式建模具身差异，人类先验不能直接作为机器人操作依据；仿真验证的配对监督与"过滤—重建—精修"框架可有效实现人→机可供性对齐，并具备真实机器人执行可行性。

Abstract: 3D affordance acts as a critical link between perception and action, providing priors for robotic manipulation. While existing approaches predominantly leverage human priors as direct references, they often overlook that affordance is not an object-intrinsic property but is inherently coupled with the agent's embodiment. Due to the structural and morphological disparities between human hands and robot end-effectors, directly applying human priors causes severe incompatibility for robotic execution. Motivated by this, we introduce a novel task setting, 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer, which addresses the affordance misalignment arising from distinct embodiment structures. To support this task, we construct the Human-Robot Paired Affordance Dataset (HPAD), the first large-scale 3D affordance benchmark in which every object is annotated with both human-prior and simulation-validated robot labels. HPAD covers 2,137 objects across 80 categories and 13 atomic action primitives, yielding over 27,000 object-action pairs. On top of HPAD, we propose the 3D Affordance tRansfer neTwork (ART). ART first filters geometric false positives in the human prior via a reliability gating stream, then reconstructs the prior into a robot-actionable distribution through hierarchical reconstruction and a graph-diffusion refinement that resolves the topological mismatch between human and robot affordances. Experiments on HPAD demonstrate the reliability of the proposed task and the superiority of ART. We further validate ART on a 6-DoF arm with a parallel-jaw gripper in a 2,400-execution benchmark across 12 objects and 4 action categories; ART attains the best success rate (86.5% over its 480 trials), confirming the practicality of the transfer paradigm. Project page: https://derek0907.github.io/ART/

</details>


### [108] [VideoACE: Benchmarking Agentic Video Understanding through High-Frame-Rate Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24184)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Xuanyu Zheng,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoACE是一个面向"智能体式视频理解"的基准，聚焦高帧率动作视频，包含1000道细粒度动作理解多选题，配备时间证据标注与防泄漏用户提示，并通过视频检查工具+用户模拟器构建可持续交互的评测环境，考察模型能否将交互机会转化为更好的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式视频理解要求模型主动与环境交互以收集证据并形成判断，因此评测不应只看固定观测条件下的性能，而应考察模型能否把"可交互的机会"转化为更优的理解。高帧率动作视频中快速、细微的变化使针对性观测尤为有价值，适合作为此类评测场景。

Method: 构建VideoACE基准：1,000道多选题，覆盖六类细粒度动作理解，附带时间证据标注与经筛查以避免答案泄漏的用户提示；评测环境将视频检查工具与用户模拟器结合，允许模型调整时间覆盖范围、采样密度与空间细节，并基于模拟用户反馈重访证据、修正判断。设置三种评测范式：单次观测、自主工具使用、工具使用+用户反馈，对五个模型进行对比。

Result: 对五个模型的评测揭示出在证据获取方式以及对进一步交互机会的响应上存在明显不同的模式；三种范式的对比显示出各模型利用交互机会的效果差异。

Conclusion: VideoACE为超越固定观测设定下的性能评估、研究智能体式视频理解提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Agentic video understanding requires models to actively interact with video environments to gather evidence and form judgments. Evaluating this capability therefore requires assessing whether models can translate opportunities for interaction into better understanding. We introduce VideoACE, a benchmark for assessing video understanding under conditions that support sustained interaction. We focus on high-frame-rate action videos, where rapid, subtle changes make targeted observation especially valuable. The benchmark comprises 1,000 multiple-choice questions spanning six categories of fine-grained action understanding, accompanied by temporal evidence annotations and user hints screened for answer leakage. Its evaluation environment combines video inspection tools with a user simulator, allowing models to adjust temporal coverage, sampling density, and spatial detail, and to revisit evidence and revise their judgments under simulated user feedback. We evaluate single-pass observation, autonomous tool use, and tool use combined with user feedback to examine how effectively models exploit these opportunities. Evaluation of five models reveals distinct patterns in how they acquire evidence and respond to opportunities for further interaction. These findings establish VideoACE as a testbed for examining agentic video understanding beyond performance under a fixed observation setting.

</details>


### [109] [Monitoring Unplanned Construction Using Satellite Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24190)
*Harsh Kashyap,Taranum Wasu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文利用预训练神经网络提取卫星影像特征，配合标准机器学习分类器检测地表建成区与物理变化，在印度以外两个公开数据集上分别达到约95%的建成区识别准确率与约83%的影像对变化判别准确率，并对阿格拉48个图块做初步描述性检验，强调遥感信息只能反映物理变化，无法判断建设是否经规划许可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度城市化用地需求增长带来生态破坏、废弃物增加与公共服务压力；NITI Aayog 2021年报告称7,933个城市聚落中65%缺乏总体规划，但该指标仅反映规划覆盖情况，无法说明单体建筑是否获得授权，故需借助AI从卫星影像中识别物理变化。

Method: 采用预训练神经网络将不同时相卫星影像编码为特征向量，输入标准机器学习分类器以预测土地类别与物理变化；另在标准变化检测基准上训练一个小型神经网络；并在阿格拉样本（48个图块，7个正例）上进行初步描述性检验。

Result: 建成区识别准确率约95%，影像对是否发生变化的判别准确率约83%（每对影像赋予单一标签）；阿格拉样本的初步描述性检验亦显示卫星影像中蕴含物理变化信息。

Conclusion: 卫星影像可有效承载物理地表变化信息，但无法据此判定建设是否经过规划或获得许可；未来需将遥感变化检测与行政审批、产权等非影像数据结合才能评估合规性。

Abstract: Cities need land for homes, shops, roads, and public services. New construction changes that land. It can harm natural areas and water bodies, add to waste, and strain public services. NITI Aayog's 2021 report stated that 65% of 7,933 urban settlements in India had no master plan. That number tells us about planning coverage at the time. It does not tell us whether any single building was authorised. Artificial intelligence, which lets computers learn patterns from data, can help find physical changes by comparing satellite images taken on different dates. We test simple methods on two public datasets from outside India. First, we turn each image into a set of numbers using a pretrained neural network, a model already trained to recognise patterns in images. We feed those numbers to standard machine-learning classifiers that predict land categories and physical changes. The methods reach about 95 percent accuracy at spotting built-up land and about 83 percent accuracy at saying whether a small pair of images shows change, where each pair gets one label. We also train a small neural network on a standard change-detection benchmark. Separately, we run a preliminary descriptive check on an Agra sample of 48 patches, 7 of them positive. The results show that satellite images carry information about physical change. They do not show whether construction was planned or permitted.

</details>


### [110] [TrajWAM: Trajectory-Conditioned Future Learning for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24192)
*Xuerui Fang,Gulimila Kezierbieke,Chen Wangxing,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrajWAM：以自车轨迹为条件的世界动作模型，通过BEV未来预测辅助学习提升视觉-语言自动驾驶规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将未来场景预测与动作规划相连接，而每条驾驶记录天然提供"已执行轨迹—观测到的未来"配对，为学习这种连接提供事实性监督。本文旨在利用该配对信号改进视觉-语言驾驶模型的规划能力。

Method: 提出轨迹条件化的WAM：以自车轨迹查询作为条件，从共享规划器表征中预测与DINOv3对齐的BEV未来特征。训练包含两项目标：(1) 在执行轨迹下的未来事实性重建；(2) 在无标签扰动轨迹下进行单侧特征分离。二者共同训练共享规划器以输出行驶路点。推理时直接生成路点，绕过辅助未来预测分支。

Result: 在Bench2Drive全部220条路线上，取得91.53 Driving Score与75.45%成功率，相比调度匹配的纯规划微调基线提升5.31个Driving Score点、8条成功路线。

Conclusion: 轨迹条件化的BEV未来学习可作为有效辅助任务增强共享规划器表征，且推理零额外开销；扰动轨迹的单侧分离约束有助于规划器区分不同动作后果，验证了"预测辅助规划"范式的实用价值。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) connect future scene prediction with action planning for autonomous driving. Each driving record pairs an executed ego trajectory with its observed future, providing a factual outcome for learning this connection. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-conditioned WAM that improves vision-language driving through auxiliary bird's-eye-view (BEV) future learning. An ego-trajectory query conditions DINOv3-aligned BEV predictions from shared planner representations. Training combines factual reconstruction under the executed trajectory with one-sided feature separation under an unlabeled perturbed trajectory. Together, these objectives train the shared planner to generate driving waypoints. Native inference directly generates waypoints and bypasses the auxiliary future-prediction branch. On all 220 Bench2Drive routes, TrajWAM achieves 91.53 Driving Score and 75.45% Success Rate, exceeding a schedule-matched planning-only fine-tuning baseline by 5.31 Driving Score points and eight successful routes.

</details>


### [111] [WM^MLLM: Turning MLLMs into World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24193)
*Baiang Li,Enze Xie,Jincheng Yu,Jin Wang,Haozhe Liu,Yitong Li,Juanxi Tian,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WM^MLLM，首个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的交互式世界模型，将世界状态外置于生成器（全景图+修订历史），由MLLM将其写为状态操作，实现单图输入的持久、可编辑世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型把世界保存在生成器上下文中，导致重访需重新合成、先前编辑会漂移或消失，缺乏持久性与可控编辑能力。

Method: 将世界状态外化为带修订历史的全景图；MLLM把每个请求转化为对该状态的操作，选择局部编辑写入区域，并在候选成为下一状态前进行校验；图像生成器仅提议视角，视频仅负责呈现。

Result: MBench-A上四项环境恢复指标均值最高（Epipolar 91.5，对比0.1–74.0），相机往返完成率85.3%（基线≤50.9%）；CompBench全部15列优于15个基线；112个盲评局部编辑中，生成后再选写入区域使完成率由34.8%升至83.0%；258个样本中46次重访均精确复原，97个通过校验的局部编辑均不改变区域外像素。

Conclusion: 以结构化外部状态替代生成器隐式上下文，可在设计上保证重访一致性、局部编辑局部性与请求链式连续性，兼顾持久性与可编辑性。

Abstract: Video world models keep the world in the context of a generator, so a revisit is synthesized again and earlier edits can drift or disappear. We present WM^MLLM, the first interactive world model built on multimodal large language models (MLLMs), for persistent, editable world generation from a single image. WM^MLLM keeps the world state outside every generator, as a panorama with a revision history, and lets the MLLMs write it: they turn each request into operations on this state, select where a local edit is written, and check every candidate before it becomes the next state. Image generators only propose views, and video only presents them. Users can walk to new places and return, edit objects through text or a box, place subjects, and change lighting, weather or season. By construction, a revisit at the same revision reproduces the earlier view exactly, a local edit leaves every panorama pixel outside its region unchanged, and each request starts from the result of the previous one. Against six world model baselines on MBench-A, WM^MLLM has the highest mean on all four environment-recovery metrics (Epipolar 91.5 versus 0.1 to 74.0) and completes the most camera round trips (85.3% versus at most 50.9%). It has the best value in all 15 CompBench columns against 15 baselines. On 112 local edits judged blind, selecting the region to write after generation raises completion from 34.8% to 83.0%. On 258 WBench and MBench-A items, all 46 revisits recover their earlier views, and all 97 local edits that pass the check leave panorama pixels outside their region unchanged.

</details>


### [112] [From Neural Tokens to Language: Dual-View EEG Semantic Bridging for Brain-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24197)
*YaoYang Liu,Jun Dai,ZhuoShi Tang,Weijun Sun,Xiaozhao Fang,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双视角语义 token 桥接框架，用原始图像与草图作为互补视觉监督，学习两类 EEG 语义 token 流，并通过分支特定语义查询桥转换为 LLM 软提示，实现免图像 EEG 条件文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG-to-text 方法将 EEG 压缩为单一全局表示并直接投影到 LLM 隐空间，在噪声大、受试者相关的 EEG 信号下难以保留互补的视觉语义。

Method: 1) 双视角语义 token 桥接：以原始图像与草图图像为互补监督，学习 raw-oriented 与 sketch-oriented 两条 EEG 语义 token 流；2) 分支特定语义查询桥（branch-specific semantic query bridge）：将每条 token 流聚合为紧凑语义槽并转为 LLM 兼容软提示；3) 渐进式适配策略：从图像辅助训练过渡到无图像、仅 EEG 条件的生成。

Result: 在多个 LLM 骨干上取得较强的按受试者划分的 EEG 分类性能与具竞争力的 EEG-to-text 生成表现；消融、分支级表示及 EEG-标签解耦分析表明两条流学到互补的视角导向表示，且 EEG 衍生的提示提供超越类别标签的样本特定信息。

Conclusion: 双视角互补监督与语义查询桥接能有效缓解单一全局表示的语义损失，渐进式适配支持实用的免图像 EEG 文本解码。

Abstract: Visually evoked EEG-to-text generation aims to produce natural-language descriptions from neural responses elicited by visual stimuli. Existing approaches typically compress EEG signals into a single global representation and directly project it into the hidden space of a large language model (LLM), which may limit the preservation of complementary visual semantics under noisy and subject-dependent EEG signals. We propose a dual-view semantic token bridging framework that exploits raw images and sketch images as complementary visual supervision to learn raw-oriented and sketch-oriented EEG semantic token streams. To interface these structured neural representations with an LLM, we introduce a branch-specific semantic query bridge that aggregates each token stream into compact semantic slots and converts them into LLM-compatible soft prompts. A progressive adaptation strategy further transitions the learned visual-semantic interface from image-supported training to image-free EEG-conditioned generation. Experiments demonstrate strong subject-wise EEG classification performance and competitive EEG-to-text generation across multiple LLM backbones. Ablation, branch-level representation, and EEG-label disentanglement analyses further show that the two EEG streams learn complementary view-oriented representations and that EEG-derived prompts provide sample-specific information beyond category labels.

</details>


### [113] [SpiderMark: Hybrid Watermarking for Generative AI via Algorithmic Injection and Learning-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24200)
*Chihcheng Hsieh,Yang Song,Flora Salim,Martin Bell,Jacinto C. Nascimento,Catarina Moreira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpiderMark：将蛛网状结构化频域水印注入扩散模型初始潜噪声的傅里叶表示，并用轻量ResNet-18概率验证器实现鲁棒的水印检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型普及，亟需可靠的AI生成图像溯源与验证手段；现有水印方法依赖各向同性频率模式与手工检测指标，存在方向性脆弱和跨图像变换阈值不稳定的问题。

Method: 算法与学习相结合的混合水印框架，无需修改预训练扩散模型权重。将径向辐条+同心多边形环构成的蛛网状图案嵌入初始潜噪声的傅里叶表示；训练轻量ResNet-18验证器，并融合PSNR、L1等辅助相似度线索，输出连续水印置信度用于检测。

Result: 在九种评测条件下有八种取得最高固定阈值准确率；在未参与验证器重训练的COCO提示词上仍保持强检测能力；CLIP相似度与FID显示检测增益仅伴随轻微的提示对齐与分布质量差异。

Conclusion: 结构化水印几何与学习式概率验证可统一于单一实用框架，在不可感知性、鲁棒性与真实可验证性之间取得平衡，服务生成式AI内容归属。

Abstract: The proliferation of diffusion-based generative models has intensified the need for reliable methods to trace and verify the provenance of AI-generated images. Existing watermarking approaches for diffusion models typically rely on isotropic frequency patterns and handcrafted detection metrics, which are prone to directional vulnerability and threshold instability across diverse image transformations. We propose SpiderMark, a hybrid watermarking framework that combines algorithmic frequency-domain injection with learning-based probabilistic verification, requiring no modifications to pretrained diffusion model weights. SpiderMark embeds a structured spider-web pattern, comprising radial spokes and concentric polygonal rings, into the Fourier representation of the initial latent noise, and trains a lightweight ResNet-18 verifier augmented with auxiliary similarity cues (PSNR and L1) to produce a continuous watermark confidence score for watermark detection. Across nine evaluation conditions, SpiderMark achieves the highest fixed-threshold Accuracy in eight, while retaining strong detection on unseen COCO prompts without verifier retraining. CLIP similarity and FID measurements show that these detection gains are accompanied by small measured differences in prompt alignment and distributional image quality. SpiderMark establishes that structured watermark geometry and learned probabilistic verification can be unified into a single, practical framework, bridging the gap between imperceptibility, robustness, and real-world verifiability for generative AI content attribution.

</details>


### [114] [DetailScale-SR: Decoupling Restoration and Continuous Detail Control for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24212)
*Shengchuan Gao,Zhongjie Duan,Yingda Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetailScale-SR 是一个偏好条件化的真实图像超分框架，可对同一低分辨率输入连续、平滑地控制重建细节程度，从保守复原过渡到富纹理增强，并兼具超出参考的高频增强与中文字符可读性恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实超分中"更多细节"并非总是用户所需：同一退化图像可能要求克制复原、忠实重建或视觉细节增强，取决于用户意图。既有方法依赖后处理或反复改写提示词，难以在固定输入下连续调节细节，且不易将内容恢复与细节偏好解耦。

Method: 提出偏好条件化（preference-conditioned）框架：在固定低分辨率输入、保持场景语义不变的前提下，学习一族连续的重建结果（continuous family of reconstructions），将内容恢复与细节偏好表征分离，通过偏好条件实现细节程度的连续插值与可控调节，无需后处理或提示词重写。

Result: 实现从保守复原到富纹理结果的平滑过渡且语义约束一致；可控范围超出常规复原，能增强高频结构并生成比配对高分辨率参考更具感知丰富性的纹理；在文本丰富场景（尤其中文海报与标牌）上，在同等文本引导下比既有生成式超分方法恢复出更清晰、字形更忠实的结果。

Conclusion: DetailScale-SR 统一了连续细节控制、超越参考的增强以及语义重要内容的可靠恢复，为按用户意图定制的真实图像超分提供了可控范式。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution often seeks the most detailed plausible reconstruction, although more detail is not always desirable. The same degraded image may require restrained restoration, faithful reconstruction, or enhanced visual detail according to user intent. We present DetailScale-SR, a preference-conditioned framework that continuously controls reconstructed detail for a fixed low-resolution input without altering the scene being restored. Rather than relying on post-processing or repeatedly rewriting prompts, it learns a continuous family of reconstructions while separating content recovery from detail preference. This enables a smooth transition from conservative restoration to richly textured results under consistent input and semantic constraints. Its controllable range also extends beyond conventional restoration: DetailScale-SR can enhance high-frequency structures and generate perceptually richer textures than the paired high-resolution reference. Text restoration is another distinct strength. For text-rich scenes, especially Chinese posters and signs, it recovers more legible and character-faithful text than prior generative SR methods under matched textual guidance. DetailScale-SR therefore unifies continuous detail control, beyond-reference enhancement, and reliable recovery of semantically important content. Our model is available at https://modelscope.cn/models/DiffSynth-Studio/Qwen-Image-2.1-SR

</details>


### [115] [Target-Consistent Action Guidance via Semi-Unbalanced Optimal Transport for Drifting World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24222)
*Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有 action-conditioned drifting world model（DriftWorld 式）中"排斥当前帧"的引导方式存在目标偏差，提出 Mass-Share Accentuation（MSA）通过半松弛最优传输消除该偏差，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升了动作排序与响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: action-conditioned world model 在规划与离线策略评估中需大量 rollout，多步扩散推理代价高；drifting model 把迭代从推理搬到训练、单次前向生成，DriftWorld 将其引入 world model，但为强化动作跟随性而"排斥当前帧预测"的构造（借自 classifier-free guidance）会带来目标偏差——即使预测已匹配真实未来，推力依旧存在。

Method: 作者先在 kernel、balanced 与 unbalanced Wasserstein drifting field 中证明该偏差普遍存在；随后提出 MSA，将当前帧视为与真实未来竞争的"反事实目的地"，构造半松弛最优传输问题，通过减去正确未来所对应的闭式份额使引导在目标处归零，剩余部分由所有预测共享；并证明在坍缩预测下真实未来是运动方向上唯一不动点，且引导随预测误差衰减。

Result: 在 Push-T、Robomimic、BridgeV2 上（训练后模型表现为反应不足而非过冲），MSA 相比 latent-space 基线在动作排序、响应斜率、运动区域误差上均有提升，且推理零额外开销；作者也指出这些基准未能单独隔离"偏差消除"这一因果因素。

Conclusion: MSA 以半松弛最优传输的闭式份额修正排斥式引导的目标偏差，理论上保证真实未来为唯一不动点，实践中以零推理代价改善动作条件响应；但偏差消除与性能提升之间的因果归因仍待针对性实验验证。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let robots imagine the outcomes of their actions for planning and offline policy evaluation, but these uses need many rollouts, which multi-step diffusion makes expensive. Drifting models move the iteration from inference to training and generate samples in one network pass. DriftWorld brought this paradigm to action-conditioned world models; to strengthen action following, it repels predictions from the current frame, a construction borrowed from classifier-free guidance. We show that this accentuation has a target bias: the push persists even when a prediction matches the recorded future, in kernel, balanced, and unbalanced Wasserstein drifting fields alike. We introduce Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA) to remove this bias. MSA treats the current frame as a counterfactual destination competing with the recorded future in a semi-relaxed optimal transport problem; subtracting the closed-form share of the correct future makes the guidance vanish at the target, and the remainder is shared by all predictions. We prove that the recorded future is the unique fixed point along the motion direction for collapsed predictions, and that the guidance fades with prediction error. On Push-T, Robomimic, and BridgeV2, where trained models under-react rather than overshoot, MSA improves action ranking, response slope, and moving-region error over latent-space baselines at no inference cost, though these benchmarks do not isolate bias removal as the cause.

</details>


### [116] [Training Denoising Models of Any Architecture Without Stored Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24223)
*Christos Koutlis,Ioannis Kompatsiaris,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种教师监督训练方法（teacher-supervised training），使任意架构与规模的连续时间扩散/流模型可从随机初始化开始训练，且无需存储原始图像数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模图像数据集存储成本高，且部分数据未公开或涉及隐私，无法获取。作者追问：预训练去噪模型能否在训练中替代其原始训练数据集？

Method: 学生模型在"滚动噪声样本池"（rolling pool of noisy samples）上回归教师模型的速度场（velocity）；样本沿随机步数的轨迹推进，从而获得独立、稠密且近似连续的噪声水平。

Result: 实验覆盖6个模型家族的12个教师模型与6个数据集。在CIFAR-10上，学生规模为教师1/4时优于同网络标准训练；与教师同规模时FID仅差0.75–1.92；并优于使用教师生成图像训练及步数缩减方法（无教师初始化）。

Conclusion: 连续时间扩散与流模型可在不存储图像的情况下从零训练，显著节省大数据的存储开销，并适用于数据未发布或私密的场景。

Abstract: Can a pretrained denoising model replace the dataset used to train it? We investigate this question through teacher-supervised training, showing for the first time that continuous-time diffusion and flow models of any architecture and size can be trained from random initialization without stored images. Our students regress the teacher's velocity on a rolling pool of noisy samples, advanced along trajectories of random step counts to obtain independent, dense and approximately continuous noise levels. This saves considerable storage for large datasets and applies even when the data are unreleased or private. Our experiments include 12 teachers of 6 families and 6 datasets. Our method outperforms standard training of the same network on CIFAR-10 for students 4x smaller than the teacher, comes within 0.75 to 1.92 FID of it at the teacher's size and surpasses training on teacher-generated images and step reduction methods without teacher-based initialization. Our code is available at https://github.com/mever-team/ddm.

</details>


### [117] [Psychometrics-Inspired Coreset Selection for Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24233)
*Susu Jiang,Xuelin Shen,Baoliang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRAM，一个受心理测量学分级反应模型（GRM）启发的质量感知表示学习框架，用于IQA任务中的coreset选择，在9个数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模主观标注是训练准确IQA模型的关键瓶颈；coreset选择可减少标注负担，但视觉特征空间中的多样性未必反映感知质量多样性，因此需明确表示应编码哪些与质量相关的因素。

Method: 借鉴GRM原理，将图像质量判断建模为三个互补因素：潜在质量、内容依赖的响应灵敏度、失真依赖的等级阈值；三者共同决定达到各质量等级的概率及期望质量分数，学习到的参数构成结构化的质量感知表示，与通用视觉特征互补用于coreset选择。

Result: 在9个数据集上的实验表明，在不同选择策略、下游IQA模型和标注预算下均取得改进。

Conclusion: GRM与感知质量评估原理天然契合，所提出的质量感知表示能有效补充通用视觉特征以提升coreset选择效果，代码将公开。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale subjective annotations remains a critical bottleneck in training accurate image quality assessment (IQA) models. Coreset selection reduces this burden by selecting representative images before annotation. However, diversity in a visual feature space does not necessarily reflect diversity in perceptual quality, raising a fundamental question about which quality-related factors the representation should encode. We propose GRAM, a quality-aware representation learning framework inspired by the graded response model (GRM) from psychometrics. GRM describes how an individual's latent ability determines the probabilities of reaching successive assessment grades, with response sensitivity and grade thresholds governing this relationship. In this paper, we find that this principle naturally aligns with perceptual quality assessment, where the impact of image degradation depends on image content and distortion characteristics. Building on this connection, GRAM models quality judgments through three complementary factors: latent quality, content-dependent response sensitivity, and distortion-dependent grade thresholds. These factors jointly determine the probabilities of attaining different quality grades and the resulting expected quality score. The learned parameters provide a structured quality-aware representation that complements general visual features for coreset selection. Experiments on nine datasets demonstrate improvements across different selection strategies, downstream IQA models, and annotation budgets. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [118] [ViLFlow: Parallel Vision-Language Generation with Continuous Latent Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24240)
*Kevin Zhai,Bian Sun,Zhenyi Wang,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViLFlow 是一个连续流(continuous flow)视觉-语言生成模型，将对输出嵌入的联合建模与并行 token 解码结合，在 VQA 与图像描述任务上优于自回归和离散扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言生成主要依赖自回归模型（逐 token 顺序生成）和离散扩散模型（每步去噪中独立预测 token），前者顺序依赖效率受限、后者缺乏 token 间并行一致性建模。作者希望构建一种连续流框架，既保留连续嵌入联合建模的优势，又实现并行 token 解码。

Method: 将预训练的连续语言生成模型适配到 VQA 与图像描述，采用两项关键技术：(1) 学习式图像引导——通过一个视觉适配的教师模型提供图像条件下的预测，作为额外去噪监督，去噪器学会直接复现这些预测，从而在每步生成中保留一个图像条件预测；(2) 预测感知的 token 解码——用去噪器预测与配对参考文本训练 token 解码器，提升连续输出到词的转换；解码器可独立适配，无需再训练去噪器。在图像描述中，解码器还在后期去噪阶段提供文本反馈，使已解码的词参与后续精修。

Result: VQAv2 上比自回归与掩码扩散基线最多高 8.46 个百分点；GQA 上（未用 GQA 训练）高 4.50 个百分点；COCO Karpathy test 上 CIDEr 比掩码扩散高 16.25、比采样自回归高 17.81。

Conclusion: ViLFlow 表明连续流建模结合并行 token 解码可有效用于视觉-语言生成，且解码器的独立适配与后期文本反馈机制能在不重训去噪器的前提下持续提升问答与描述性能。

Abstract: Vision-language generation has predominantly relied on autoregressive and discrete diffusion models. Autoregressive models generate tokens sequentially, while discrete diffusion models typically predict tokens independently within each denoising step. We propose ViLFlow (Vision-Language Flow), a continuous-flow vision-language model that combines joint modeling of output embeddings with parallel token decoding. ViLFlow adapts pretrained continuous language generation to visual question answering and image captioning through learned image guidance and prediction-aware token decoding. A visually adapted teacher provides image-guided predictions as additional denoising supervision. The denoiser learns to reproduce these predictions directly, retaining one image-conditioned prediction per generation step. We train the token decoder on denoiser predictions and paired reference text to improve the conversion of continuous outputs into words. The decoder can be adapted independently, improving answering and captioning without further denoiser training. For captioning, it also supplies text feedback during late denoising, allowing decoded words to inform subsequent refinement. ViLFlow outperforms autoregressive and masked-diffusion baselines by up to 8.46 percentage points on VQAv2 and 4.50 points on GQA without GQA training. On COCO Karpathy test, it improves CIDEr by 16.25 over masked diffusion and 17.81 over sampled autoregression.

</details>


### [119] [End-to-End Landsat Land Surface Temperature Reconstruction with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24243)
*Shengjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出A²E²，一个端到端U-Net框架，融合年温度循环（ATC）、地球系统模式粗分辨率LST与AlphaEarth嵌入，实现Landsat 30 m地表温度（LST）云污染区域的连续重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landsat 30 m LST对多类应用至关重要，但云污染导致无法获得空间连续观测，需在云覆盖条件下重建高质量LST。

Method: 构建端到端U-Net模型A²E²：ATC提供物理引导的热先验，粗分辨率LST提供时间背景场，U-Net利用观测像元将嵌入场转换为日尺度的精细尺度校正量。

Result: 在2022年纽约市四幅季节分布Landsat 8/9场景上，MAE为0.78 K、RMSE为1.01 K，较无空间校正基线降低误差60.1%，较以年均光谱反射率引导的同结构U-Net降低3.7%；对晴空像元距离与遮挡像元比例不敏感；ECOSTRESS LST独立验证表明地球基础模型嵌入优于年均光谱波段。

Conclusion: 融合物理先验、模式粗LST与地球基础模型嵌入可有效重建云下Landsat LST，且嵌入特征在重建任务中较传统光谱特征更具优势。

Abstract: Land surface temperature (LST) data at Landsat's 30 m resolution is important for many applications, but cloud contamination prevents its spatially continuous observations. Here, we propose A$^2$E$^2$, an end-to-end U-Net that integrates annual temperature cycle (ATC), coarse LST from Earth system modeling, and AlphaEarth embeddings for Landsat LST reconstruction. The ATC supplies a physics-guided thermal prior, the coarse LST provides a temporal background, and the U-Net converts the embedding field into day-specific fine-scale correction using observed pixels. Experimental results on four seasonally distributed Landsat 8/9 scenes over New York City in 2022 show that A$^2$E$^2$ achieves 0.78 K mean absolute error and 1.01 K root mean squared error, reducing error by 60.1% relative to baseline method without spatial correction and by 3.7% relative to the same U-Net guided by annual mean spectral reflectance. Sensitivity analysis shows stable performance regardless of distance to clear pixels and percentage of blocked pixels. Independent validation with ECOSTRESS LST shows that Earth foundation model embeddings have better performance than annual mean spectral bands for LST reconstruction.

</details>


### [120] [Allocentric Memory, Egocentric Acting: A Dual-Frame VLA for Urban Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24247)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Tuo Feng,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CityNav-VLA双坐标系视觉-语言-动作模型，融合动态自我中心感知与持久化异中心空间记忆，用于城市语言目标无人机导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言目标空中导航的核心难点在于将不断变化的自我中心（egocentric）感知与锚定在稳定位置的长期空间证据联系起来，以实现长距离导航。

Method: 以地理与地标先验初始化的固定北向地图增量累积视觉观测，保留跨视角变化的空间证据；以指令、当前观测、UAV状态与运动历史为条件的动态查询检索记忆证据，预测地图坐标系目标；确定性控制器结合当前位姿将目标转换为机体系指令；提出早期目标承诺（EGC），用前K次预测的逐坐标中位数固定执行目标，且不引入额外可训练参数。

Result: Test Unseen上NE降低21.2%，SR与SPL分别提升15.8%与30.9%；数据精炼后相对前SOTA，NE降低6.2%，SR与SPL提升5.6%与23.2%。消融显示导航地图特征贡献最大，度量坐标进一步提升终点精度。4B骨干下单个A100平均每次决策0.68 s、峰值显存8.6 GB。

Conclusion: 双坐标系设计与空间记忆机制有效提升长程语言目标城市导航性能与效率，EGC可抑制长时序目标抖动。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires a UAV to reach a destination specified in natural language using observations collected during flight. A key challenge is linking changing egocentric perception with spatial evidence anchored to stable locations for long-range navigation. We propose CityNav-VLA, a dual-frame vision-language-action model for urban navigation integrating dynamic egocentric context with persistent allocentric spatial memory. The UAV incrementally accumulates visual observations on a fixed north-up map initialized with geographic and landmark priors, preserving evidence across viewpoint changes. A dynamic query conditioned on the instruction, current observations, UAV state, and motion history retrieves relevant memory evidence to predict a map-frame target. A deterministic controller converts this target into body-frame commands using the UAV's current pose, connecting global reasoning with egocentric execution. To reduce target fluctuations over long horizons, early goal commitment (EGC) fixes the execution target to the coordinate-wise median of the first $K$ predictions without extra trainable parameters. On Test Unseen, CityNav-VLA reduces NE by 21.2% and improves SR and SPL by 15.8% and 30.9%, respectively. It also reduces NE by 6.2% and improves SR and SPL by 5.6% and 23.2%, respectively, relative to the former SOTA after dataset refinement. Cumulative ablations show that navigation-map features yield the largest gains, while metric coordinates further improve endpoint accuracy. With a 4B-scale backbone, inference averages 0.68 s per decision with 8.6 GB peak memory on a single A100, presenting remarkable model efficiency.

</details>


### [121] [One Shot, Any View: High-Fidelity Video Reshooting with Geometry Guidance and Visual References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24253)
*Kai Huang,Runze Xu,Hui Ren,Taiyi Wu,Xinyuan Li,Xun Gao,Quande Liu,Dong Tang,Tongtong Shen,Xuefeng Su,Alan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 One Shot Any View 系统，基于单段实拍视频与显式 3D 先验（点视频），沿用户指定相机轨迹生成同一表演的"补拍"视频；通过真实多相机视频训练保证训练-推理一致性，并用几何锚定的 RoPE 偏移实现时序同步，在客观与人工评测中优于现有重拍方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后期想从额外角度拍摄同一场景通常需昂贵的补拍。现有几何接地（geometry-grounded）方法依赖前馈重建生成的点视频作为条件，但以人为中心的电影镜头常使用长焦、浅景深与低调光，导致重建质量严重下降，生成结果继承退化的点视频外观，画质明显劣化。

Method: 1) 以源视频与显式 3D 先验（由前馈重建沿目标轨迹渲染的点视频）为条件的新视角视频生成；2) 除 3D 资产渲染视频外，引入真实多相机视频训练，其点视频由与推理阶段相同的重建管线生成，实现训练-推理一致性，使生成器仅从点视频获取人物与场景的构图/结构、从源视频获取外观；3) 引入几何锚定的 RoPE 偏移，保持生成视频与源视频时序同步，同时抑制复制点视频的退化外观；4) 生成器基于预训练 reference-to-video (R2V) 模型，可选接受角色与场景参考图像以补全源视频未出现的区域外观。

Result: 在客观指标与人工评测中，One Shot Any View 均优于现有重拍（reshooting）方法。

Conclusion: 通过训练-推理一致的数据构造与几何锚定 RoPE 偏移，可将低质量点视频转化为可用的结构条件，从而实现单视频输入的任意轨迹高质量重拍，并兼容参考图像以补全未观测区域。

Abstract: Filming a scene from an additional angle after production usually requires a costly reshoot. We present One Shot Any View, a system that takes a recorded clip and generates a reshot video of the same performance along a user-specified camera trajectory. As in recent geometry-grounded methods, the system conditions generation on the source video and an explicit 3D prior, namely a point video rendered along the requested trajectory from a feed-forward reconstruction of the source video. We target human-centric cinematic videos, which are often shot with long lenses, shallow depth of field, and low-key lighting, all of which degrade feed-forward reconstruction. Existing geometry-grounded methods tend to inherit the degraded point video, and their output quality drops visibly. Two designs make such point videos usable. First, in addition to rendered videos of 3D assets, we train on real-world multi-camera videos, whose point videos are produced by the same reconstruction pipeline as at inference. This training-inference consistency enables the generator to take only the composition of people and scene elements from the point video, and their appearance from the source video. Second, a geometry-anchored RoPE offset keeps the generated video temporally synchronized with the source while largely preventing the output from copying the degraded appearance of the point video. Meanwhile, since the generator is a pretrained reference-to-video (R2V) model, the system also accepts optional character and scene reference images, which supply the appearance of regions that the source never shows. In both objective and human evaluations, One Shot Any View outperforms existing reshooting methods.

</details>


### [122] [Spark-H3: Adaptive Block Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24256)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Xinyao Rao,Liuxuan Sun,Jian Zhang,Yao Yao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练的块稀疏注意力方法 Spark-Attn（含 Spark-Reblock 与 Spark-Reweight），并在 MiniMax-H3 上实例化为 Spark-H3，实现视频扩散模型的质量-效率最优折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频序列使注意力成为视频扩散模型的主要开销；现有 BSA 方法存在两个缺陷：固定 token 分组会把注意力偏好不同的 token 归入同一组，而均匀 key 平均会低估压缩块的注意力质量（attention mass）。

Method: Spark-Reblock 依据注意力偏好递归地对 token 分组，同时保持规则块形状以利于 GPU 高效执行；Spark-Reweight 以全局平均 query 为锚点，为压缩块构造注意力加权的 key-value 表示与 logit 偏置，从而校准其注意力质量。整体为无训练（training-free）BSA 方案。

Result: 在 MiniMax-H3（20 步去噪、345 帧、1344×768）上，10% 精确块密度时相对稠密输出达到 24.81 dB PSNR 与 2.49× DiT 加速；密度提升至 20% 时 PSNR 升至 26.76 dB，仍有 2.26× 加速。

Conclusion: Spark-H3 在视频扩散模型的无训练 BSA 中取得了当前最优的质量-效率折中，验证了注意力偏好感知分组与压缩块重加权对稀疏注意力精度的有效性。

Abstract: Long video sequences make attention a substantial cost in video diffusion models. Block sparse attention (BSA) reduces this cost by computing selected blocks exactly while compressing the remaining blocks. However, fixed token grouping can place tokens with dissimilar attention preferences in the same group, while uniform key averaging can underestimate the attention mass of compressed blocks. We introduce Spark-Attn, a training-free BSA method comprising Spark-Reblock and Spark-Reweight. Spark-Reblock recursively groups tokens by attention preference while preserving regular block shapes for efficient GPU execution. Spark-Reweight uses the global mean query as an anchor to construct attention-weighted key-value representations and logit biases for the compressed blocks, calibrating their attention mass. We instantiate Spark-Attn in MiniMax-H3 as Spark-H3. At 10% exact-block density, Spark-H3 delivers a 2.49$\times$ DiT speedup and reaches 24.81 dB PSNR against the corresponding dense outputs on MiniMax-H3 (20 denoising steps, 345 frames, 1344$\times$768). Increasing the density to 20% further improves PSNR to 26.76 dB while still achieving a 2.26$\times$ DiT speedup. These results demonstrate that Spark-H3 achieves a state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-off for training-free BSA on video diffusion models.

</details>


### [123] [Do Thinking VLMs Need to Keep Looking at Long Videos? Write It Down, Then Look Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24259)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Heng Wang,Chengqian Ma,WenHao Wang,Wenxuan Huang,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LookAway：让 VLM 先针对问题写下带时间戳的笔记，随后在推理引擎中隐藏视频 KV cache，从而在不损失精度的前提下大幅降低长视频推理的解码开销，并通过自蒸馏+强化学习进一步缩短"观看"阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频 VLM 推理时，每个解码步都要重读整段视频的 KV cache，代价高昂。作者观察到：混合注意力与纯注意力 VLM 对视频的依赖均集中在回答的早期阶段，因此"全程可见"可能是冗余的。

Method: 1) 推理阶段策略：模型先写出针对问题所需信息的带时间戳笔记，之后引擎丢弃/隐藏视频 KV cache，仅凭笔记继续推理；2) 对照实验验证笔记的必要性（替换为其他视频笔记有害、视频与自身笔记共存无显著增益）；3) 在模型自身正确的 LookAway 响应上微调以缩短初始观看阶段；4) 用强化学习同时奖励正确答案、惩罚 KV 读取。

Result: Qwen3.6-27B 上，笔记式推理在相同撤除步数下优于自由推理；免训练 LookAway 以约 0.12–0.13 倍建模解码成本接近全视频思考的精度。经微调+RL 后，模型中位数 66–68 个 token 即"收起"视频；在三个留出基准上，写出的 token 数多于同法训练的全视频模型，而在同等精度下仅读取 27–35% 的 KV 位置。

Conclusion: 长视频推理中的视频 KV 访问高度集中于早期，通过"笔记后丢弃视频"可显著降低解码成本且保持精度；结合自蒸馏与 KV 惩罚 RL 可进一步压缩观看阶段，为高效长视频 VLM 推理提供了一条免架构改动的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) reason about long videos over hundreds to thousands of tokens. Every decoding step re-reads the key-value (KV) cache of the entire video. Must the model keep looking while it reasons? In LookAway, the model first writes timestamped notes on what the question needs and then puts the video away. Once the notes close, the inference engine hides the video's KV cache for the rest of the response. Across both hybrid and pure-attention VLMs, dependence on the video is concentrated early in the response. On Qwen3.6-27B, writing notes preserves accuracy better than free reasoning at the same withdrawal step. Replacing them with another video's notes harms accuracy, whereas keeping the video visible alongside the model's own notes adds no significant benefit. Without additional training, LookAway nearly matches full-video thinking accuracy at 0.12-0.13 times its modeled decode cost. To shorten this initial look, we fine-tune the model on its own correct LookAway responses. Reinforcement learning then rewards correct answers while penalizing KV reads. The trained model puts the video away after a median of 66-68 tokens. Across three held-out benchmarks, it writes more tokens than an identically trained full-video model yet reads only 27-35% as many KV positions at matched accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [Look Closer, Look Back, or Wait for Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24267)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Wenxuan Huang,YiCheng Xiao,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActiveForager：一个面向流式视频问答的主动式智能体，保持干净的短时工作窗口，仅在需要时按需采集额外证据（高清放大、历史回忆、等待未来帧），并用反事实信息增益训练策略，在OVO-Bench和Video-Holmes上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频问答受严格内存与延迟约束。仅输入最近几帧可保持当前感知清晰，但加入历史帧会稀释对当前场景的注意力并引发幻觉。现有级联方法在不确定时才检索过去帧，但在当前帧模糊或决定性未来线索尚未出现时仍然失败。

Method: 提出ActiveForager流式视频智能体，维护干净的短时工作窗口，按需采集额外证据。对每个查询，智能体可选择：直接回答、放大高分辨率近期帧、从记忆中回忆过去事件、或等待未来帧。回忆历史时，由共享权重子智能体独立验证候选片段并返回紧凑证据摘要；再用流解耦注意力掩码（stream-decoupled attention mask）防止历史证据干扰当前视觉token。策略使用反事实信息增益训练，仅当采集证据使正确答案更可能时才奖励工具调用，避免工具坍缩。

Result: 在OVO-Bench上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B的实时感知、历史回忆和主动响应能力同时提升，Qwen3-VL-8B总体准确率从47.6%提升至65.9%；在多跳Video-Holmes基准上零样本准确率翻倍，且每次查询仅增加极少视觉token。

Conclusion: 按需采集证据的主动式流式视频智能体在严格内存与延迟约束下，可同时改善实时感知、历史回忆与前瞻响应，兼顾准确率与token效率。

Abstract: Streaming video models must answer questions about a live video under strict memory and latency limits. Feeding only the latest few frames keeps present perception sharp, whereas adding past frames dilutes attention on the current scene and triggers hallucinations. Recent cascade methods retrieve past frames when uncertain, but they still fail when current frames are blurry or when decisive future clues have not appeared yet. We introduce ActiveForager, a streaming video agent that maintains a clean short working window and gathers extra evidence only on demand. For each query, the agent either answers directly, zooms into high-resolution recent frames, recalls past events from memory, or waits for upcoming frames. When recalling history, a shared-weight sub-agent verifies candidate clips in isolation and returns a compact evidence summary. A stream-decoupled attention mask then keeps historical evidence from interfering with present visual tokens. We train this policy using counterfactual information gain, which rewards a tool call only when the gathered evidence makes the true answer more likely and prevents tool collapse across all actions. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B on OVO-Bench, ActiveForager improves real-time perception, historical recall, and proactive response at once, lifting overall accuracy on Qwen3-VL-8B from 47.6% to 65.9%. It also doubles zero-shot accuracy on the multi-hop Video-Holmes benchmark while adding minimal visual tokens per query.

</details>


### [125] [Semantic Guidance for Joint Infrared-Visible Video Fusion and Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24277)
*Yushen Xu,Xiaosong Li,Huaming Wu,Dongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAFI，用目标时刻语义引导红外与可见光视频的联合插值与融合，在帧率不一致/缺失时减少误差累积与亮度闪烁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高帧率红外-可见光视频融合需在相机帧率不同或低于目标帧率时恢复缺失观测；级联插值+融合会累积误差并引起亮度波动；运动与遮挡使内容选择困难，且缺失可见光帧会同时失去语义引导。

Method: 以目标时刻语义作为引导进行联合插值与融合：可见帧率高于红外时直接用可见帧提供语义；无可见观测时用运动引导局部匹配并由传播恢复语义。语义上下文与对齐后的图像特征互补，匹配置信度用于选择可靠的红外/可见信息；重建与融合在各尺度共享源选择，使缺失帧监督可指导融合。限制热增强幅度并对跨帧亮度不一致施加惩罚并考虑热对比变化。

Result: 在三个数据集上视觉质量提升、计算量低于级联基线；输入帧率不同时具有一致的时间增益；缺失帧下仍保持较强下游跟踪性能。

Conclusion: SAFI通过语义引导的联合插值-融合框架统一了缺失帧恢复与融合，兼顾效率、时序一致性与下游任务性能。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion at high frame rates requires recovering missing observations when camera rates differ or fall below the desired output rate. Cascading interpolation and fusion can accumulate errors and cause brightness fluctuations. Motion and occlusion further complicate content selection, particularly when missing visible frames also remove direct semantic guidance. We propose SAFI, which uses semantics at the target time to guide joint interpolation and fusion. When the visible frame rate exceeds the infrared rate, available visible frames provide semantic context directly. At times without visible observations, motion guides local matching, and propagation recovers semantics from neighboring frames. Semantic context complements aligned image features at the target time, while matching confidence helps select reliable infrared and visible information. Reconstruction and fusion share source selection within each scale, allowing supervision on missing frames to guide fusion. To preserve visible appearance and reduce flicker, we bound thermal enhancement and penalize brightness inconsistencies across frames while accounting for changes in thermal contrast. Experiments on three datasets show improved visual quality with less computation than cascaded baselines and consistent temporal gains when input rates differ. SAFI maintains strong downstream tracking performance despite missing frames. The code is available at https://github.com/XylonXu01/SAFI.

</details>


### [126] [CROMPT: Adapting CLIP via Cross-Modal Prompting for Robust Zero-Shot Generalization under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24284)
*Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Snehashis Dhibar,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CROMPT提示学习框架，用于训练时无目标域图像条件下的零样本域自适应语义分割，在无额外推理开销下取得1.63–2.63% mIoU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域自适应语义分割依赖目标域图像，零样本域自适应仅用目标域文本描述进行适配；但现有提示学习方法不适配基于ResNet的CLIP视觉主干，也难以实现像素级跨模态特征对齐。

Method: CROMPT包含：(1) 跨模态特征增强CROMA，将随机视觉提示与混合文本提示HTP结合，联合适配视觉与文本表征；(2) CROMA-VL，利用视觉-语言模型生成以域为条件的提示；(3) 渐进式由易到难的自适应策略以增强域不变性。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上相较强基线及SOTA方法取得稳定提升，mIoU增益1.63–2.63%，且推理复杂度不变。

Conclusion: CROMPT是面向零样本多域语义分割的有效且高效的框架；局限在于仅在两个基准上验证，且依赖文本描述与视觉-语言模型生成提示的质量。

Abstract: Domain adaptation for semantic segmentation is challenging when target-domain images are unavailable during training. Zero-shot domain adaptation addresses this by adapting models using only textual descriptions of the target domain. However, existing prompt-learning methods are not well suited for ResNet-based CLIP vision backbones or pixel-level cross-modal feature alignment. We propose CROMPT, a prompt-learning framework for zero-shot domain adaptation in semantic segmentation. CROMPT introduces Cross-Modal Feature Augmentation (CROMA), combining Random Visual Prompting with Hybrid Textual Prompting (HTP) to jointly adapt visual and textual representations. We further propose CROMA-VL, which leverages vision-language models to generate domain-conditioned prompts, and a progressive easy-to-hard adaptation strategy for improved domain invariance. Experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods, achieving 1.63-2.63% mIoU gains while maintaining inference-time complexity. These results establish CROMPT as an effective and efficient framework for zero-shot multi-domain semantic segmentation. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FxqFsIL685

</details>


### [127] [AnchorUp: Anchoring Feature Upsampling on a Classical Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24296)
*Kotaro Horikawa,Keisuke Toida,Junya Komori,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorUp 不再直接预测高分辨率特征图，而是以无参数的经典插值结果作为锚点，仅学习残差校正，用一个极轻量的引导网络加单标量门控完成融合，参数仅为 NAF 的四分之一，却在多个密集预测任务上与 NAF 持平或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在粗粒度 patch 网格上产生语义强的特征，而密集预测需要像素级分辨率。现有学习型上采样器通过交叉注意力从低分辨率特征回归高分辨率特征图，但作者观察到其中大部分内容无需学习：双线性插值已能复现平滑区域，近期基准甚至显示双线性/双三次插值与若干学习式上采样器相当，剩余部分主要是边缘与细节。这说明学习型上采样器将大量容量浪费在重复计算经典插值已能提供的平滑内容上。

Method: 提出 AnchorUp：保留一个无参数的经典上采样器（bilinear）作为锚点，由一个小型引导网络给出第二个预测，再通过单个可学习标量门控将两者融合；由于锚点已提供平滑内容，引导网络只需校正边缘与细节，因而可极其轻量（167,425 参数）。训练上只在 DINOv3 上执行 NAF 两阶段中的第一阶段。

Result: 相较最强基线 NAF，参数量约为其 1/4，延迟为其 64%。在 DINOv3 及六个留出骨干上评测，于 Pascal VOC 与 Cityscapes 上已与经完整两阶段训练的 NAF 持平；在 DAVIS-2017 视频目标分割上超越 NAF，且该增益在所有骨干上均成立，在轮廓精度 F 上提升更明显。对学习到的校正的可视化分析证实其集中于边缘与精细细节。

Conclusion: 将"预测高分辨率特征图"重构为"预测残差校正"是一种高效的上采样范式：经典插值承担平滑内容，神经网络专注边缘与细节，从而以极小模型取得与重型基线相当甚至更好的精度、更低的延迟，并具备良好的跨骨干泛化性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision foundation models produce semantically strong features on a coarse patch grid, and dense prediction needs them at pixel resolution. Existing learned upsamplers close that gap by regressing the high-resolution feature map from the low-resolution one, typically through cross-attention driven by the input image. We observe that most of what they regress needs no learning: plain bilinear interpolation already reproduces the smooth regions of the target, and recent benchmarks even report bilinear and bicubic on par with several learned upsamplers. The rest is mostly edges and fine details, which suggests that these learned upsamplers spend much of their capacity re-computing the smooth content that classical interpolation provides. We therefore introduce AnchorUp, which stops predicting the high-resolution feature map and predicts only its correction from the interpolation. It keeps a parameter-free classical upsampler as an anchor, obtains a second prediction from a small guidance network, and blends the two through a single learnable scalar gate. Because the anchor supplies the smooth content, the guidance network only has to correct what remains. An analysis of the learned correction confirms that it concentrates on edges and fine details, and so the network can be small: AnchorUp uses 167,425 parameters, a quarter of the budget of the strongest baseline, NAF, and runs at 64% of its latency. Trained once on DINOv3 with only the first of NAF's two training stages, and evaluated on that backbone plus six held-out ones, it is already on par with NAF trained through both stages on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes. Our method surpasses it on DAVIS-2017 video object segmentation; the gain holds on every backbone and is larger in contour accuracy F. Code is available at https://github.com/KotaroHorikawa/AnchorUp.

</details>


### [128] [Fuse to Recognize the Unseen: Class-Relative Infrared-Visible Fusion for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24316)
*Zehao Gao,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Junpeng Zhang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EmergeFuse，一种面向开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）的红外-可见光融合框架，通过"RGB锚定、类相对"的热线索选择，把互补热证据转化为对未见类别的语义纠正，而非直接引入IR破坏RGB-语言对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外融合虽能提升困难光照下的视觉感知，但CLIP类OVSS模型主要在RGB图像上预训练，直接引入IR会破坏RGB与语言的对齐，使热线索变成语义干扰，损害未见类识别。像素级RGB-IR语义分析显示：类相关热线索可纠正部分RGB误分类，同时保留可靠的RGB预测，因而需要"以RGB为锚、类相对"的热线索筛选。

Method: 1) 类相对线索选择：以RGB预测为参照，评估RGB-IR语义响应与校准后的热证据，通过竞争性选择生成类相对纠正请求；2) 文本对比图像校正：借助文本对比将所选请求转译为语义纠正方向，引导证据门控的特征更新，从而将互补热证据用于未见类识别。

Result: 在三个基准上，相较现有融合方法，未见类mIoU最高提升4.755%，严格互补转化率最高提升15.730%，同时保持有竞争力的融合质量。

Conclusion: 以RGB为锚、按类相对方式选择性引入热证据，可有效避免IR带来的语义干扰，将感知互补性转化为OVSS的语义增益，实现互补热证据向未见类语义纠正的转化。

Abstract: Visible-infrared image fusion leverages complementary thermal cues to enhance visual perception, particularly under challenging illumination. However, such perceptual complementarity does not readily translate into semantic gains in open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS), where CLIP-based models are predominantly pretrained on RGB imagery. Directly introducing IR information may instead disrupt RGB-language alignment, turning promising thermal cues into semantic interference that undermines unseen-class recognition. Our pixel-level RGB-IR semantic analysis reveals that class-relevant thermal cues can correct certain RGB misclassifications while preserving reliable RGB predictions, motivating RGB-anchored, class-relative thermal cue selection. Based on this insight, we propose EmergeFuse, an infrared-visible fusion framework for OVSS that selectively converts complementary thermal evidence into unseen-class semantic corrections. Specifically, class-relative cue selection evaluates RGB-IR semantic responses and calibrated thermal evidence relative to RGB predictions, generating class-relative correction requests through competitive selection. Text-contrast image rectification then translates these selected requests into semantic rectification directions through text contrasts, guiding evidence-gated feature updates to realize complementary thermal evidence for unseen-class recognition. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate gains of up to 4.755% in unseen-class mIoU and 15.730% in strict complementary conversion rate over existing fusion methods, while maintaining competitive fusion quality.

</details>


### [129] [Pixel2Perspective: A Benchmark for Multi-Modal and Multi-View Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24327)
*Aditya Gandhamal,Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pixel2Perspective基准，用于跨空中/地面视角、多传感器（含红外）与恶劣天气条件下的开放词汇语义分割鲁棒性评估，并提出MSRIQ不确定性量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI（无人机、自动驾驶）需在含未见物体的环境中可靠感知，而基于CLIP的OVSS模型在真实户外条件、恶劣天气、多视角下的跨域泛化能力不足，缺乏系统性评估手段。

Method: 构建多角度物体定位与开放词汇分割基准，覆盖空中与地面视角、多传感器模态（含RGB与红外）；采用经筛选的数据集涵盖多样户外条件与仿真到现实场景；评估CLIP等基础模型及先进OVSS模型；提出多源RGB-IR不确定性量化框架MSRIQ，联合建模偶然不确定性与认知不确定性。

Result: 提供跨视角、跨模态、跨域条件的系统性评估，揭示现有基础模型与OVSS模型在开放世界具身感知中的鲁棒性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 建立了以鲁棒性为导向的基准，为开放世界具身AI感知研究提供关键洞见与基础。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, such as drones and autonomous vehicles, improve navigation by enhancing perception in environments containing unseen objects. While Visual Language Models (VLMs) offer strong image understanding, their real-world application is underexplored. For effective deployment, open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) models, leveraging models like CLIP to assign pixel-level labels from text, must be robust to outdoor conditions, adverse weather, and varying viewpoints. Despite their potential, these models encounter significant challenges in cross-domain generalization, hindering their practical use in embodied AI applications. In this work, we present Pixel2Perspective, a benchmark for multi-angle object localization and open-vocabulary segmentation across both aerial and ground perspectives. This benchmark supports thorough evaluation across different viewpoints and sensor modalities. We assess foundational models like CLIP and advanced OVSS models using a curated dataset that spans varied outdoor conditions, sim-to-real scenarios, and multiple sensors, including infrared, to support the development of robust and generalizable perception systems. We also present a novel framework, Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification (MSRIQ), which jointly models aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties across RGB and infrared imagery. This approach supports robust evaluation of open-vocabulary segmentation models across diverse environmental conditions. Our work sets a robustness-driven benchmark, providing key insights and laying the foundation for future research in open-world embodied AI perception. The benchmark and project page are available at https://aniruddhsikdar1.github.io/publications/pixel2perspective/

</details>


### [130] [Can Weight-Space Programming Alter Learned Ethical Priorities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24330)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出"权重编辑"方法，检验能否选择性改变模型在"守承诺"与"惠及更多人"之间的取舍优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型内在的权衡机制是否可被定向编辑，从而区分对正式承诺与普通承诺的尊重程度，并验证此类选择性修改的可检验性。

Method: 在概念监督的文本模型中编辑拟合的选择权重，使推翻正式承诺所需的收益阈值高于普通承诺；在六个独立训练的文本阅读器上验证，并辅以分类器对照实验与YOLO11n（COCO128）偏置编辑试点。

Result: 编辑在100%的定向普通冲突与83.3%的定向正式冲突中按两种顺序均选中较大群体；保留原模型在小冲突上的成功率与事实准确率；剩余失败与受益人数字读取错误相关。分类器显示复用事实答案可通过对无条件选择测试但无法保留小差距承诺；YOLO11n小目标匹配由3/43提升至9/43，等价分数校准可复现保留的检测。

Conclusion: 探索性结果表明可在具备显式权衡机制的模型中检验对学习优先级的定向修改；该方法能否在结构更弱的语言模型中奏效仍待验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether a weight edit can alter how a model trades off honoring a commitment against benefiting more people. In a concept-supervised text model, we alter fitted choice weights so that the benefit required to override a formal pledge is larger than for an ordinary promise. Across six independently trained text readers, the edit selects the larger group in both orders on 100% of targeted ordinary conflicts and 83.3% of targeted formal conflicts. It preserves the source model's success rates on smaller conflicts and its factual accuracy. The remaining formal-conflict failures coincide with errors reading a beneficiary number. A separate classifier demonstrates why this distinction matters: reusing a factual answer passes an unconditional choice test but fails to preserve small-gap promises. In a YOLO11n pilot on COCO128, a bias edit increased small-target matches from 3/43 to 9/43, and an equivalent score calibration reproduced the retained detections. These exploratory results show a way to test selective changes to learned priorities in a model with an exposed trade-off mechanism. Whether the method can discover such changes in less structured language models remains open.

</details>


### [131] [OCTR2A: Real-Time RGB-to-BEV Trajectory Projection and Forecasting at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24352)
*Emanuele Iacobelli,Simone Teglia,Francesco Pro,Claudia Melis Tonti,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OCTR2A，仅用对角落 RGB 相机完成 BEV 轨迹重建与预测，并构建合成基准 R3ST++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交叉口轨迹预测研究默认已有可靠 BEV 运动历史，但实际获取需 LiDAR、航拍、高清地图或标定设施等高成本资源，本文研究这一上游感知到预测的问题。

Method: 模块化级联流水线：单视角检测+类别精修、几何跨相机匹配、Transformer-based BEV 投影、自中心 GRU 预测；以软多尺度空间重叠损失为主要监督。构建基于 Blender 与 SIND 轨迹的合成基准 R3ST++（同步对角落视图、跨视图对应、BEV 真值），兼作站点微调模拟器。

Result: 10 Hz 下观测 12 帧、预测 12 帧，投影-预测 ADE/FDE 为 0.144/0.140–0.154/0.241 米，单消费级 GPU 实时；给定真值 BEV 历史时预测模块在 SIND 上较最强 SOTA 提升 ADE 66.7%、FDE 41.7%；TUMTraf-V2X 迁移实验表明 R3ST++ 预训练可适配不同传感配置的真实路侧域。

Conclusion: 纯 RGB 对角落相机即可端到端实现 BEV 轨迹重建与预测，性能高且实时，合成预训练可迁移至真实路侧场景。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting at urban intersections is often studied assuming that reliable bird's-eye-view (BEV) motion histories are available, which in practice requires costly resources such as LiDAR, aerial views, HD maps, or calibrated infrastructure. We address this upstream perception-to-forecasting problem by reconstructing and predicting agents' BEV trajectories only from paired opposite-corner RGB cameras mounted on existing urban infrastructure at variable heights at inference time. We introduce the Opposite-Corner Trajectory Reconstruction and Anticipation Architecture (OCTR2A), a modular pipeline integrating per-view detection with class refinement, geometric cross-camera matching, transformer-based BEV projection, and egocentric GRU forecasting, trained in cascade and mainly supervised with a soft multi-scale spatial-overlap loss. Since no public benchmark provides this sensing configuration, we introduce R3ST++, a Blender-based synthetic benchmark built from SIND trajectories that provides synchronized opposite-corner views, cross-view correspondences, and BEV ground truth, while also serving as a deployment-support simulator for site-specific fine-tuning. Experiments on these datasets show that, with 12 observed and 12 predicted frames at 10\,Hz, OCTR2A achieves projection~-~forecasting ADE/FDE of 0.144/0.140~-~0.154/0.241~meters in real time on a single consumer GPU. Evaluated on ground-truth BEV histories, the forecasting module improves over the strongest state-of-the-art methods on SIND by 66.7\%/41.7\% ADE/FDE. Transfer experiments on TUMTraf-V2X show that R3ST++ pretraining supports adaptation to real-world roadside domains with different sensing configurations.

</details>


### [132] [SCOUT: Risk-Guided Foundation-Model Escalation for Robust Tissue Detection in Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24366)
*Cristian Tommasino,Antonio M. Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出风险引导级联方法SCOUT，用轻量审计网络定位分割错误并选择性调用冻结病理基础模型精修组织掩膜，配合非对称共识规则与校准的切片级复核分数，在公开多源多染色基准上将需修正病例组比例从38.1%降至18.6%，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像分析依赖可靠的组织检测，但高平均分割精度会掩盖浅淡组织、小碎片和染色异质性导致的失败；病理基础模型虽表征可迁移，却存在全量推理计算开销大且易产生假阳性组织预测的问题。

Method: SCOUT为风险引导级联：以缩略图生成初始组织掩膜；轻量审计网络估计分割误差的空间分布并对候选区域排序；仅将高优先级区域送入冻结的病理基础模型推理；用非对称共识规则将新预测与初始掩膜融合，以补回漏检组织同时抑制假阳性；另设经校准的切片级复核分数，在标定假设下控制未被标记的病例组失败边际率。

Result: 在公开多源、多染色基准上，需掩膜修正的病例组比例由最佳已发布检测器的38.1%降至18.6%，免疫组化改善尤为显著；源域留出实验显示其泛化性优于用相同标注训练的分割网络；选择性推理的实测失败率不高于处理全部候选区域；下游淋巴结分类性能与三个已发布检测器中的两个相当（非劣）。

Conclusion: 风险引导的基础模型推理是应对异质病理数据组织检测的有效范式；基于开放许可基础模型的变体已作为可安装Python包及训练权重公开发布。

Abstract: Reliable tissue detection is essential for whole-slide image analysis, yet high average segmentation accuracy can conceal failures involving pale tissue, small fragments, and heterogeneous staining. Pathology foundation models offer transferable representations, but exhaustive inference is computationally demanding and may introduce false-positive tissue predictions. We propose SCOUT (Selective Correction of Uncertain Tissue), a risk-guided cascade that selectively refines an initial thumbnail-based tissue mask. A lightweight audit network estimates the spatial distribution of segmentation errors and prioritizes candidate regions for processing by a frozen pathology foundation model. An asymmetric consensus rule integrates the resulting predictions with the initial mask to recover missed tissue while limiting false positives. A calibrated slide-level review score additionally controls the marginal rate of unflagged case-group failures under the calibration assumptions. Evaluation on a public multi-source, multi-stain benchmark shows that SCOUT reduces the proportion of case groups requiring mask correction from 38.1% for the best released detector to 18.6%, with particularly pronounced improvements in immunohistochemistry. Source-held-out experiments demonstrate better generalization than a segmentation network trained on the same annotations, while selective inference achieves an observed failure rate no higher than processing all candidate regions. Downstream, lymph-node classification with SCOUT masks is non-inferior to that obtained with two of the three released detectors evaluated. A variant built on an openly licensed foundation model is publicly released as an installable Python package with trained weights. These results support risk-guided foundation-model inference as an effective approach to tissue detection across heterogeneous histopathology data.

</details>


### [133] [One Affinity, Two Axes: Bidirectional Slot Attention for Iterative Aggregation across the Structure--Detail Spectrum in Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24371)
*Zihang Wang,Wenkai Zhu,Lingdong Kong,Teng Yan,Alan Liang,Shuyuan Li,Xieyuanli Chen,Dong Kong,Guanyu Zong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向槽注意力（BiSA），将图像块聚合法重构为"竞争—选择"双轴耦合的迭代系统，配合渐进式Token过滤（PTF）实现自增强的聚合与净化，在三类检索任务上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型提供丰富的局部特征，但现有聚合头依赖单次映射，缺乏显式协调结构性与细粒度线索的机制，难以同时获得非冗余的场景分解与多粒度特化表征。

Method: BiSA 将聚合视为迭代耦合系统，核心为跨轴共识（CAC）：基于单一共享的 token–slot 亲和度，在竞争轴上以全局共享温度让槽位争夺 token，决定归属并诱导非冗余分解；在选择轴上以槽位专属温度控制响应广度，覆盖从整体布局到稀疏细粒度细节的谱系，两轴共识实现分解与特化的耦合。同时两轴零成本暴露 token 可靠性线索（竞争下的"主张强度"与选择下的"显著性"），渐进式Token过滤（PTF）将其转化为保留概率，在下一轮迭代前衰减不可靠 token，无需分割分支或外部先验，形成"竞争分解—选择性聚焦—反馈净化"的闭环。

Result: 地点识别六个基准中五个领先（Baidu Mall R@1 +4.2）；无人机—卫星地理定位全部16项指标最优（150 m 处 R@1 +8.4）；ROxford-Hard 实例检索 mAP +4.5。消融显示选择轴仅增加20个可学习标量，却带来平均 R@1 +2.8。

Conclusion: 结构性与细粒度线索的显式双轴耦合是提升全局描述子判别力的关键，且可靠性驱动的 token 过滤可低成本自增强聚合过程。

Abstract: Aggregating dense patch tokens into compact and discriminative global descriptors is central to visual retrieval. Although foundation models provide rich local features, existing aggregation heads still rely on single-pass mappings and lack an explicit mechanism to coordinate structural and fine-grained cues. Therefore, we propose Bidirectional Slot Attention (BiSA), which reformulates aggregation as an iterative coupled system. Cross-Axis Consensus (CAC) progressively refines learnable slots by interpreting a single shared token--slot affinity along two complementary axes. Along the competition axis, slots vie for each token under a temperature shared across all of them, determining token ownership and inducing a non-redundant scene decomposition. Along the selection axis, a slot-specific temperature governs the breadth of each slot's response, spanning a spectrum from broad layouts to sparse fine-grained details. Their consensus couples non-redundant decomposition with multi-granular slot specialization. Furthermore, the two axes expose intrinsic token-reliability cues at no extra cost: a token's claim strength under competition and its salience under selection. Progressive Token Filtering (PTF) converts these cues into a retention probability that down-weights unreliable tokens before the next CAC iteration, without any segmentation branch or external prior, forming a self-reinforcing loop of competitive decomposition, selective focusing, and feedback purification. Across all three retrieval tasks, BiSA leads on five of six place recognition benchmarks (+4.2 R@1 on Baidu Mall), tops all 16 metrics of drone--satellite geo-localization (+8.4 R@1 at 150 m), and improves instance-level retrieval on ROxford-Hard by 4.5 mAP points. Notably, the selection axis adds only 20 learnable scalars, yet improves average R@1 by 2.8 points.

</details>


### [134] [CanopyKV: Remember beyond the Crop for Fast Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24373)
*Lei Yang,Dayan Wu,Yiliang Zhu,Xinze Liu,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Ding Wang,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练KV压缩框架CanopyKV，利用父子裁剪关系保留互补证据，在多个基准上实现精度与端到端效率双优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉推理需迭代裁剪图像并调用工具，导致 cropping 与推理 token 累积使KV缓存膨胀。现有KV压缩方法基于注意力、时效性或冗余性选 token，无法区分"被局部替换的旧证据"与"未来探索仍需要的互补信息"，丢弃会损伤精度或触发额外搜索，而评分与缓存重排又会抵消压缩收益。

Method: CanopyKV为免训练框架：1）利用父子裁剪关系（parent-child crop relationships）保留互补证据；2）保持已保留历史的稳定性（stable retained history）；3）保护持续推理所需的状态；4）以工具元数据提供保留信号，无需额外注意力打分；5）采用紧凑KV存储降低后续注意力开销。

Result: 在 VisualProbe、V*、HR-Bench、MME-Lite 上，所评估的KV压缩方法中取得SOTA答案精度与端到端效率。MME-Lite上仅保留约Dense总KV的四分之一，精度提升10.5个百分点，完整推理加速1.64×。

Conclusion: 以裁剪层级关系为结构先验进行KV压缩，可在无需训练、无额外注意力评分的情况下兼顾证据完整性与计算效率，验证了结构化保留策略在主动视觉推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Active visual reasoning enables multimodal models to answer challenging visual questions by gathering fine-grained evidence through iterative image inspection and tool use. However, accumulating crops and reasoning tokens expands the key-value (KV) cache, making compression important for reducing memory and inference costs. Existing KV compression methods typically select historical tokens based on attention, recency, or redundancy. These criteria do not explicitly distinguish locally replaced evidence from complementary information needed for future exploration: each crop refreshes a local region, while earlier views may still support subsequent decisions. Discarding such evidence can impair accuracy or require additional search, while scoring and cache reorganization can offset the computational savings from compression. We introduce CanopyKV, a training-free KV compression framework that uses parent-child crop relationships to retain complementary evidence, keeps retained history stable, and protects the state needed for ongoing reasoning. Tool metadata provides retention signals without additional attention scoring, while compact KV storage reduces subsequent attention costs. Across VisualProbe, V*, HR-Bench, and MME-Lite, CanopyKV achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and end-to-end efficiency among the evaluated KV compression methods. On MME-Lite, it retains approximately one-quarter of Dense's total KV while improving accuracy by 10.5 percentage points and accelerating complete reasoning by $1.64\times$.

</details>


### [135] [Where Should a Diffusion Transformer Meet Its Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24380)
*Zhuoqi Hu,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Yihao Ouyang,Wenting Shen,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Minyi Guo,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaREPA，通过测量流匹配目标与对齐目标之间的梯度兼容性，在线自适应选择REPA监督层，加速DiT训练并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REPA方法将预训练视觉教师的监督固定绑定到单一中间层，但学生表征随训练演化，最有效对齐层在不同训练阶段会变化；穷举搜索代价高昂，因此需要在线追踪层适用性。

Method: 测量流匹配损失与对齐损失之间的梯度兼容性作为层适配度分数，经平滑后做softmax得到各层的概率分布，每个训练步按该分布采样一个层施加教师对齐监督（概率路由）。额外训练开销极小，推理阶段无额外成本。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，跨不同模型规模均加速收敛并提升生成质量；同时揭示了训练过程中最适宜监督层会发生迁移的现象。

Conclusion: 层选择不应固定，基于梯度兼容性的在线概率路由是一种低开销、可解释的对齐层调度策略，为REPA类方法的监督位置设计提供新视角。

Abstract: Representation Alignment (REPA) significantly accelerates Diffusion Transformer training by guiding intermediate student features with a pretrained visual teacher, yet existing methods typically attach supervision to a single fixed layer. As student representations evolve, however, we find that the most effective layer for alignment varies across training stages. To track this evolving layer suitability online without costly search, we measure gradient compatibility between the flow-matching and alignment objectives, and propose AdaREPA, an online routing framework that adaptively selects the supervision layer as training progresses. AdaREPA uses a softmax distribution over smoothed layer compatibility scores to sample a layer for alignment at each training step. This probabilistic routing adapts teacher supervision to the student's changing learning needs, with minimal training overhead and no additional inference cost. On ImageNet-256, AdaREPA accelerates convergence and improves generation quality across model sizes, while revealing how the most suitable layers for supervision shift over training.

</details>


### [136] [Enhancing Active Learning Performance with Initial Data Selection Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24384)
*Mohammad Mirzaee,Mehrshad Fallah Estalkhzir,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向目标检测主动学习初始轮次优化的框架：用YOLO-World特征聚类结合objectness分数筛选图像，后续轮次采用一致性采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习初始标注池的选择至关重要，尤其在标注昂贵、资源预算低的复杂数据集上；传统方法在初始轮次依赖不确定性采样效果有限。

Method: 基于YOLO-World模型提取特征并进行特征聚类，按objectness分数对图像进行精炼筛选，优先选取特征多样且基础模型高置信的样本；后续轮次采用基于一致性的采样策略维持训练稳健性。

Result: 在Aquarium与NWPU VHR-10两个复杂数据集上评估；NWPU上图像级mAP50平均提升6.6%，图像精炼方法在目标级评估提升约11%，并缩短标注时间。

Conclusion: 利用基础模型在主动学习早期轮次可同时提升模型性能与降低标注成本，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: In active object detection, selecting the initial labeled pool is essential, especially in complex datasets with expensive labeling and low resource budget. This study presents an active learning framework designed to optimize the initial training cycle of active learning for the object detection task. Our approach diverges from traditional methods by utilizing feature-based clustering from the YOLO-World model and refining images based on objectness scores rather than relying on uncertainty sampling in the initial cycle. This approach focuses on finding samples with various features and emphasizes training in areas where the foundation model is most confident. Doing so helps the model better understand different types of objects. For subsequent cycles, we employ a consistency-based sampling technique to maintain the robustness of the training process. To evaluate our method, we use two complex datasets called Aquarium and NWPU VHR-10, which are well known for their diverse object sizes and challenging conditions. In the NWPU dataset, our method increases mAP50 by an average of 6.6% in image-based evaluation. Moreover, the image refinement method shows an improvement of approximately 11% in object-based evaluation. The results show improvements in model performance and labeling time, demonstrating the advantages of using foundation models in early cycles of active learning. An implementation of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/Mhrshd-fllh/ALBoost-FM

</details>


### [137] [Reliable Tree Recruitment from Scarce Annotations for Forest Inventory Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24401)
*Luiz Fernando Souza Luz,Pedro J. Soto Vega,Matheus Pinheiro Ferreira,Laura Elena Cué la Rosa,Hudson Franklin Pessoa Veras,Ernandes Macedo da Cunha Neto,Evandro Orfanó Figueiredo,Ana Paula Dalla Corte,Carlos Roberto Sanquetta,José Matheus Segre Moneva Viveiros,Matheus Santos Fuza,Heitor Eduardo Ferreira Campos Morato Filpi,Cristiano Vinícius Vidal,Vinícius Moura Costa,Débora Nascimento Rosário,Danilo Roberti Alves de Almeida,Paulo Guilherme Molin,Dário Augusto Borges Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多任务DeepLabV3迭代自训练的单木树冠物种清单扩充框架，仅用少量标注树冠即可在UAV正射影像上显著扩充各物种样本并提升树冠级F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林单木树冠（ITC）物种级清查受标注成本限制，许多物种仅有极少量野外验证树冠，难以支撑监督学习模型。

Method: 构建多任务DeepLabV3网络，将语义分割与树冠距离回归头联合训练；迭代自训练循环中，候选树冠须同时通过类别概率与距离图的置信度滤波，以及树冠面积与实心度（solidity）的形态学滤波，且每物种最多新增5个树冠，以保证招募样本与参考清单一致。在巴西两个样地（Acre亚马逊雨林：17种、355个标注树冠、4 cm GSD；Maranhão塞拉多：4种、51个标注树冠、1.8 cm GSD）的RGB正射影像上验证。

Result: 20轮迭代后，保留树冠数在Acre由约500增至960，Maranhão由约700增至3100以上；树冠级F1优于单任务与多任务DeepLabV3基线（53.5% vs. 50.0%与45.2%；92.2% vs. 79.7%与83.6%）。

Conclusion: 该框架为使用消费级UAV与有限野外工作低成本扩充物种清单提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Species-level inventories of individual tree crowns (ITCs) from UAV imagery are limited by annotation cost: in tropical forests, many species are represented by only a handful of field-validated crowns.
