The benchmark evaluates whether models can jointly recognize aggressive actions, distinguish aggressors from victims and bystanders, and correctly bind actions to participants under challenging distractor settings. We benchmark 17 multimodal models and find that they struggle substantially on fine-grained aggressive-scene understanding. Performance degrades consistently as reasoning complexity increases, with models frequently confusing aggressors with victims or bystanders, revealing a systematic failure in role-action association rather than coarse aggression recognition alone. We further observe that increasing model scale or generic reasoning models experience limited improvement. Finally, we explore a simple role-graph prompting strategy that encourages structured reasoning over participant interactions, leading to improvements in compositional reasoning. Our findings highlight that reliable aggressive-scene understanding remains a major challenge for current MLLMs and motivate future research on role-aware video reasoning beyond coarse violence detection. Code and dataset: https://github.com/austincbaker/A3Bench.

</details>


### [173] [Read Together, Answer Apart: How Earlier Questions Help Vision-Language Models Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24785)
*Xinyi Guo,Kaile Huang,Xitong Ling,Mingyi He,Chende Zheng,Jianyu Xu,Chao He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文细粒度追踪同一图像多问题共问(co-asking)下每个答案的变化，揭示"修复错误"与"引入新错"两种相反效应，并结合注意力读出与层窗口干预解释其机制，提出免训练解码掩码"Read Together, Answer Apart"提升共问准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅在提示层面报告共问带来的准确率增益与幻觉等互相矛盾的结论，未追踪单个答案如何变化，因而无法解释冲突结果，也缺乏对哪些模型、哪些层的干预能改善共问的机制性理解。

Method: 在22个开放权重VLM（1B–35B）上配对比较每题的单问与共问答案；使用注意力读出与层窗口干预（14个支持自定义注意力掩码的模型）定位视觉信息注入机制；提出免训练解码掩码Read Together, Answer Apart，保留共享prefill而隔离各答案对其他问题stem的注意力。

Result: 在DCI上所有模型修复15.4%–29.8%的单问错误，正确转错比例从弱模型30.8%降至强模型4.7%；14个模型中屏蔽前序问题对图像的注意力均降低目标定位与准确率；新方法在DCI上改善14个模型中的12个，最高+4.71个百分点、弥合合并差距的70%，并在WebUI、FinTabNet、GQA上分别改善10、8、5个模型，最高+11.4、+5.3、+1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 共问的净效应是错误修复与正确受损两股相反力量的叠加：跨问题帮助发生在共享问题阅读阶段（增强图像注意力与目标定位），共问损害则部分发生于答案解码阶段。据此在解码阶段隔离答案可在不训练的情况下稳定提升共问表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked several questions about the same image in a single prompt (co-asking), yet existing evaluations report conflicting accuracy gains and hallucinations at the prompt level without tracking how individual answers change. By pairing each question's single-question and co-asked answers across 22 open-weight VLMs (1B-35B), we find that erratic net shifts reflect two opposing effects: on Densely Captioned Images (DCI), every model repairs 15.4%-29.8% of its single-question errors under co-asking, while the share of correct answers turned wrong falls from 30.8% on weaker models to 4.7% on stronger ones. Across all 14 models supporting custom attention masks, we show through attention readouts and layer-window interventions that earlier same-image questions read visual information into their question tokens in the early-to-middle layers during prefill, helping later questions concentrate image attention onto their queried targets. This visual help accumulates across the scene even across non-overlapping regions, whereas blocking earlier questions from attending to the image lowers both target localization and accuracy in all 14 models. While cross-question help is established during shared question reading, part of the co-asking damage enters later during answer decoding when the decoder attends to other question stems. We therefore introduce Read Together, Answer Apart, a training-free decoding mask that keeps shared prefill intact and isolates each answer from other stems, improving co-asking accuracy on 12 of 14 models on DCI (up to +4.71 percentage points, closing 70% of the pooled gap to asking alone) and on 10, 8, and 5 of the 14 models on WebUI, FinTabNet, and GQA (up to +11.4, +5.3, and +1.1 points).

</details>


### [174] [Training-free motion statistics match self-supervised behavior encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24792)
*Ziwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文以预注册协议公平比较"免训练的手工特征表示"（逐帧通用测量+固定时间窗汇总+PCA降维）与重训练及已发布的自监督行为编码器（BAMS、hBehaveMAE），在等维度冻结表征、共享同一分类器的条件下，发现简单基线在多个基准上普遍优于学习到的编码器，呼吁将其作为必需基线报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物行为的自监督编码器通常以手工运动特征为训练输入或馈入数据，但作者质疑其是否在公平条件下真正优于这些"原料"特征。现有工作缺乏等维度、共享分类器、预注册的受控对照，导致编码器优越性可能被高估。

Method: 提出免训练表征：对通用逐帧测量值在固定时间窗上汇总，再用PCA降维。与重训练及已发布的BAMS、hBehaveMAE编码器进行对比，遵循预注册协议：冻结表征、维度对齐、单一共享分类器；并用三个随机种子重训练编码器检验稳健性；在CalMS21、MABe22小鼠三体、MABe22果蝇群体、Shot7M2合成篮球基准上评测；另做消融，考察短时间窗贡献及"各身体本体坐标系统下原始关键点+同窗汇总"的可替代性。

Result: CalMS21上达85.8 macro F1（最佳编码器82.4），与四个标注视频持平，在标注者变化或仅需少量视频学习新行为时领先；MABe22小鼠三体83.7 vs hBehaveMAE 80.3（+3.4，95%区间+2.1~+4.7）；MABe22果蝇群体50任务平均F1 51.6 vs BAMS 48.2；Shot7M2 68.8 vs hBehaveMAE 64.7（但后者在最短动作上仍更优）。短时间窗贡献主要结果；本体坐标系原始关键点在两个小鼠数据集与Shot7M2上已可匹敌或超越编码器，仅在果蝇群体上落后，此时加入测量值提升5.3分。

Conclusion: 在这些基准上，当前行为编码器尚未超越其所学习的输入——手工构造特征；免训练的简单基线应作为标准对照与编码器并列报告，以客观评估自监督表征的实际增益。

Abstract: Self-supervised encoders for animal behavior are often trained on, or fed, hand-built movement features. We ask whether they beat those ingredients when compared fairly. A training-free representation, made of generic per-frame measurements summarized over fixed time windows and reduced with PCA, is compared with retrained and released encoders (BAMS, hBehaveMAE) under a preregistered protocol: frozen representations of equal size and one shared classifier. On CalMS21 it reaches 85.8 macro F1 against 82.4 for the best encoder, ties with four labelled videos, and leads when the annotator changes or new behaviors must be learned from a few videos; the reading holds for encoders retrained with three seeds. On the MABe22 mouse triplets it scores 83.7 against 80.3 for the released hBehaveMAE (gap +3.4, 95% interval +2.1 to +4.7). On a second species, the MABe22 fly groups, the same recipe scores 51.6 mean F1 over 50 tasks against 48.2 for BAMS, the best published method. On Shot7M2, the synthetic basketball benchmark built by hBehaveMAE's authors, it scores 68.8 against 64.7 for hBehaveMAE, the best method in that paper, though hBehaveMAE remains better on the shortest movements. Short time windows carry most of the result. Raw keypoints in each body's own frame, summarized over the same windows, already match or beat the encoders on both mouse datasets and on Shot7M2; on the fly groups they fall short, and the measurements add 5.3 points. On these benchmarks, current behavior encoders have yet to beat the hand-built features they learn from, and such baselines should be reported alongside them.

</details>


### [175] [The Elephant in the Question: Unified Models Picture the Concepts Behind Their Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24793)
*Tobias Braun,Marcus Rohrbach,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 V-lens，无需训练解码器即可借助统一多模态模型的图像生成器可视化指定 token 的隐藏状态，并通过 lens tuning 用图像监督隐藏状态，大幅提升指代可视化正确率并间接改善文本答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化中间表示有助于检查模型输出背后的推理步骤。统一多模态模型在同一架构中结合理解与生成，其图像生成器可访问文本处理时形成的表示，为免解码器的表示可视化提供了天然接口。

Method: V-lens：直接利用统一模型中图像生成器对文本侧隐藏状态的访问通路，将选定 token 的隐藏状态"翻译"为图像，不额外训练解码器。在 4 个扩散类与 2 个纯自回归统一模型上验证，并进行控制实验考察上下文、token 位置与层深对可视化内容的影响。进一步提出 lens tuning：用图片经由 V-lens 监督选定的隐藏状态。

Result: 可视化图像揭示了连接问题与答案的关键中间概念，即使这些概念从未被显式命名；控制实验显示内容随上下文、token 位置与深度变化。经 lens tuning 后，BAGEL、SenseNova-U1、HunyuanImage-3.0 上代词指代对象的可视化正确率从 2–11% 提升至 86–94%；且该视觉监督在未直接监督答案 token 的情况下也提升了文本答案质量。

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型的生成通路可作为一种免训练的可视化探针，兼具可解释性分析价值；图像形式的外部监督能有效塑形隐藏表示，并对语言输出产生跨模态的正向迁移。

Abstract: Visualizing intermediate representations could help us inspect the reasoning steps underlying a model output. Unified multimodal models offer this opportunity by combining understanding and generation in one architecture. Their image generators can access representations formed while processing text. We introduce the V-lens, which uses this connection to visualize a chosen token's hidden state without training a decoder. Across four diffusion-based and two purely autoregressive unified models, the resulting images reveal key intermediate concepts that connect a question to its answer even when the concepts are never named explicitly. Controlled tests show how this content changes with context, token position, and depth. We then introduce lens tuning, which supervises selected hidden states with pictures through the V-lens. Across BAGEL, SenseNova-U1, and HunyuanImage-3.0, the rate of correct visualizations of pronoun referents rises from 2-11% to 86-94%. Surprisingly, such visual supervision also improves text answers without supervising the answer tokens directly. Code is available at https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/V-lens.

</details>


### [176] [Ultimate3D: Teaching Multimodal LLMs 3D Camera-Object Relations by Large-Scale Visual Instruction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24795)
*Liu He,Yizhi Song,Ruiqi Xu,Baris Sarper Tezcan,Xiao Zeng,Albert Y. C. Chen,Lu Xia,Shashwat Verma,Sankalp Dayal,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Daniel Aliaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于3D资产的合成生成流水线，构建大规模3D接地的2D视觉指令数据集Ultimate3D，显著提升MLLM对相机-物体关系（朝向、视角、镜头）的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）难以准确捕捉相机与物体之间的关系（尤其是物体朝向、相机视角、相机镜头），根源在于训练图像中相机-物体关系多样性不足，且缺乏对应的文本描述。因此需要可精确控制关系的大规模数据。

Method: 构建合成数据生成流水线：以3D资产为输入，利用渲染与基于扩散的图像生成模型产出保持精确相机-物体关系的照片级真实图像；同时用LLM生成文本提示，用于引导视觉指令微调与控制图像生成。据此构建Ultimate3D数据集，包含24万条VQA，配有精确的相机-物体标注及对应基准。

Result: 在该数据集上微调的MLLM大幅超越商业模型，在相机-物体关系识别任务上平均准确率提升29.7%。

Conclusion: 精确可控的3D合成数据可有效弥补真实图像中相机-物体关系多样性不足的问题，为MLLM的广泛视觉应用提供数据与评测基础；代码、数据集与基准将公开。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) struggle with accurately capturing camera-object relations, especially for object orientation, camera viewpoint, and camera shots. This stems from the fact that existing MLLMs are trained on images with limited diverse camera-object relations and corresponding textual descriptions. To address this, we propose a synthetic generation pipeline to create large-scale 3D visual instruction datasets. Our framework takes 3D assets as input and uses rendering and diffusion-based image generation models to create photorealistic images preserving precise camera-object relations. Additionally, large language models (LLMs) are used to generate text prompts for guiding visual instruction tuning and controlling image generation. We create Ultimate3D, a large-scale 3D-grounded 2D visual instruction dataset. Ultimate3D consists of 240K VQAs with precise camera-object annotations, and corresponding benchmark. MLLMs fine-tuned on our proposed dataset outperform commercial models by a large margin, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 29.7% on camera-object relation recognition tasks. Our code, dataset, and benchmark will contribute to broad MLLM applications.

</details>


### [177] [Edge-Efficient Joint RAW Image Compression and Restoration for Smart Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24805)
*Yunong Sun,Sicheng Chen,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出非对称的RAW联合压缩与复原框架CRAW：端侧轻量编码器将含噪RAW压缩为比特流，后端高容量解码器联合去噪与去马赛克，借知识蒸馏压缩编码器算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜等穿戴设备的紧凑传感器产生含噪RAW图像，本地算力与功耗受限，难以高质量端侧复原。现有两条路线各有缺陷：直接卸载复原导致RAW传输开销大；先压缩再传输则比特率被用于保留传感器噪声。需同时降低本地计算与传输成本且不损图像质量。

Method: 非对称框架：轻量端侧编码器将含噪RAW压缩为比特流；高容量后端解码器对编码表示联合去噪与去马赛克，使传输表示直接面向干净图像重建优化；并用知识蒸馏训练更小的编码器以适应更紧的算力预算。

Result: 在真实与合成数据上，率-复原性能优于强级联压缩-复原流水线及学习式压缩基线；SIDD上约0.25 bpp时PSNR较级联基线提升1.5 dB以上。用商用智能眼镜采集的12 MP真实RAW验证有效性，并测手机与眼镜端发送侧延迟：256×256输出块上，蒸馏编码器相较端侧NAFNet获27.6× NPU推理加速。

Conclusion: 联合优化传输表示与复原目标可缓解“噪声占比特率”与“RAW传输昂贵”的根本失配，在端侧低算力、低带宽条件下实现高质量成像，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Smart wearable devices, such as augmented reality glasses, increasingly rely on cameras to understand and interact with their surroundings. However, their compact sensors often capture noisy RAW images, while limited compute and power make high-quality on-device restoration difficult. This creates an emerging need to reduce both local computation and transmission cost without sacrificing image quality. Two common solutions are to offload restoration or to compress the raw data before transmission. The first makes RAW transmission costly; the second reduces the payload but can spend bitrate preserving sensor noise. We address this fundamental mismatch with an asymmetric framework for joint RAW compression and restoration that directly optimizes the transmitted representation for clean image reconstruction. A lightweight on-device encoder compresses noisy RAW measurements into a bitstream, while a higher-capacity backend decoder jointly denoises and demosaics the encoded representation. We use knowledge distillation to train a smaller encoder for tighter compute budgets. Experiments on real and synthetic data show improved rate-restoration performance over strong cascaded compression-restoration pipelines and learned compression baselines. On SIDD, our method improves PSNR by more than 1.5 dB over cascaded baselines at approximately 0.25 bits per pixel. We further validate the method on real 12 MP RAW images captured with commercial smart glasses and measure sender-side latency on both smartphone and smart-glasses hardware. The distilled encoder achieves a 27.6$\times$ NPU inference speedup over on-device NAFNet for 256$\times$256 output patches. The code is available at https://github.com/coolsyn2000/CRAW

</details>


### [178] [ReCoCo: From Patches to Compositions for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24806)
*Juan Gutiérrez,Saman Zadtootaghaj,José Luis Blanco-Murillo,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReCoCo（Regional Composition Consistency），将跨视图一致性从图像级扩展到区域级：用patch级原型预测的组合表示两个增强视图间共享的内容，对齐这些组合即可在无需对应关系或语义分组的前提下学习区域级稠密特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在图像级表示上成效显著，但稠密（局部）特征学习仍困难。将视图间一致性用于局部内容并不直接：现有方法多在视图间匹配单个patch或识别连贯区域，从而引入对应关系估计的不确定性与对视觉内容应如何分组的先验假设。

Method: ReCoCo 通过"组合"方式刻画两个增强视图间共享的内容——即在学习到的视觉原型上对patch级预测进行组合（区域组合），并对齐这些组合。因此模型可从包含多个视觉概念的区域中学习，而无需事先分离这些概念。该方法自然融入teacher-student自蒸馏框架，且不引入额外可学习参数。

Result: 大量实验显示：在冻结特征的分割、聚类与区域检索任务上取得显著提升，同时保持有竞争力的图像级识别性能，缓解了稠密表示与全局表示质量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 跨视图一致性可推广到区域层面，且无需显式patch对应或语义分组；ReCoCo 以零额外参数的简洁形式同时提升稠密特征与全局识别表现。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has produced powerful visual representations, yet learning strong dense features remains challenging. While consistency between augmented views provides strong image-level supervision, extending this principle to local visual content is less straightforward. Existing approaches often match individual patches across views or identify coherent regions, introducing uncertainties in correspondence estimation and assumptions about how visual content should be grouped. We introduce ReCoCo (Regional Composition Consistency), a principled approach that extends cross-view consistency to the regional level without requiring such correspondences or semantic grouping. ReCoCo represents the content shared between augmented views through compositions of their patch-level predictions over learned visual prototypes. By aligning these compositions, the model learns from regions containing multiple visual concepts without requiring them to be separated beforehand. ReCoCo integrates naturally with teacher-student self-distillation and introduces no additional learned parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in frozen-feature segmentation, clustering, and regional retrieval while maintaining competitive image-level recognition, alleviating the trade-off between dense and global representation quality.

</details>


### [179] [Does the Action Matter? Probing Action-Conditioned Semantic Forecasting as a World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24840)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"动作依赖性"评估，检验动作条件语义预测模型是否真正依赖条件动作，发现精度提升不能反映依赖强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型评估只关注预测保真度与规划性能，很少验证预测是否实际依赖输入的自车动作，导致"动作条件化"可能是名义上的。

Method: 比较带特征级与输入级动作条件化的语义预测器与无动作输入的同一模型；在测试时施加动作干预，用标准分割指标衡量动作依赖性；先在动作动态已知的合成环境验证，再应用于Cityscapes与BDD100K，并做数据集内与跨平台评估。

Result: 执行动作最多提升中期mIoU 2.5点(Cityscapes)与0.6点(BDD100K)；错误动作则下降最多23.2与7.0点，低于无动作模型。真实数据上特征级条件化的动作依赖约为输入级的1.6倍(精度相近)；合成环境中两者依赖相同，因历史并未决定动作。

Conclusion: 精度提升只能说明世界模型使用了动作，却不能说明其预测对动作的依赖有多强；需以动作干预评估动作条件化的实质有效性。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in autonomous driving to predict how a scene evolves under the ego vehicle's actions, supporting counterfactual reasoning and model-based planning. However, existing evaluations focus on prediction fidelity and planning performance, and rarely verify that the predictions depend on the conditioning action. In this paper, we study action dependence in action-conditioned semantic forecasting, which predicts future semantic segmentation from past segmentations and the ego action. We compare a forecaster with feature-wise and input-level action conditioning against an identical model without action input, and measure action dependence with test-time action interventions scored by standard segmentation metrics. The evaluation is validated in a synthetic environment with known action-dependent dynamics and applied to Cityscapes and BDD100K, both within each dataset and across the two vehicle platforms. Experiments show that the executed action improves mid-term mIoU by at most 2.5 points on Cityscapes and 0.6 points on BDD100K, whereas a wrong action reduces it by up to 23.2 and 7.0 points, below the model without action input. On real data, feature-wise conditioning depends 1.6 times more on the action than input-level conditioning at similar accuracy, whereas both depend on it equally in the synthetic environment, where the history does not determine the action. These results show that an accuracy gain indicates that a driving world model uses its action, but not how strongly its forecasts depend on it. Code is available at https://github.com/binayksingh/does-action-matter.

</details>


### [180] [Gaspard: Expressive unified speech and gesture synthesis with continuous autoregressive flow-matching and decoupled classifier-free guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24862)
*Téo Guichoux,Théodor Lemerle,Catherine Pelachaud,Nicolas Obin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一语音-手势生成模型Gaspard，以连续自回归+next-step flow-matching联合生成语音与共语手势，并用解耦CFG平衡两模态影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音与共语手势生成多采用级联流水线：先生成语音再驱动手势，导致语音生成与手势相互独立，难以建模二者的协同性。

Method: 基于连续自回归生成框架，采用next-step flow-matching预测，在Seamless Interaction大规模多模态数据集上训练；针对语音与手势异构的时间特性设置模态专属预测头，并对两条生成流施加解耦的classifier-free guidance。

Result: 客观与感知评测表明，Gaspard相对单模态基线是强的手势生成模型；与匹配的级联系统相比，生成的语音与手势同步性显著更高。解耦引导在提升表现力的同时缓解了连续自回归手势生成中的动态塌缩。

Conclusion: 统一联合建模优于级联方案，模态专属预测头与解耦CFG是平衡语音-手势影响、保持生成动态的关键设计。

Abstract: Human communication involves highly coordinated multimodal signals such as speech and co-speech gestures. Existing approaches for generating both modalities commonly follow a cascaded pipeline, where speech is generated first and subsequently used to drive gesture generation; speech generation is therefore independent of gestures. We introduce Gaspard, a unified speech--gesture generation model based on continuous autoregressive generation with next-step flow-matching predictions, and trained on Seamless Interaction, a large-scale multimodal dataset. The heterogeneous temporal properties of speech and gesture motivate modality-specific prediction heads and decoupled classifier-free guidance over the two generation streams. This guidance rebalances the influence of speech and gesture at inference time, improving expressivity while mitigating dynamics collapse in continuous autoregressive gesture generation. Objective and perceptual evaluations show that Gaspard is a strong gesture generation model against unimodal baselines and that it produces highly synchronous speech and gestures compared to a matched cascaded system.

</details>


### [181] [NereidVLA: Learning to Understand and Navigate the Underwater World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24864)
*Chenjie Zhao,Zhiliang Zhu,Quan Chen,Yuquan Xue,Xingxue Shi,Puyue Wang,Zhiqiang Zhao,Kangye Zheng,Boyang Xu,Mingwei Lin,Dejun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 NereidVLA，先在大规模海洋视觉-语言数据上预训练 Sea2Know 获取空间知识，再用仿真专家轨迹适配为语言条件的三维导航策略，实现水下闭环导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航需要将视觉空间线索转化为运动，但现有海洋视觉-语言数据集普遍缺乏配对的机器人轨迹，导致视觉-语言知识难以直接用于具身导航。

Method: 两阶段训练：1）Sea2Know 在约200万海洋样本上预训练，学习目标定位、空间关系与场景结构，无需配对机器人运动监督；2）在仿真水下载具动力学下采集的专家轨迹上适配。模型输入RGB历史与语言指令，输出离散运动 token，解码为局部三维轨迹用于闭环跟踪。评估结合独立的六维水下导航理解基准、离线预测与闭环导航。

Result: 在匹配的导航训练与执行设置下，海洋空间预训练将仿真成功率从72.4%提升至84.4%；未在真实测试环境微调的情况下，NereidVLA 在实验室水池机器人试验中成功率达78.75%，对比 InternVLA-N1 的47.50%。

Conclusion: 海洋空间监督可有效支撑下游运动学习与真实水下物理导航，验证了"空间知识预训练+仿真轨迹适配"范式的可行性与跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Underwater navigation requires translating visual spatial cues into motion, yet marine vision-language datasets rarely include paired robot trajectories. We present NereidVLA, a vision-language-action model that transfers marine spatial knowledge to language-conditioned 3D navigation. We first train Sea2Know on approximately two million marine examples to learn target grounding, spatial relations, and scene structure without paired robot-motion supervision. We then adapt it using expert trajectories executed under simulated underwater vehicle dynamics. Given RGB histories and a language instruction, NereidVLA predicts discrete motion tokens that decode into local 3D trajectories for closed-loop tracking. Evaluation combines an independent six-dimensional underwater navigation understanding benchmark with offline prediction and closed-loop navigation. Under matched navigation training and execution settings, marine spatial pretraining increases simulation success from 72.4% to 84.4%. Without fine-tuning on the real test environment, NereidVLA achieves 78.75% success in laboratory-pool robot trials, compared with 47.50% for InternVLA-N1. These results show that marine spatial supervision can support downstream motion learning and physical underwater navigation.

</details>


### [182] [ShadowTransfer: A Geographic Transfer Benchmark for Overhead Shadow Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24904)
*Shagun Mittal,Raymond A. Yeh,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ShadowTransfer跨城市阴影检测基准（4500张人工校验掩膜、留一城市协议），用三探针框架诊断地理迁移失效机制（特征空间漂移、阴影像素置信度坍塌、不同架构可恢复组件不同），据此设计即插即用模块SIB，在9个（架构,留出城市）组合上平均差距最小（6.42 mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俯视航空影像的稠密预测模型常在单一地区训练、跨地区部署，但缺乏衡量阴影检测跨区域迁移能力的基准；现有俯视阴影数据集要么没有地理迁移评估协议，要么依赖自动生成标签。

Method: 构建ShadowTransfer基准：覆盖美国三座城市、两种原生分辨率、4500张人工校验阴影掩膜，并配套留一城市交叉验证（LOCO）协议。在四种架构上评估原始训练与八种适配方法，提出三探针诊断框架（特征空间探针、像素直方图匹配对照、置信度分析、组件级重训练消融）；据此设计Spectral Information Bottleneck（SIB）即插即用模块，每个诊断发现对应一个组件。

Result: 原始训练下留出测试城市导致3.5–7.8 mIoU下降；训练集从225扩至900张时差距依旧显著；八种适配方法中六种平均表现劣于原始训练。诊断显示失效发生在特征空间、像素直方图匹配无法弥合、表现为阴影像素置信度坍塌而背景置信度完好、并可通过重训练不同架构组件恢复（部分模型为编码器、部分为解码器）。结论在INRIA建筑轮廓数据上复现。SIB在9个单元上平均差距最小（6.42 mIoU），与FDA持平。

Conclusion: 跨区域地理迁移是俯视稠密预测中被忽视的系统性问题，无法靠简单域适配或数据扩容解决；基于机制诊断的针对性设计（SIB）有效缩小城市间差距，且其诊断结论可推广至阴影以外的任务。基准、LOCO协议、代码与模型均已开源以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: Overhead aerial imagery feeds dense-prediction pipelines that are routinely trained in one geographic region and deployed in another, yet no benchmark measures how shadow detection models transfer across regions. Existing overhead shadow datasets either lack a geographic transfer protocol or rely on automatically generated labels. We introduce ShadowTransfer, a benchmark of 4,500 human-verified shadow masks across three U.S. cities at two native resolutions, paired with a leave-one-city-out evaluation protocol. Under vanilla training, holding out the test city costs 3.5 to 7.8 mIoU across four architectures, the gap remains significant when the training set grows from 225 to 900 images, and six of eight adaptation methods degrade more than vanilla training on average. We propose a three-probe framework that diagnoses where and how the transfer breaks: the failure operates in feature space, beyond the reach of pixel-histogram matching, shows up as a confidence collapse on shadow pixels while background confidence stays intact, and is recoverable by retraining different architectural components depending on the model: encoder for some, decoder for others. These findings replicate on INRIA building footprints, suggesting they describe geographic transfer beyond shadows. Guided by the diagnosis, we build Spectral Information Bottleneck (SIB), a plug-in module with one component per finding. Across the 9 (architecture, held-out city) cells, SIB has the smallest mean gap to the within-city upper bound of the ten methods we evaluate (6.42 mIoU), level with FDA. The benchmark, LOCO protocol, code, and trained models are released to support reproducible evaluation of geographic transfer in overhead dense prediction.

</details>


### [183] [PrismRestore: A Compact Restoration Toolbox for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24915)
*Zhaocheng Yu,Kui Jiang,Jing Cao,Yiang Chen,Xiaocheng Feng,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrismRestore 从单个冻结的 all-in-one 修复模型中构建紧凑的可调用修复动作库，并用策略网络组合这些动作，实现单骨干的智能体式图像修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic 图像修复方法依赖多个修复网络作为工具箱，存储开销大；本文希望仅用单个冻结模型即可提供可组合的修复动作。

Method: 利用共享模型中任务相关但相互重叠的通道响应：离线任务先验搜索基于激活与梯度统计将响应相似的通道分组，在非代表通道上搜索任务特定的调制系数，代表通道不调制，形成先验库；再用状态条件策略通过 Group Relative Policy Optimization 学习动作组合，其目标在同一输入的轨迹组内分别归一化保真与感知反馈，并结合终端评估与由逐步质量变化得到的 return-to-go。

Result: 在合成复合退化与真实多任务基准上优于所评估的 all-in-one 基线，修复质量与多模型智能体相当，且仅用单个修复骨干、推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 通过通道级任务先验构建动作库并配合 GRPO 策略组合，可在无额外骨干或任务适配模块的前提下实现高效、可组合的智能体式图像修复。

Abstract: Agentic image restoration composes operations to handle mixed degradations, yet its toolbox often requires storing multiple restoration networks. We propose PrismRestore, which constructs a compact bank of callable restoration actions from a single frozen all-in-one model. Our approach builds on task-dependent yet overlapping channel responses in the shared model. An offline task-prior search uses activation and gradient statistics to group channels with similar response profiles, then searches task-specific modulation coefficients over non-representative channels while leaving representative channels unmodulated. The resulting prior bank enables explicit action selection and composition without additional restoration backbones or task-specific adapter modules. A state-conditioned policy learns to compose these actions using Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its objective separately normalizes fidelity and perceptual feedback within groups of trajectories sampled for the same input, and combines terminal evaluation with return-to-go derived from stepwise quality changes. Experiments on synthetic composite-degradation and real-world multi-task benchmarks show improvements over the evaluated all-in-one baselines and restoration quality competitive with multi-model agents. PrismRestore achieves these results with a single restoration backbone and faster inference than the evaluated agentic baselines.

</details>


### [184] [Unified Law for Visual Tasks (The Four Laws of Computer Vision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24917)
*Sen Fang,Yalin Feng,Yancheng Zhang,Yanxin Zhang,Zonglin Lyu,Hongbin Zhong,Liwei Che,Rui Wu,Chen Chen,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“视觉任务统一定律”（细分为计算机视觉四定律），以“世界快照”假设将所有视觉/可视化任务重构为生成任务，用世界快照模型（WSMs）在千余个视觉任务上统一训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉任务长期碎片化，各任务各自建模、各自训练；作者类比大语言模型对NLP的统一，希望用单一框架/方法统一训练并执行几乎所有视觉任务。

Method: 核心假设：世界信息被编码进图像（World Snapshots），故图像处理即世界处理、图像生成即世界快照生成；若所有视觉与可视化任务都能重构为生成任务，则统一方法WSMs可完成绝大多数视觉任务。通过定义不同的输入-输出映射界定任务，在1000+视觉任务上训练与评测以验证定律。

Result: 论文称在1000余个已训练并评估的视觉任务上验证了四定律，并给出面向未来视觉任务研究与应用的统一训练框架。

Conclusion: 视觉任务可由生成式建模范式统一，WSMs为跨任务统一训练提供了可行路径，为视觉领域的“基础模型化”提供理论表述与框架雏形。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a Unified Law for Visual Tasks, which could be further divided into Four Laws of Computer Vision, with the goal of enabling a single framework or method to uniformly train on and perform nearly all visual tasks. Our starting point is an inspiration and hypothesis: if the world information is encoded into image, which we call World Snapshots, then image processing is world processing, and image generation is World Snapshot generation. Furthermore, if all visual and visualization tasks can be reformulated as generation tasks, then a unified method, World Snapshot Models (WSMs), can perform the vast majority of visual tasks, much as large language models have largely unified natural language processing. This paper validates these laws across more than 1,000 trained and evaluated visual tasks (defined by distinct input-output mappings) and presents a unified training framework for future visual-task research and applications.

</details>


### [185] [UltraTex: Aligning Physical Knowledge for High-Fidelity PBR Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24941)
*Jingzhi Bao,Xiaohe Ma,Hong Li,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraTex：级联多视图扩散框架，将物理知识与VLM材质先验注入扩散过程，从单张参考图生成高保真PBR纹理（反照率+金属度/粗糙度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张参考图生成PBR纹理高度病态：观测外观混杂了材质身份、表面反射与光照。现有生成方法虽能产出逼真反照率图，但难以推断准确的金属度与粗糙度，导致重光照效果不佳。

Method: 采用级联多视图扩散：①先联合预测反照率与世界空间着色法线，以获得更细致的反照率图；②以生成的反照率为条件，结合参考图与材质语义先验预测金属度-粗糙度（MR）图。语义注入方面，用轻量适配器将VLM隐层特征转换为内在材质表示token，提供部件级知识锚点；物理建模方面，提出光度表示对齐，将扩散中间特征对齐到受控光照下真实材质渲染，使金属度/粗糙度通过高光强度、镜面锐度与局部反射对比变得可观测，无需显式光照监督。

Result: 大量实验表明UltraTex可生成高质量纹理，在视觉保真度、材质准确性与物理合理性上优于现有开源与商业方法。

Conclusion: 将物理知识（世界空间法线、光度渲染对齐）与VLM材质语义先验融入多视图扩散，可超越单纯外观匹配，显著提升单图PBR材质推断的可重光照可用性。

Abstract: Generating physically based rendering (PBR) texture maps for a 3D mesh from a single reference image is highly ambiguous, as the observed appearance entangles material identity, surface reflectance, and illumination. While current generative methods produce realistic albedo maps, they still struggle to infer accurate metallic and roughness attributes for relighting. We present UltraTex, a cascaded multi-view framework that moves beyond appearance matching by integrating physical knowledge into multi-view diffusion-based PBR generation. UltraTex first jointly predicts albedo and world-space shading normals to generate more detailed albedo maps, and then predicts metallic-roughness (MR) maps conditioned on the generated albedo, reference image, and material semantic priors. To inject material semantics, a lightweight adapter transforms hidden-layer features of a vision-language model (VLM) into intrinsic material representation tokens, providing part-level knowledge anchors for MR estimation. To model material-light interaction, photometric representation alignment guides intermediate diffusion features toward ground-truth material renderings under controlled lighting. This makes metallic and roughness variations observable through highlight intensity, specular sharpness, and local reflectance contrast, providing stronger guidance for fine-grained material prediction without explicit lighting supervision. Extensive experiments show that UltraTex generates high-quality textures and outperforms existing open-source and commercial methods in visual fidelity, material accuracy, and physical plausibility.

</details>
